###### Paragraphe 3 : Illegal taking of interest

###### Art. 613

Constitutes illegal taking of interest, the act for a person holding public authority or charged with a public service mission or for a person invested with an elective public mandate, of taking, receiving or retaining, directly or indirectly, any interest whatsoever in an enterprise or in an operation of which they have, at the time of the act, in whole or in part, the responsibility to ensure the supervision, administration, liquidation or payment.

###### Art. 614

Any person guilty of unlawful taking of interest is punished by a term of imprisonment of one (01) to three (03) year(s) and a fine of five million (5,000,000) to twenty million (20,000,000) CFA francs.

###### Art. 615

The penalties provided for in the preceding article are applicable to the civil servant, public officer or agent of the State or of a local authority who, within five (05) years from the cessation of his function, following resignation, dismissal, leave, retirement or placement on availability or for any other cause, has taken any interest whatsoever in the aforementioned acts, operations or enterprises, previously subject to his supervision, control, administration or of which he ensured payment or liquidation.

In the cases provided for in the preceding paragraph, the directors of the enterprises, public corporations or companies considered as accomplices as well as the persons who interposed themselves in the commission of the act, are punished by the same penalties. The provisions of this article apply to agents of local authorities, public establishments, nationalized companies, mixed economy companies in which the State or local authorities hold directly or indirectly more than fifty percent (50%) of the capital.

###### Art. 616

The spouse, relative up to the fourth (4th) degree or person living in notorious concubinage with the civil servant, public officer or agent of the State, of a local authority; of a public establishment, of a State company or of a mixed economy company with majority State participation, responsible for functions of administration, supervision or control, who has taken interests in the enterprise after taking up these functions or before a period of two years after their cessation, is presumed to be an interposed person.

###### Art. 617

The fact for any agent of the State, of a local authority, of a public establishment, of a State company or of a mixed economy company with majority State participation, in a public tender or in a public contract or market, to encourage or admit, either directly or indirectly, overinvoicing or fictitious invoicing

by the tenderer or supplier, with the intention of deriving any profit therefrom, is assimilated to an unlawful taking of interest.

###### Art. 618

Any person guilty of the acts provided for in the preceding article is punished by a term of five (05) to ten (10) years of criminal imprisonment and a fine of five million (5,000,000) to twenty million (20,000,000) CFA francs.

###### Art. 619

The court seized may pronounce, where applicable, against the convicted person the following additional penalties:

1) the confiscation of sums, objects or valuables irregularly acquired or held. If the thing has not been seized or cannot be produced, the confiscation is ordered in value;

2) the posting or dissemination of the decision pronounced at the expense of the convicted person;

3) the prohibition from residing in Togo for a period not exceeding ten (10) years after the execution of the sentence, if the convicted person is a foreigner.

###### Paragraphe 4 : De l'enrichissement illicite

###### Art. 620

Any acquisition of movable or immovable property by a public agent or a person holding public authority, which he or she cannot justify by his income declared to the tax administration or by his legitimately acquired and duly attested patrimony, is considered as illicit enrichment.

###### Art. 621

Any person guilty of illicit enrichment is punished by a term of imprisonment of one (01) to five (05) year(s) and a fine corresponding to double the value deemed excessive in relation to the value of the property that the accused is likely to possess.

#### Chapitre VI : Des infractions contre l'économie nationale et l'équipement public

##### Section 1 : Des atteintes à l'économie nationale

###### Paragraphe 1 : De la spéculation et des atteintes à la transparence des marchés

###### Art. 622

Any person who is punished by a term of imprisonment of six (06) months to two (02) years and a fine of five hundred thousand (500,000) to twenty million (20,000,000) CFA francs or one of these two penalties:

1) facilitates or participates in any sale of bonds of the Togolese State of which the seller is not the owner at the time of the conclusion of the sale when at that date, the seller has not taken the necessary steps for the availability of the bonds at the delivery date;

2) facilitates or participates in any derivative instrument contract under which one of the parties pays the other a premium in consideration of a payment or other benefit in the event of a credit event or default affecting the Togolese State.

###### Art. 623

Anyone who, with the aim of influencing or distorting the normal course of the market, of a security, of a financial instrument, of a good or of a title representing a good or a set of goods admitted to trading on a stock exchange or an equivalent organization, is punished by imprisonment of three (03) months to five (05) years and a fine of five hundred thousand (500,000) to twenty million (20,000,000) CFA francs:

1) knowingly disseminates slanderous, false or misleading information;

2) carries out directly or indirectly abnormal operations on these goods, securities or titles; in order to obtain or attempt to obtain an economic advantage for himself or for a third party.

Anyone who, as a member of a management or supervisory or control body of an issuing company, or who, by profession or by function, has access to precise confidential information likely to have an influence on the normal course of a market, of a security, of a financial instrument, of a good or of a title representing a good or a set of goods admitted to trading on a stock exchange or an equivalent organization, obtains or attempts to obtain for himself or for a third party an economic advantage by knowingly using this information before the public becomes aware of it, is punished by imprisonment of three (03) to five (05) years and a fine of one million (1,000,000) to fifty million (50,000,000) CFA francs:

1) by exploiting them directly or indirectly by disposing by acquisition, transfer, exchange or any other mode, of the aforementioned goods, securities and titles;

2) by disclosing them to anyone with or without consideration; 3) by exploiting them through the recommendation to a third party of the purchase or sale of these goods, securities and titles.

Any person who exploits confidential information that he has obtained or that he knows to have been obtained by the commission of a crime or an offence is punished by the same penalties as those prescribed in the preceding paragraph.

Any person who obtains or attempts to obtain for himself or for a third party a pecuniary advantage by exploiting confidential information is punished by imprisonment of two (02) to four (04) years and a fine of five hundred thousand (500,000) to twenty million (20,000,000) CFA francs.

###### Paragraphe 2 : Des entraves à la liberté du travail

###### Art. 624

Any act tending to obstruct, in a concerted manner, the free exercise of work constitutes an obstruction to freedom of work.

###### Art. 625

Any person who obstructs freedom of work is punished by a term of imprisonment of one (01) to six (06) months and a fine of one hundred thousand (100,000) to five hundred thousand (500,000) CFA francs or one of these two penalties. The penalties provided for in the preceding paragraph are doubled if the obstruction to freedom of work was done by means of violence, assault, false news, threats or fraudulent maneuvers.

###### Art. 626

Any employer who organizes the poaching for their benefit of employees of competing enterprises, by fraudulent maneuvers, is punished by a term of imprisonment of two (02) months to one (01) year and a fine of two hundred thousand (200,000) to one million (1,000,000) CFA francs or one of these two penalties.

###### Art. 627

Any person who participates in a concerted work stoppage practiced outside the regulations in force is punished by a term of imprisonment of five (05) to fifteen (15) days or one (01) to twenty (20) day(s) of community service or a fine of two thousand (2,000) to thirty thousand (30,000) CFA francs.

The organizers of the concerted work stoppage are punished by a term of one (01) to six (06) months of imprisonment.

###### Paragraphe 3

Violation of business secrets of the enterprise and protection of the interests of the national economy

###### Art. 628

Any person who discloses to a person not authorized to have knowledge of it, other than duly empowered Togolese judicial or administrative authorities, information falling under business secrets of an enterprise, is punished by a term of imprisonment of one (01) to three (03) years and a fine of five hundred thousand (500,000) to two million (2,000,000) CFA francs or one of these two penalties.

Protected strategic information falling under business secrets within the meaning of this code constitutes any element, whatever the medium, of a commercial, financial, scientific, technical, technological or strategic nature:

1) knowledge or disclosure of which would be likely to harm the interest of the enterprise; 2) or which have been the subject by the enterprise of a classification and internal or contractual protection in its

relations with third parties, intended to enable the safeguarding of their confidential character.

Subject to international treaties or agreements and laws and regulations in force, it is prohibited, under penalty of imprisonment of one (01) to three (03) years and a fine of five hundred thousand (500,000) to two million (2,000,000) CFA francs, for any legal or natural person, to request, seek or communicate, in writing, orally or in any other form, in order to constitute evidence for foreign judicial or administrative proceedings or in the context thereof:

1) documents or information of an economic, commercial, industrial, financial, scientific, technical or strategic nature the communication of which is likely to undermine the sovereignty, security, essential economic interests of Togo or public order;

2) documents or information of an economic, commercial, industrial, financial, scientific, technical or strategic nature not of a public nature the disclosure of which would be likely to seriously compromise the interests of an enterprise, by undermining its scientific and technical potential, its strategic positions, its commercial or financial interests or its competitive capacity or which have been the subject as such of a classification and specific protection.

###### Paragraphe 4: Fraud and violation of rules regarding investment

###### Art. 629

Without prejudice to the administrative sanctions provided for by the investment code, any fraud or any serious and repeated violation of the obligations and commitments made by the investor in accordance with the regulations in force is punished by a term of imprisonment of six (06) months to two (02) years, a fine of five million (5,000,000) to fifty million (50,000,000) CFA francs, or one of these two penalties.

##### Section 2: Offenses against public infrastructure

###### Art. 630

Is punished by a term of six (06) months to two (02) years of imprisonment and a fine of five hundred thousand (500,000) to two million (2,000,000) CFA francs or one of these two penalties, any person who:

1) by acts of violence, occupation, demonstrations or gatherings opposes the execution of public works or the demolition of buildings or structures ordered by the administrative or judicial authority;

2) directly or indirectly interrupts or directly or indirectly participates in the interruption of the production or distribution of water, electrical energy, fuels, minerals and raw materials;

3) voluntarily obstructs or participates in the obstruction of railways, national roads, port quays; 4) by maneuvers, acts of violence, threats or coercion, enters a place assigned to an administrative, scientific or cultural public service, or remains there irregularly and voluntarily, after having been informed by the authority of the irregular nature of their presence.

###### Art. 631

If the offense was committed by an organized gang, the perpetrators are punished by a term of imprisonment of three (03) to five (05) years and a fine of one million (1,000,000) to five million (5,000,000) CFA francs or one of these two penalties.

###### Art. 632

When the total or partial destruction of hydraulic facilities and structures results from an act of vandalism, terrorism or any other reprehensible act knowingly orchestrated and executed, the perpetrators are liable to a term of imprisonment of three (03) to five (05) years and a fine of five million (5,000,000) to twenty million (20,000,000) CFA francs or one of these two penalties when this act has caused minor material damage.

This penalty may be doubled when the damage results in the total putting out of service of the structures in question.

When these reprehensible acts have caused serious injuries or loss of human life, the perpetrators are liable to the penalties provided for by this code for voluntary injuries and involuntary homicide.

###### Art. 633

The organizers responsible for the offenses referred to in this section are punished by a term of five (05) to ten (10) years of criminal imprisonment and a fine of five million (5,000,000) to twenty million (20,000,000) CFA francs or one of these two penalties, without prejudice to the penalties applicable in case of destruction or damage to property of the public domain or of private individuals.

##### Section 3: Destruction or damage of foodstuffs, goods or materials

###### Art. 634

Any person guilty of pillage or destruction of foodstuffs or goods, effects, movable property, committed in assembly or in a gang and by open force, is punished by a term of one (01) to five (05) years of imprisonment and a fine of one million (1,000,000) to five million (5,000,000) CFA francs or one of these two penalties.

If the foodstuffs or goods pillaged or destroyed are of primary necessity or essential to the economic life of the country; the instigators are liable to a term of five (05) to ten (10) years of criminal imprisonment and a fine of two million (2,000,000) to ten million (10,000,000) CFA francs or one of these two penalties.

###### Art. 635

Any person who, by any means, voluntarily damages foodstuffs, goods, materials or any instruments used for manufacturing, is punished by a term of six (06) months to two (02) years of imprisonment and a fine of one hundred thousand (100,000) to five hundred thousand (500,000) CFA francs.

If the offense is committed by an employee of the enterprise, they are punished by a term of imprisonment of one (01) to three (03) year(s) and a fine of five hundred thousand (500,000) to one million (1,000,000) CFA francs.

##### Section 4

Gaming and lottery houses and online gambling or games of chance sites

###### Art. 636

Any person who, without the authorization required by law, operates or causes to be operated for themselves or on behalf of others, a gambling house, organizes or operates a lottery or betting games on the outcome of sporting events or other random events, is punished by imprisonment of six (06) months to two (02) years and a fine of five hundred thousand (500,000) to five million (5,000,000) CFA francs or one of these two penalties. The court seized shall in all cases order the confiscation of utensils, apparatus, printed materials or other movable objects used by the offenders. It shall also order the confiscation of gaming proceeds and the closure of the premises at the time the offense is established, without prejudice to the penalties provided for in Article 54 of this code in case of conviction of a legal person.

Raffles and games organized for charitable purposes on the occasion of events regularly authorized by public authorities are permitted.

###### Art. 637

Anyone who offers or proposes to the public of Togo or from abroad, an online offer of betting or gambling and games of chance without holding an authorization required by law is punished by a penalty of six (06) months to two (02) years imprisonment and one million (1,000,000) to five million (5,000,000) CFA francs fine or one of these two penalties.

###### Art. 638

Anyone who advertises, by any means whatsoever, from Togo or from abroad, in favor of a betting site or unauthorized online gambling and games of chance is punished by a fine of five hundred thousand (500,000) to three million (3,000,000) CFA francs.

#### Chapitre VII : Des atteintes au patrimoine culturel

###### Art. 639

Is punished by a penalty of two (02) months to one (01) year imprisonment and a fine of one hundred thousand (100,000) to one million (1,000,000) CFA francs or one of these two penalties, any person in breach of the obligations:

1) of the owner, holder or occupant of any property listed on the national list not to modify its appearance and not to undertake any action likely to affect its life or integrity, without having notified the competent authority at least three (03) months before the date envisaged for the commencement of works;

2) not to proceed with the alienation of a listed cultural property without having notified the competent authority at least thirty (30) days before the date set for the act;

3) to respect a notice period of three (03) months before carrying out any displacement, transfer of ownership of properties proposed for classification and all works other than those of normal maintenance or current operation;

4) of notification by private individuals regarding alienation of cultural properties of which they are the owners;

5) of obtaining prior authorization from the competent authority before proceeding with any exploitation and reproduction for commercial purposes of a classified cultural property.

###### Art. 640

Is punished by a penalty of two (02) months to (01) year imprisonment and a fine of one hundred thousand (100,000) to one million (1,000,000) CFA francs or one of these two penalties only, any person found guilty of an offense against:

1) the prohibition on exportation of a property proposed for classification;

2) the prohibition on proceeding with any works of destruction, demolition, disfigurement, mutilation, modification, transformation, repair, painting, enlargement or restoration, without the express and prior authorization of the minister responsible, as the case may be, for culture or public works and urban planning;

3) the prohibition on proceeding or causing to proceed with the sale of debris or fragments of a classified cultural property;

4) the obligation to proceed, before any intervention on a classified property or in the process of classification in relation to an expropriation or a land development zone, with the archaeological and historical inventory of the intervention zone;

5) the prohibition on the exportation of classified cultural properties, except in cases provided for by law; 6) the prohibition on affixing posters or any advertising devices on classified sites and monuments and possibly, in the protection zone or in the perimeter determined for each specific case under the conditions provided for by law.

###### Art. 641

Is punished by a penalty of two (02) months to one (01) year imprisonment and a fine of one hundred thousand (100,000) to two million (2,000,000) CFA francs or one of these two penalties only, any person who removes or takes away elements from sacred sites, as defined by the legislative and regulatory provisions in force.

###### Art. 642

The perpetrators of intentional degradation or destruction of classified cultural properties are punished by the penalties provided for, as the case may be, in Articles 694 to 695 of this code, without prejudice to any damages.

They are further required to proceed, at their expense, with the restoration of the property they have degraded or mutilated.

###### Art. 643

The perpetrators of theft, pillage or receiving of classified cultural properties are punished, according to the circumstances, by the penalties provided for in Articles 412, 416 and following of this code.

#### Chapitre VIII : Des infractions contre la défense nationale et la sécurité de l'Etat

##### Section 1: Des atteintes contre la défense nationale

###### Paragraphe 1 : De la violation du secret de la défense nationale

###### Art. 644

For the purposes of this code, any element concerning national defense, whatever its medium, has the character of a national defense secret:

1) whose disclosure, access or knowledge is likely to harm or damage national defense and the armed forces, or to make possible the knowledge of a national defense secret;

2) or which has been subject to classification measures intended to restrict their dissemination, access or knowledge by a person not authorized for this purpose.

Elements concerning national defense as decided by the competent authorities for this purpose may be subject to classification and authorization measures. The levels of classification and authorization of elements concerning national defense, having the character

of a national defense secret and the authorities responsible for defining the methods by which their protection is organized are determined by decree as well as the classification of places and installations subject to national defense secrecy.

###### Art. 645

The act, by any person who is a depositary of an element concerning national defense, by status or profession, or by reason of a temporary or permanent function or mission making them obliged to respect national defense secrecy by effect of law or regulations or by contract:

1) of destroying, diverting, removing or reproducing it; 2) giving access to it to an unauthorized person; 3) bringing it to the knowledge of the public or an unauthorized person;

Is punished by a penalty of five (05) to ten (10) years criminal imprisonment and a fine of one million (1,000,000) to five million (5,000,000) CFA francs fine.

Is punished by the same penalties the act, by the depositary person, of having allowed access to, destruction, diversion, removal, reproduction or disclosure of any element concerning national defense.

When the depositary person acted through imprudence or negligence, the offense is punished by a penalty of one (01) to five (05) year(s) imprisonment and a fine of five hundred thousand (500,000) to one million (1,000,000) CFA francs.

###### Art. 646

Any person not referred to in the preceding article who commits the following acts is punished by imprisonment of three (03) to five (05) years and a fine of one million (1,000,000) to five million (5,000,000) CFA francs:

1) securing possession of, accessing, or obtaining knowledge of an element of interest to national defence;

2) destroying, removing or reproducing, by any means whatsoever, an element of interest to national defence; 3) bringing to the knowledge of the public or of an unauthorized person an element of interest to national defence.

###### Art. 647

Direct or indirect disclosure to a foreign power of a national defence secret by a Togolese national or by a foreigner, whether or not a depositary within the meaning of article 645 affiné a 1 of this code, is punished by the maximum term of criminal imprisonment and confiscation of property.

###### Art. 648

Attempted commission of the offences provided for in the first paragraph of article 645 and in article 646 is punished by the same penalties.

###### Paragraphe 2 : Du mercenariat

###### Art. 649

Any person specially recruited in Togo or abroad to fight in an international armed conflict and who is neither a national of a State party to the conflict nor a member of the armed forces of a State party to the conflict, nor has been sent by a State other than a State party to the conflict on an official mission as a member of the armed forces of that State, and who takes or attempts to take a direct part in hostilities with a view to obtaining personal advantage or material remuneration, is punished by criminal imprisonment of five (05) to seven (07) years and a fine of one million (1,000,000) to five million (5,000,000) CFA francs.

###### Art. 650

Any person specially recruited in Togo or abroad to take part in a concerted act of violence aimed at undermining the institutions or territorial integrity of a foreign State, and who takes part in such an act with a view to obtaining personal advantage or material remuneration, is punished by criminal imprisonment of seven (07) to ten (10) years and a fine of five million (5,000,000) to ten million (10,000,000) CFA francs.

###### Art. 651

Any natural or legal person who recruits, uses, finances, equips or trains mercenaries, as defined in the preceding articles, is punished by criminal imprisonment of twenty (20) to thirty (30) years and a fine of fifty million (50,000,000) to one hundred and fifty million (150,000,000) CFA francs.

###### Art. 652

The competent court may also impose, against any person found guilty of one of the offences provided for in this paragraph, the following penalties:

1) deprivation of civil rights provided for in articles 80 et seq. of this code;

2) publication, at the expense of the convicted person, of extracts of the conviction judgment in the press, in the official gazette or by posting in public places, as provided for in article 142 of this code;

3) prohibition of residence for ten (10) years provided for in articles 113 et seq. of this code.

###### Art. 653

Where the acts mentioned in this paragraph are committed abroad by a Togolese national or by a person habitually resident in Togolese territory, Togolese law is applicable even in cases where the offence is not punishable under the legislation of the State where it was committed, in derogation from the principles established by title I of this code.

##### Section 2: Des atteintes à la sécurité extérieure de l'Etat

###### Art. 654

Any Togolese national who, at any time, commits the following acts is punished by the maximum term of criminal imprisonment for treason:

1) delivers to a foreign power or its agents, or to a terrorist group, territories, places, works, stores, vessels, aircraft or war materiel belonging to Togo;

2) maintains intelligence with a foreign power or a terrorist group with a view to preparing hostilities against Togo or facilitating the success of military operations directed against Togo;

3) bears arms against Togo;

4) incites Togolese or allied military personnel to disobedience and treason for the benefit of a foreign power or a terrorist group;

5) deliberately destroys a vessel, an aircraft, a weapon or war materiel or other installation used for national defence.

###### Art. 655

Any foreigner who, at any time, initiates or maintains intelligence with a Togolese national to favour one of the acts of treason referred to in the preceding article is punished by the maximum term of criminal imprisonment.

###### Art. 656

Any person who, in time of peace, commits the following acts is punished by criminal imprisonment of five (05) to twenty (20) years:

1) deliberately removes or damages materiel or supplies intended for national defence or deliberately obstructs the movement of such materiel;

2) participates by means of leaflets, posters, or rumours maintained by any means whatsoever, in a campaign undermining the morale or discipline of the armed forces.

###### Art. 657

If the acts referred to in the preceding article are committed in time of war, the offenders are punished by the maximum term of criminal imprisonment.

###### Art. 658

Any person who, in time of peace, commits the following acts is punished by criminal imprisonment of five (05) to ten (10) years:

1) commits any act of provocation likely to lead to hostilities, retorsions or reprisals by a foreign power or a terrorist group against the Togolese population;

2) maintains intelligence with the agents of a foreign power or a terrorist group to harm the military or diplomatic situation of Togo;

###### Art. 659

Any person who, in time of peace, involuntarily facilitates, through negligence, imprudence or failure to observe regulations, an act of treason, terrorism or espionage is punished by imprisonment of one (01) to five (05) year(s).

###### Art. 660

Any person who, in time of peace, commits the following acts is punished by criminal imprisonment of five (05) to ten (10) years:

1) enters, in defiance of a signalled prohibition, a work, post, arsenal, camp, vessel, base or other military installation or serving or contributing to national defence;

2) flies over, without authorization, Togolese territory or takes photographs, surveys or drawings in a prohibited zone established by the military authority, or transports any materiel or armament for military purposes.

###### Art. 661

If the acts referred to in article 659 are committed in time of war, the offenders are punished by criminal imprisonment of five (05) to ten (10) years.

Similarly, where the acts referred to in article 660 are committed in time of war, the offenders are punished by criminal imprisonment of ten (10) to fifteen (15) years.

###### Art. 662

Those who, knowingly, have provided them with subsidies, means of subsistence, lodging, a place of retreat or meeting or have received or transmitted correspondence, documents or objects relating to such an offence are considered accomplices and liable to the same penalties as the perpetrators of offences against the external security of the State.

##### Section 3: Offences against the internal security of the State

###### Art. 663

Any person who sponsors or organizes actions aimed at destabilizing, endangering or eliminating the institutions of the Togolese Republic, in particular by inciting the population or the armed forces to arm themselves or to rise up against the authority of the Togolese State is punished by a sentence of twenty (20) to thirty (30) years of criminal imprisonment, or by the maximum term of criminal imprisonment when the acts have been committed by a person holding public authority.

Any person who knowingly participates in the actions referred to in the preceding paragraph is punished by a sentence of imprisonment of three (03) to five (05) years.

Any person who arms, recruits, uses, finances, equips or trains mercenaries for the purposes referred to in the preceding paragraph is punished by a sentence of twenty (20) to thirty (30) years of criminal imprisonment.

###### Art. 664

A conspiracy against the internal security of the State is constituted by a resolution agreed between several persons with a view to committing the acts referred to in the preceding article when this resolution is made concrete by one or more material acts tending towards the planning or organization of the conspiracy.

The conspiracy is punished by a sentence of twenty (20) years of criminal imprisonment. The sentence is increased to the maximum term of criminal imprisonment when it is committed by a person holding public authority.

###### Art. 665

Any person who publishes, disseminates or reproduces, by any means whatsoever, false news for the purposes referred to in article 688 and any person who refuses to immediately remove such information or to make access to it impossible is punished by a sentence of imprisonment of one (01) to three (03) year(s).

The author of the false news referred to in the preceding paragraph is punished by a sentence of imprisonment of three (03) to five (05) years.

###### Art. 666

Any person who sponsors or organizes actions aimed at arming or supplying weapons to the Togolese population or sponsors or organizes gang actions by open force with the aim of inciting civil war or provoking massacre, is punished by the maximum term of criminal imprisonment.

##### Section 4: Common provisions

###### Art. 667

Any author of a conspiracy having as its purpose one of the offences referred to in the preceding sections who, before any commencement of execution, reports it to the public authorities, is exempted or excused from punishment under the conditions provided for in articles 30 and 31 of this code. Any author of a conspiracy who, after a commencement of execution, permits or facilitates the arrest of his co-authors or accomplices benefits from a mitigating excuse under the conditions provided for in articles 32 to 34 of this code.

###### Art. 668

Any person who, having knowledge of acts constituting offences against the security of the State, does not immediately report them to the public authority, is punished by imprisonment of one (01) to five (05) years.

###### Art. 669

The court seized orders the seizure and confiscation of remuneration received by those guilty of offences against the security of the State.

If the remuneration could not be seized, its amount is declared acquired by the public treasury by the court and recovered as court costs.

#### Chapter IX: Offences against public confidence

##### Section 1: Forgery

###### Art. 670

Forgery is constituted by any fraudulent alteration of the truth, of a nature to cause prejudice and accomplished by any means whatsoever, in a writing or any other medium for expressing thought which has the object or which may have the effect of establishing proof of a right or of a fact having legal consequences.

###### Paragraph 1: Forgery in private, commercial or banking documents

###### Art. 671

Any person who counterfeits, alters or falsifies an act or a document containing acknowledgment, obligation or discharge, is punished by a sentence of imprisonment of one (01) to five (05) year(s) and a fine of one million (1,000,000) to five million (5,000,000) CFA francs.

If the author of the forgery is a public officer having the capacity to receive or establish the act, he is punished by a sentence of criminal imprisonment of five (05) to ten (10) years.

###### Art. 672

Any person who counterfeits, alters or falsifies a banking or commercial document containing acknowledgment, account, obligation or discharge, is punished by a sentence of imprisonment of six (06) months to two (02) years and a fine of five hundred thousand (500,000) to two million (2,000,000) CFA francs.

If the author of the forgery acted in the exercise of his profession, he is punished by a sentence of imprisonment of one (01) to five (05) year(s).

The court seized may, in addition, pronounce disqualification from the right to exercise his profession for a period of up to five (05) years.

###### Art. 673

Any person who knowingly makes use of counterfeited, altered or falsified acts and other documents, is punished by the same penalties as those applied to the author of the forgery.

###### Art. 674

Any person who reproduces, fraudulently imitates or falsifies the seals, stamps, marks or letterhead prints of a legal person under private law or of a private individual, is punished by a sentence of imprisonment of six (06) months to two (02) years and a fine of five hundred thousand (500,000) to two million (2,000,000) CFA francs.

Any person who knowingly makes use of seals, stamps, marks or letterhead prints that have been reproduced, fraudulently imitated or falsified, is punished by the same penalties as those applied to the author of the forgery.

###### Paragraph 2: Public forgery

###### Art.675

Any person who counterfeits or falsifies the seal of the State or of a public administration, the marks, hallmarks and other instruments used by public administrations to distinguish acts, documents, materials or objects, is punished by a sentence of imprisonment of one (01) to five (05) year(s) and a fine of one million (1,000,000) to five million (5,000,000) CFA francs.

Any person who knowingly makes use of counterfeited or falsified seals, marks, hallmarks and other instruments, is punished by the same penalties as those applied to the author of the forgery.

###### Art. 676

Any person who counterfeits or falsifies a certificate, document or paper the establishment of which is reserved to public administrations, is punished by a sentence of imprisonment of six (06) months to two (02) years and a fine of five hundred thousand (500,000) to two million (2,000,000) CFA francs.

Any person who knowingly makes use of counterfeited or falsified certificates, documents or papers, is punished by the same penalty as that applied to the author of the forgery.

###### Art. 677

If the author of the forgery is a civil servant or an agent having by virtue of his functions access to the seals, stamps, marks, instruments, document forms, certificates mentioned in the preceding articles, the sentence is increased to five (05) to ten (10) years of criminal imprisonment.

###### Art. 678

Any person who is punished by a sentence of imprisonment of one (01) to six (06) months and a fine of one hundred thousand (100,000) to five hundred thousand (500,000) CFA francs, or by one of these two penalties:

1) manufactures, sells, distributes even free of charge any objects, printed matter, forms which by their appearance, their size, could lead to confusion with the objects, printed matter or forms used by public administrations;

2) reuses a postage stamp or a fiscal stamp already used, or who overprints, perforates or alters said postage or fiscal stamps to increase or renew their official value.

###### Paragraph 3: False certificates

###### Art. 679

Any person who is punished by imprisonment of six (06) months to two (02) years and a fine of one hundred thousand (100,000) to two million (2,000,000) CFA francs, or one of these two penalties:

1) knowingly establishes a certificate or attestation stating materially false facts;

2) falsifies or modifies in any manner whatsoever, a certificate or attestation originally false;

3) knowingly makes use of an inaccurate or falsified certificate or attestation.

Any person belonging to the medical profession or to a profession related to public health who, in the exercise of their functions and to favor another, falsely certifies or conceals the existence of diseases, incapacities, infirmities or a state of pregnancy or provides false information on the origin of a disease, an incapacity, an infirmity or the cause of a death, is punished by the penalties provided for in paragraph 1 of this article.

###### Art. 680

Any person is punished by imprisonment of one (01) to five (05) year(s) and a fine of five hundred thousand (500,000) to five million (5,000,000) CFA francs, or one of these two penalties:

1) any person who establishes under the name of a doctor or other health professional a certificate falsely alleging a state of health, a vaccination or any other act of care;

2) any doctor or health professional who issues a certificate falsely alleging or concealing a disease, infirmity, a state of pregnancy, a cure or the cause of a death;

3) any person who establishes under the name of an employer or head of a school or vocational training establishment, a work certificate, a diploma, an attestation of good service or makes use of such certificates, attestations or diplomas that are counterfeited or falsified;

4) any person who falsely attests before a public officer, in an administrative or authentic act, facts which the act is intended to prove the truth of;

5) any person who knowingly makes inaccurate declarations in the constitution of an administrative file relating to the allocation of benefits or the recognition of a situation opening entitlement to certain advantages or distinctions.

##### Section 2: Counterfeit currency

###### Art. 681

Any person who counterfeits, falsifies or alters monetary signs having legal tender on the national territory or abroad is punished by ten (10) to twenty (20) years of criminal imprisonment and a fine of twenty million (20,000,000) CFA francs.

If the offender benefits from mitigating circumstances, they are punished by imprisonment of two (02) to five (05) years and a fine of five (05) to twenty million (20,000,000) CFA francs. They cannot benefit from a suspended sentence.

###### Art. 682

Any person is punished by five (05) to ten (10) years of criminal imprisonment and a fine of five million (5,000,000) to one hundred million (100,000,000) CFA francs or one of these two penalties, who:

1) counterfeits or alters gold or silver coins having legal tender on the national territory or abroad;
2) colors coins having legal tender on the national territory or abroad, with the aim of deceiving as to the nature of the metal.

###### Art. 683

Any person who counterfeits, falsifies or alters banknotes or coins other than gold or silver having legal tender on the national territory or abroad is punished by imprisonment of one (01) to five (05) years and a fine of two million (2,000,000) to fifty million (50,000,000) CFA francs or one of these two penalties.

###### Art. 684

Any person who participates in the issuance, use, display, distribution, importation or exportation of monetary signs that are counterfeited, falsified, altered or colored, is punished by the penalties provided for in the above articles according to the distinctions made therein.

###### Art. 685

Any person who receives as genuine monetary signs that are counterfeited, falsified, altered or colored, and who uses or attempts to use them after having known their defects, is punished by imprisonment of six (06) months to one (01) year and a fine the amount of which is equal to at least four times and at most ten times the value of said signs without this fine being less than two hundred thousand (200,000) CFA francs or one of these two penalties.

If the offender knowingly keeps them or refuses to hand them over to the authorities, they are punished by a fine the amount of which is equal to at least twice and at most four times, which cannot be less than one hundred thousand (100,000) CFA francs.

###### Art. 686

Any person is punished by imprisonment of one (01) to five (05) years and a fine of two million (2,000,000) to fifty million (50,000,000) CFA francs or one of these two penalties, who manufactures, subscribes, issues, uses, displays, distributes, imports or exports:

1) means of payment intended to supplement or replace monetary signs having legal tender on the national territory or abroad;
2) printed matter, tokens or other objects which would present with said monetary signs a resemblance likely to facilitate their acceptance or use in place of said signs.

###### Art. 687

Any person who reproduces, totally or partially, by any process whatsoever, monetary signs having legal tender on the national territory or abroad, without the prior authorization of the central bank or the issuing authority if they are foreign monetary signs, is punished by imprisonment of one (01) to ten (10) months and a fine of five hundred thousand (500,000) to one million (1,000,000) CFA francs or one of these two penalties.

Any person who displays, distributes, imports or exports the reproductions referred to in the preceding paragraph, by way of newspapers, books or prospectuses without the prior authorization of the central bank or the issuing authority if they are foreign monetary signs, is punished by the same penalties.

###### Art. 688

Any person who uses banknotes or coins having legal tender on the national territory or abroad as a medium for any advertising without the prior authorization of the central bank or the issuing authority if they are foreign monetary signs, is punished by a fine of one hundred thousand (100,000) to five hundred thousand (500,000) CFA francs.

The court seized orders the confiscation of the banknotes or coins used in the hands of all holders or depositaries.

###### Art. 689

Any person who manufactures, offers, without having been authorized, marks, materials, apparatus or other objects intended by their nature for the manufacture, counterfeiting, falsification, alteration or coloring of monetary signs, is punished by imprisonment of two (02) to five (05) years and a fine of five million (5,000,000) to one hundred million (100,000,000) CFA francs or one of these two penalties.

###### Art. 690

The penalties provided for in the preceding articles of this section apply:

1) to offenses committed on the national territory;
2) to offenses committed abroad, according to the distinctions and under the conditions provided for in articles 8 and following of this code.

###### Art. 691

For all the different cases referred to in this section, the court seised shall order the confiscation of banknotes, coins, metals, papers and other materials used, found in the possession of the guilty parties or in the hands of any holders and depositaries and intended for the commission of the offence or similar offences.

It shall also order the confiscation of instruments used to commit the offence, unless they were used without the knowledge of their owner.

The objects, metals, papers and other materials confiscated shall be handed over to the central bank upon its request, subject to the requirements of the administration of justice.

###### Art. 692

Any person guilty of one of the offences referred to in this section who reports the co-perpetrators and accomplices to the competent authorities before any prosecution, is exempted from the penalty provided for the completed offence.

Any person guilty of one of the offences referred to in this section who reports and facilitates the arrest of the co-perpetrators and accomplices to the competent authorities after the commencement of prosecution, may be exempted totally or partially from the penalty provided for the completed offence.

#### Chapitre X : Des destructions, dégradations, détériorations

###### Art. 693

Destruction is the act of annihilating, demolishing or pulling down voluntarily, without right, property belonging to another or to the State.

###### Art. 694

Any person who voluntarily destroys to the prejudice of another buildings, ships, aircraft, warehouses, operating buildings used for habitation or occupied by personnel is punished:

1) by the maximum term of imprisonment for a fixed period if the destruction was carried out by fire or explosives;

2) by a term of imprisonment of ten (10) to twenty (20) years if the destruction was carried out by any other means.

###### Art. 695

Any person who voluntarily destroys to the prejudice of another uninhabited immovable property, roadways, dykes, bridges, engineering structures, buildings for public use or operating buildings not occupied by personnel is punished:

1) by a term of imprisonment of twenty (20) to thirty (30) years if the destruction was carried out by fire or explosives;

2) by a term of imprisonment of five (05) to ten (10) years if the destruction was carried out by any other means.

###### Art. 696

Any person who voluntarily destroys to the prejudice of another huts, straw huts, cabins or other light constructions, is punished by a term of imprisonment of:

1) one (01) to five (05) years if at the time of the act, the construction was occupied by one or more persons; 2) six (06) months to two (02) years if the construction was unoccupied at the time of the act.

###### Art. 697

Any person who voluntarily destroys public or private land vehicles is punished by a term of:

1) five (05) to ten (10) years of criminal imprisonment if the destruction was carried out at the time when the vehicle was transporting persons, even while parked or stopped; 2) one (01) to five (05) years of imprisonment if the destruction was carried out at the time when the vehicle contained no person.

###### Art. 698

Any person who voluntarily or maliciously destroys or burns in any manner whatsoever securities, banknotes, commercial or banking instruments or any other document containing or effecting obligations, disposition or discharge, is punished by a term of imprisonment of one (01) to five (05) year(s).

###### Art. 699

Any person who voluntarily destroys installations, machines, tools, instruments, materials or products used for manufacturing, agricultural, industrial, commercial, artisanal operations, public or private administration is punished:

1) by a term of imprisonment of one (01) to five (05) year(s) and a fine of one hundred thousand (100,000) to five million (5,000,000) CFA francs if the perpetrator acted to the prejudice of the State or a public company;

2) by a term of imprisonment of six (06) months to two (02) years and a fine of one hundred thousand (100,000) to two million (2,000,000) CFA francs in all other cases.

###### Art. 700

Any person who voluntarily cuts down one or more trees or devastates crops, harvests or fodder to the prejudice of another or of the State, is punished by a term of imprisonment of one (01) to six (06) months and a fine of one hundred thousand (100,000) to five hundred thousand (500,000) CFA francs.

Any person who allows crops, harvests or fodder to be devastated by his herd to the prejudice of another or of the State is punished by the same penalties.

###### Art. 701

Any person who voluntarily poisons horses or other riding or draught animals, cows, oxen, sheep, goats or pigs, or any other domestic animals, fish in ponds, fish farms or reservoirs, is punished by a term of imprisonment of one (01) to five (05) year(s) and a fine of one hundred thousand (100,000) to five hundred thousand (500,000) CFA francs.

If the perpetrator of the offence is a foreigner, the court seised may, in addition, prohibit the guilty party from staying in the national territory for a period of two (02) years at least and five (05) years at most.

###### Art. 702

Any person who, without any necessity, kills or mutilates one of the animals mentioned in the preceding article, is punished by a term of imprisonment of:

1) six (06) months to two (02) years if the offence was committed in the buildings, enclosures, and appurtenances or on the lands of which the owner of the animal killed or mutilated was the owner, lessee, sharecropper or tenant farmer;

2) one (01) to six (06) months, if the offence was committed in any other place, and a fine of one hundred thousand (100,000) to five hundred thousand (500,000) CFA francs.

###### Art. 703

Any person who, in whole or in part, fills in ditches, low-lying areas and gutters, destroys fences, of whatever materials they are made, occupies or tears out live or dry hedges, is punished by a term of imprisonment of one (01) to six (06) months and a fine of one hundred thousand (100,000) to five hundred (500,000) CFA francs.

The same penalty is applicable to any person who moves or removes boundary markers, stakes, or trees planted or recognised to establish the boundaries between different properties or who opposes by violence or threats the placing of such markers.

###### Art. 704

The attempt to destroy property is punished as the completed offence.

###### Art. 705

Degradation or deterioration constitutes the voluntary act of putting in bad condition or damaging a thing or property belonging to another or to the State.

###### Art. 706

Any person who voluntarily degrades or deteriorates a thing or property belonging to another or to the State is punished by a term of imprisonment of two (02) months to one (01) year and a fine of one hundred thousand (100,000) to ten million (10,000,000) CFA francs, or one of these two penalties, if the degradation or deterioration does not affect the main structural works of immovable property, buildings, engineering structures, monuments, operating and service buildings, public or private.

The same penalty is applicable to perpetrators of voluntary degradations or deteriorations of works of art, public or private collections, classified objects belonging to another or to the State.

###### Art. 707

The attempt to degrade or deteriorate a thing or property is punished as the completed offence.

###### Art. 708

Any person who enters a meeting or demonstration, and incites other persons to commit destructions or degradations, is liable to a term of imprisonment of one (01) to three (03) year(s) and a fine of one million (1,000,000) to three million (3,000,000) CFA francs.

###### Art. 709

Any person who, during a public meeting or demonstration, wilfully causes destruction or damage to movable or immovable property, private or public, is punished by a term of imprisonment of two (02) months to one (01) year or a fine of one hundred thousand (100,000) to ten million (10,000,000) CFA francs.

#### Chapitre XI : De l'occupation frauduleuse des immeubles

###### Art. 710

Fraudulent occupation is constituted by the act of any person occupying without right, in whatever manner, an immovable property which another person or the State could dispose of by virtue of a land title, or an administrative or judicial decision.

##### Section 1 : De l'occupation frauduleuse des terrains

###### Art. 711

Any person who occupies, by erecting shacks, huts, cabins or other light constructions or fraudulently cultivates, land belonging to another, is punished by a term of imprisonment of six (06) months to two (02) years and a fine of one hundred thousand (100,000) to two million (2,000,000) CFA francs.

The court seised shall order the eviction of the occupant as well as the destruction of the plantations and constructions that have been erected thereon.

###### Art. 712

Any person who knowingly undertakes heavy construction works on land belonging to another, is punished by a term of imprisonment of one (01) to three (03) year(s) and a fine of one hundred thousand (100,000) to three million (3,000,000) CFA francs or one of these two penalties.

The court seised may order, if it deems it necessary, the demolition of the works illegally undertaken.

###### Art. 713

Any person who fraudulently registers in his or her name or in the name of another person an immovable property belonging to another or subject to judicial dispute, is punished by a term of imprisonment of one (01) to three (03) year(s) and a fine of one hundred thousand (100,000) to three million (3,000,000) CFA francs or one of these two penalties.

###### Art. 714

Any person who knowingly occupies without right land forming part of the national land domain or registered in the name of the State or a public authority or who concludes or attempts to conclude an agreement relating to such land, is punished by a term of imprisonment of one (01) to three (03) year(s) and a fine of one hundred thousand (100,000) to three million (3,000,000) CFA francs.

##### Section 2 : De l'occupation frauduleuse des bâtiments

###### Art. 715

Any person who occupies without right a dwelling, professional premises, a public building or a building assigned to public use, despite a formal notice from the owner of the premises, is punished by a term of imprisonment of one (01) to six (06) months and a fine of one hundred thousand (100,000) to five hundred thousand (500,000) CFA francs.

### TITRE V : DES INFRACTIONS RELATIVES AU TERRORISME

###### Art. 716

For the purposes of this code, offences of a terrorist nature include:

1) offences relating to the security of civil aviation, maritime navigation, ports and fixed platforms; 2) hostage-taking and offences against persons enjoying international protection;

3) terrorist bombings and nuclear terrorism which tend, by any means, to intentionally cause:

a) the death of another person, serious injury or serious bodily harm to another person; or

b) serious damage to public or private property, including public property, a government or public facility, a public transport system, an infrastructure or the environment; or c) damage to the property, places, facilities or systems mentioned in point b of this article, which causes or is likely to cause considerable economic losses, where the conduct in question, by its nature or context, is intended to intimidate a population or to compel a government or an international organisation to do or refrain from doing something.

A terrorist organisation within the meaning of this code is constituted by any group or any association of persons acting in an organised gang and committing one or other of the offences of a terrorist nature referred to in this title.

Offences relating to terrorism are constituted by the financing of terrorism and acts of support, recruitment, supply of weapons and incitement to terrorism.

#### Chapitre 1

Des infractions relatives à la sécurité de l'aviation civile, de la navigation maritime et des plates-formes fixes

###### Art. 717 : Les infractions visées par la présente section sont

1) le détournement ; 2) les infractions contre la sécurité de l'aviation civile ; 3) les infractions contre la sécurité des aéroports ; 4) les infractions contre la sécurité d'un navire ou d'une plate- forme fixe ou port.

##### Section 1 : Du détournement

###### Art. 718

Any person who, by violence, threat of violence or any other form of intimidation, seizes an aircraft in flight, a ship or a fixed platform or exercises control over it, commits hijacking and is punished by a term of ten (10) to twenty (20) years of criminal imprisonment and a fine of five million (5,000,000) to ten million (10,000,000) CFA francs.

If the acts referred to in the preceding paragraph have resulted in the death of one or more persons, the maximum term of imprisonment may be required.

###### Art. 719 : Aux fins d'application du présent paragraphe

1) l'expression «aéronef en vol» couvre la période qui s'étend depuis le moment où l'embarquement étant terminé, toutes les portes extérieures ont été fermées jusqu'au moment où l'une de ces portes est ouverte en vue du débarquement ; en cas d'atterrissage forcé, le vol est censé se poursuivre jusqu'à ce que l'autorité compétente prenne en charge l'aéronef ainsi que les personnes et les biens à bord ;

2) le terme «navire» désigne un bâtiment de mer de quelque type que ce soit qui n'est pas attaché en permanence au fond de la mer et englobe les engins à portance dynamique, les engins submersibles et tous les autres engins flottants ; 3) l'expression «plate-forme fixe» désigne une île artificielle, une installation ou un ouvrage attaché en permanence au fond de la mer aux fins de l'exploration ou de l'exploitation de ressources ou d'autres fins économiques.

##### Section 2: Offences against the safety of civil aviation or aircraft

###### Art. 720

Commits offences against the safety of civil aviation and is punished by a sentence of ten (10) to twenty (20) years of criminal imprisonment and a fine of ten million (10,000,000) to thirty million (30,000,000) CFA francs

if any of the following acts committed compromises or is likely to compromise the safety of an aircraft, any person who:

1) performs an act of violence against a person on board an aircraft in flight, if that act is likely to compromise the safety of the aircraft;

2) destroys an aircraft in service or causes damage to such aircraft which renders it incapable of flight or which is likely to compromise its safety in flight;

3) places or causes to be placed on an aircraft in service, by any means whatsoever, a device or substances likely to destroy the said aircraft or to cause damage to it which renders it incapable of flight or which is likely to compromise its safety in flight;

4) destroys or damages air navigation facilities, equipment or services or disrupts their operation, if any of these acts compromises or is likely to compromise the safety of an aircraft in flight;

5) communicates information which it knows to be false and thereby compromises the safety of an aircraft in flight.

The expression "aircraft in service", within the meaning of this article, covers the period extending from the moment when ground personnel or the crew begins to prepare it for a specific flight until the expiration of a period of twenty-four hours following any landing. The period extends in any event to the entire time during which the aircraft is in flight within the meaning of article 719 point 1.

If the facts referred to in this article have resulted in the death of one or more persons, the maximum sentence of imprisonment for a fixed term may be required.

###### Art. 721

Any person who threatens to commit any of the offences provided for in points 2, 3 and 4 of the preceding article with a view to compelling a natural or legal person to perform or refrain from performing any act is punished by a sentence of five (05) to ten (10) years of criminal imprisonment.

###### Art. 722

Commits offences against the safety of airports and is punished by a sentence of ten (10) to twenty (20) years of criminal imprisonment if any of the acts committed compromises or is likely to compromise the safety of that airport, any person who, using a device, a substance or a weapon:

1) performs against a person, in an airport serving international civil aviation, an act of violence which causes or is likely to cause serious injury or death;

2) destroys or seriously damages the facilities or disrupts the services of an airport serving civil aviation.

###### Art. 723

Any person who threatens to commit any of the offences provided for in the preceding article in order to compel a natural or legal person to perform or refrain from performing any act is punished by a sentence of five (05) to ten (10) years of criminal imprisonment.

###### Paragraphe 3: Offences against the safety of a ship or a fixed platform

###### Art. 724

Commits offences against the safety of a ship or a fixed platform and is punished by a sentence of five (05) to twenty (20) years of criminal imprisonment if any of the following acts committed compromises or is likely to compromise the safety of the ship or the fixed platform, any person who:

1) performs an act of violence against a person on board a ship or a fixed platform;

2) destroys or causes serious damage to a ship, its cargo or a fixed platform;

3) places or causes to be placed on a ship or a fixed platform, by any means whatsoever, a device or a substance likely to destroy the ship or the fixed platform or to cause damage to the ship, its cargo or the fixed platform;

4) destroys or seriously damages maritime navigation facilities or services or seriously disrupts their operation;

5) communicates information which it knows to be false.

###### Art. 725

Any person who threatens to commit any of the offences provided for in points 1, 2 and 4 of the preceding article in order to compel a natural or legal person to perform or refrain from performing any act is punished by a sentence of five (05) to ten (10) years of criminal imprisonment.

#### Chapitre 2

Hostage-taking and offences against persons enjoying international protection

###### Art. 726

Commits the offence of hostage-taking and is punished by a sentence of ten (10) to twenty (20) years of criminal imprisonment, any person who seizes a person or detains them and threatens to kill them, injure them or continue to detain them in order to compel a third party, namely the State, an intergovernmental organization, a natural or legal person or a group of persons to perform any act or to refrain from doing so as an explicit or implicit condition for the release of the hostage.

The sentence is increased to the maximum sentence of criminal imprisonment for a fixed term in the event of the death of the hostage.

###### Art. 727

Is punished by a sentence of the maximum imprisonment for a fixed term any person who commits against persons enjoying international protection:

1) a murder, kidnapping or other attack against the person or liberty of a person enjoying international protection;

2) an attack likely to endanger that person by resorting to violence against the official premises, private residence or means of transport of a person enjoying international protection.

Any person who threatens to commit such an attack is punished by ten (10) to twenty (20) years of criminal imprisonment. The expression "person enjoying international protection" is understood within the meaning of this article as:

a) any Head of State, including each member of a collegial body performing under the constitution of the State concerned the functions of Head of State, any Head of Government or any Minister for Foreign Affairs, when such person is in a foreign State, as well as members of their family accompanying them; b) any representative, official or official personality of the State or other agent of an intergovernmental organization who, at the date and place where an offence is committed against their person, official premises, private residence or means of transport, is entitled under international law to special protection against any attack on their person, liberty or dignity, as well as members of their family who are part of their household.

#### Chapitre 3: Terrorist bombings and nuclear terrorism

##### Section 1: Terrorist bombings

###### Art. 728

Constitute terrorist bombings offences committed with explosives or other lethal devices.

###### Art. 729

Any person who, with the intent to cause death or serious bodily injury or with the intent to cause massive destruction where such destruction results in or is likely to result in major economic loss, delivers, places, causes to be placed or diverts in or against a public place, a government or other public facility, a public transportation system or an infrastructure:

1) a weapon or an explosive or incendiary device designed to or having the capacity to cause death, serious bodily injury or extensive material damage;

2) a weapon or device designed to cause death, serious bodily injury or extensive material damage, or having the capacity to do so, through the release, dissemination or impact of toxic chemicals, biological agents, toxins or similar substances or radioactive materials.

is punished by the maximum term of imprisonment.

###### Art. 730: For the purposes of this paragraph

1) the term "government or public facility" means any permanent or temporary equipment or means of transport that is used or equipped by representatives of the State, members of the Government, Parliament or the Judiciary, or by agents or personnel of the State or any other public authority or entity, or by agents or personnel of an intergovernmental organization, in the course of their official functions;

2) the term "infrastructure" means any publicly or privately owned equipment providing public utility services, such as water supply, sewage disposal, energy, fuel or communications;

3) the term "public place" means those parts of any building, land, public thoroughfare, waterway and other location that are accessible to the public, on a continuous, periodic or occasional basis, and includes any commercial, cultural, historical, educational, religious, official, entertainment, recreational or other place that is so accessible or open to the public; 4) the term "public transportation system" means all facilities, vehicles and means, whether public or private, that are used in connection with services for the transport of persons or goods accessible to the public.

##### Section 2: Nuclear terrorism

###### Art. 731: Nuclear terrorism comprises

1) offences involving nuclear material; 2) offences involving a ship or a fixed platform; 3) offences involving radioactive material or devices.

###### Art. 732

Any person who commits offences involving nuclear material and commits any of the following acts is punished by the maximum term of imprisonment:

1) the receipt, possession, use, transfer, alteration, disposal or dispersal of nuclear material, without the required authorization and resulting in or likely to result in death or serious injury to any person or substantial damage to property or to the environment;

2) simple theft or aggravated theft of nuclear material;
3) the embezzlement or any other unlawful taking of nuclear material;

4) an act directed against a nuclear facility, or an act disrupting the operation of a nuclear facility, by which the perpetrator intentionally causes or knows that he may cause death or serious injury to any person or substantial damage to property or to the environment as a result of exposure to radiation or the release of radioactive substances, unless such act is undertaken in conformity with the law of the Togolese State in whose territory the nuclear facility is situated;

5) demanding nuclear material by threat, use of force or any other form of intimidation;

6) the threat to use nuclear material for the purpose of causing death or serious injury to any person or substantial damage to property or to the environment or to commit the offence referred to in point 5; or to commit offences referred to in points 2 and 5 for the purpose of compelling a natural or legal person, an international organization or the State to do or to refrain from doing any act.

For the purposes of this article:

a) the term "nuclear facility" means a facility, including associated buildings and equipment, in which nuclear material is produced, processed, used, handled, stored or disposed of permanently, if damage to such a facility or an act which disrupts its operation may result in the release of significant amounts of radiation or radioactive material;

b) the term "nuclear material" means plutonium except plutonium with an isotopic concentration of plutonium 238 exceeding 80%, uranium 233, uranium enriched in uranium 235 or 233, uranium containing the mixture of isotopes occurring in nature other than in the form of ore, and any material containing one or more of the elements or isotopes mentioned above.

###### Art. 733

Any person who commits offences involving a ship or a fixed platform and, when any of the following acts, by its nature or context, is intended to intimidate a population or to compel a government or an international organization to do or to abstain from doing any act:

1) uses against or on board a ship or a fixed platform, explosives, radioactive material or biological, chemical or nuclear (BCN) weapons, in a manner that causes or is likely to cause death or serious bodily or material damage;

2) discharges from a ship or a fixed platform, hydrocarbons, liquefied natural gas, or other harmful or particularly dangerous substances, which are not covered by point 1, in quantities or concentrations that cause or are likely to cause serious bodily or material damage;

3) uses a ship that causes death or serious bodily or material damage;

4) threatens to commit any of the offences referred to in points 1 and 2 or 3 above, whether or not such threat is made subject to a condition.

is punished by the maximum term of imprisonment.

###### Art. 734

The same penalty provided for in the preceding article is applicable to any person who transports on board a ship:

1) explosives or radioactive material, knowing that they are intended to cause or to threaten to cause death, serious bodily or material damage, whether or not such threat is made subject to a condition, in order to intimidate a population or to compel a government or an international organization to do or to abstain from doing any act;

2) any BCN weapon, knowing that it is a BCN weapon; 3) any source or special fissionable material, equipment or material especially designed or prepared for the processing, use or production of special fissionable material, or knowing that such material or equipment is intended for a nuclear explosive activity or subject to safeguards under a comprehensive safeguards agreement of the International Atomic Energy Agency (IAEA);

4) equipment, materials or software or related technologies that contribute significantly to the design, manufacture or delivery of a BCN weapon, with the intention of using them for that purpose.

Any person who transports on board a ship another person knowing that that person has committed an act which constitutes an offence covered by this article and with the intention of assisting that same person to evade criminal prosecution, is punished by a term of five (05) to twenty (20) years of criminal imprisonment.

###### Art. 735: For the purposes of application of articles 733 and 734

1) the term "transport" means to engage, organize the movement of a person or a product or exercise effective control, including decision-making power over such movement;

2) the expression "serious bodily or material damage" means:

a) serious bodily harm;

b) massive destruction of a public place, a governmental or public facility, an infrastructure or a public transport system resulting in considerable economic losses;

c) substantial damage to the environment, including air, soil, water, fauna and flora.

3) the expression "BCN weapons" means:

a) biological weapons which are:

- microbial agents or other biological agents as well as toxins whatever their origin or mode of production, of types and in quantities that are not intended for prophylactic, protective or other purposes;

- weapons, equipment or vectors intended for the use of such agents or toxins for hostile purposes or in armed conflicts;

b) chemical weapons which, taken together or separately are:

- toxic chemicals and their precursors, with the exception of those intended: for industrial, agricultural, research, medical, pharmaceutical or other peaceful purposes; for protective purposes namely, purposes having a direct relationship with protection against toxic chemicals and protection against chemical weapons and which are not dependent on the use, as a means of warfare, of toxic chemicals; for law enforcement purposes, including domestic riot control as long as the types and quantities involved are compatible with such purposes;

- munitions and specific devices designed to cause death or other harm through the toxic action of toxic chemicals that would be released as a result of the use of such munitions and devices;

- any equipment specifically designed for use in direct connection with the use of munitions and devices as defined previously;

c) nuclear weapons and other nuclear explosive devices;

2) the expression "toxic chemical" means any chemical which, through its chemical action on biological processes, can cause death, temporary incapacity or permanent harm to humans and animals. This includes all such chemicals whatever their origin or mode of manufacture, whether they are obtained in munitions or elsewhere;

3) the term "precursor" means any chemical reactant which enters at any stage in the manufacture of a toxic chemical, whatever the process used; this includes any key component of a binary or multi-component chemical system.

###### Art. 736

Commits offences involving radioactive materials and devices and is punished by the maximum term of criminal imprisonment, any person who:

1) possesses radioactive materials, manufactures or possesses a device with the intent to cause the death of a person or to cause them serious harm; or with the intent to cause substantial damage to property or to the environment;

2) uses in any manner radioactive materials or devices, or uses or damages a nuclear facility in such a way as to release or risk releasing radioactive materials:

a) with the intent to cause the death of a person or to cause them serious bodily harm;

b) with the intent to cause substantial damage to property or to the environment;

c) with the intent to compel a natural or legal person, an international organization or a government to perform an act or refrain from doing so.

###### Art. 737

The penalty provided for in the preceding article is applicable to any person who:

1) threatens, in circumstances which make the threat credible, to commit an offence referred to in point 2 of the preceding article;

2) demands the surrender of radioactive materials or devices or nuclear facilities by resorting to threat, in circumstances making it credible, or to the use of force.

1) an act constituting an offence provided for by this chapter;

2) any other act intended to kill or seriously injure a civilian, or any other person not taking direct part in hostilities in a situation of armed conflict, when by its nature and context, such act is intended to intimidate a population or to compel a government or an international organization to perform or refrain from performing any act, regardless of the occurrence of such acts.

###### Art. 738: For the purposes of application of articles 736 and 737

1) the expression "radioactive material" means any nuclear material or other radioactive substance containing nuclides which disintegrate spontaneously, such as alpha, beta, gamma and neutron radiation, and which could, due to their radiological or fissile properties, cause death, substantial damage to property or to the environment;

2) the expression "nuclear facility" means:

a) any nuclear reactor, including a reactor on board a ship, vehicle, aircraft or spacecraft as a source of energy serving to propel said ship, vehicle, aircraft or spacecraft, or for any other purpose;

b) any device or means of transport for the purposes of producing, storing or transporting radioactive materials;

3) the term "device" means:

a) any nuclear explosive device; b) any radioactive material dispersion device or any other device emitting radiation which, due to its radiological properties, causes death, serious bodily harm or substantial damage to property or to the environment.

#### Chapter 4

Financing of terrorism and acts of support, recruitment, provision of weapons and incitement to terrorism

##### Section 1: Financing of terrorism

###### Art. 739

Is punished by a sentence of ten (10) to twenty (20) years of criminal imprisonment any person who, by any means whatsoever, directly or indirectly by giving advice to that end, provides, collects or manages funds with the intention of having them used or knowing that they could be used, in whole or in part, with a view to committing:

###### Art. 740

For an act to constitute an offence, it is not necessary that the funds have actually been used to commit an offence provided for in the preceding article.

This provision is also applicable to natural persons who have committed offences of financing of terrorism without prejudice to the criminal liability incumbent upon them.

###### Art. 741

When the natural person responsible for the management or control of a legal person established on the territory of the Togolese State or governed by its legislation, in that capacity, has committed an offence of financing of terrorism, that legal person is punished by the penalties provided for in article 751.

###### Art. 742

For the purposes of this paragraph the term "funds" means assets of any nature, tangible or intangible, movable or immovable, acquired by any means whatsoever, including in electronic or magnetic form, which attest to a right of ownership or an interest in such assets, and in particular financial credits, travellers' cheques, bank cheques, money orders, shares, bonds, drafts, letters of credit and securities, without this list being exhaustive.

##### Section 2
