Acts of support, recruitment, provision of weapons and incitement to terrorism

###### Art. 743

For an act to constitute an offence provided for in this paragraph, it is not necessary that an offence referred to in articles 716 to 738 has actually been committed.

###### Art. 744

Any person who participates in the organisation, preparation or perpetration of one or more offences provided for in this chapter, or who provides any form of support whatsoever, active or passive, with the intention or knowing that the purpose of such participation or such support contributes to enabling the commission of one or more offences referred to in this chapter is punished by a sentence of ten (10) to twenty (20) years of criminal imprisonment.

###### Art. 745

Any person who recruits another person to commit or participate in the commission of one of the offences provided for in this chapter, or asks them to commit or participate in the commission of one of these offences, or to join an association or group, with the intention or knowing that the purpose of this recruitment or this request is to participate in the commission of one or more offences referred to in this chapter, is punished by the same sentences as those provided for in the preceding article.

###### Art. 746

Any person who supplies weapons and who knew or should have known that they could be intended for members of a terrorist organisation is punished by a sentence of ten (10) to twenty (20) years of criminal imprisonment and general confiscation of property.

###### Art. 747

Any person who distributes or makes available to the public a message, with the intention or knowing that this message may incite the commission of one or more offences provided for in this chapter, when such an act expresses the danger that one or more offences may be committed, is punished by a sentence of ten (10) to twenty (20) years of criminal imprisonment or loss of civil rights.

#### Chapitre 5

Organisation and contribution to the commission of acts of terrorism and liability of legal persons

##### Section 1

Organisation and contribution to the commission of acts of terrorism

###### Art. 748

Any person who organises the commission of an offence referred to in articles 557 and 558 or in articles 568 and 569 or gives orders to other persons to commit them is punished by the sentence provided for in the said articles.

###### Art. 749

Any person who contributes to the commission of one or more offences provided for in articles 557 and 558 or in articles 568 and 569 by a group of persons acting in concert, if they do so deliberately, either to facilitate the general criminal activity of the group or serve its purposes, or knowing the intention of the group to commit such an offence is punished by a sentence of ten (10) to twenty (20) years of criminal imprisonment.

###### Art. 750

Any person unable to justify resources corresponding with their lifestyle who maintains relations with one or more persons engaging in one or more of the offences relating to terrorism as provided for in this code is punished by a sentence of one (01) to five (05) years of imprisonment and a fine of ten million (10,000,000) to thirty million (30,000,000) CFA francs.

##### Section 2: Liability of legal persons

###### Art. 751

Any legal person, Togolese or foreign, committing an offence of financing of terrorism or against the safety of air, maritime or fixed platform navigation, is liable to dissolution if its registered office is located in Togolese territory and a permanent ban on operation of its branch if its registered office is located abroad as well as a fine of five hundred million (500,000,000) to ten billion (10,000,000,000) CFA francs and any other sentence provided for by article 54 of this code, without prejudice to civil reparations that could be pronounced against it and the criminal liability of natural persons who committed the offences.

#### Chapitre 6: Provisions relating to jurisdiction

###### Art. 752

Togolese courts have jurisdiction to hear offences provided for in this chapter when an offence has been committed:

1) in whole or in part on the territory of the Togolese State or the damage resulting from these offences has been suffered in whole or in part on Togolese territory;

2) on board or against a vessel flying the Togolese flag, an aircraft registered in accordance with its legislation or a fixed platform located on its continental shelf; 3) on board or against an aircraft leased without crew to a person who has the principal place of their business or, failing that, their permanent residence on the territory of the Togolese State.

###### Art. 753: Togolese courts also have jurisdiction

1) if the offence has been committed by a national of the Togolese State;

2) in the case of an offence involving aircraft, whether it be aircraft hijacking or an offence against the safety of civil aviation, if the offence has been committed on board the aircraft and if the said aircraft lands on the territory of the Togolese State with the alleged perpetrator of the offence still on board;

3) in the case of an offence classified as hostage-taking, if the offence has been committed in order to compel the Togolese State to perform any act or to refrain from doing so;

4) or in the case of an offence committed against persons enjoying international protection, if the offence has been committed by virtue of the functions they exercise on the territory of the Togolese State.

###### Art. 754

For the purposes of application of this chapter, and subject to the two preceding articles, an act constitutes an offence regardless of the place where it was committed.

###### Art. 755

Togolese courts have jurisdiction to try the perpetrators of the offences provided for in this chapter in the case where any person suspected of one of these offences is located on the territory of the Togolese State and regardless of the nationality of the alleged perpetrator or their stateless status.

To this end, the matter must be submitted to the competent judicial authority for it to initiate criminal proceedings, without undue delay and without any exception, whether or not the offence was committed on its territory, unless a decision has been made to extradite this person.

### TITRE VI

OFFENCES RELATING TO THE PROTECTION OF THE ENVIRONMENT AND NATURAL RESOURCES

#### Chapitre I: Offences relating to the protection of the environment

###### Art. 756

Those who are liable to a fine of one hundred thousand (100,000) to five hundred thousand (500,000) CFA francs:

1) hold or abandon waste in violation of legislative provisions in force, except those relating to the importation of toxic nuclear waste or radioactive products;

2) carry out discharges in violation of the regimes of prohibition and prior authorisation enacted by orders by the minister responsible for the environment.

###### Art. 757

Are liable to a fine of one hundred thousand (100,000) to one million (1,000,000) CFA francs those who:

1) neglect to hand over the waste they produced or held, under the conditions provided for by the regulations in force, to the public services or approved legal entities responsible for their disposal, in application of municipal or prefectoral waste disposal plans and the prescriptions of the minister responsible for the environment relating to the handing over of such waste as provided for by the environment code;

2) carry out prohibited discharges or carry out without authorization discharges subject to authorization in application of the provisions of the environment code or contravene the conditions of the authorization of which they were holders;

3) carry out water intakes, set up on the public domain facilities, connections or installations, or dig a well with a view to carrying out water intakes, without the authorization provided for by the environment code or outside the limits and conditions thereof;

4) open, establish, enlarge, increase the production capacity, substantially modify the technical characteristics of an installation listed in the nomenclature provided for in article 63 of the environment code or have commenced works to these effects without the required authorization, have disregarded the regulations applicable to their installation or the prescriptions of the authorization of which they were holders;

5) introduce or attempt to introduce into Togo a new animal or plant species without the authorization of the minister responsible for the environment provided for by the environment code.

###### Art. 758

Are liable to a fine of two hundred thousand (200,000) to two million (2,000,000) CFA francs those who:

1) transmit information and statistics, which they were legally required to provide, that are voluntarily erroneous or incomplete;

2) obstruct or attempt to obstruct controls legally organized pursuant to the provisions of the environment code and those of the regulations adopted for its application;

3) manufacture, establish, hold with a view to sale or making available to a user, transport products, substances or materials prohibited in application of a decision to suspend or withdraw the authorization for discharges by the minister responsible for the environment and the competent authorities, carry out one of these operations without authorization when it was legally required or contravene the provisions of the authorization of which they were holders.

###### Art. 759

Are liable to a fine of five hundred thousand (500,000) to five million (5,000,000) CFA francs those who disregard the provisions of article 52 of the environment code and those of the authorizations they provide for, as well as those who have unduly appropriated the materials and equipment concerned.

###### Art. 760

Are liable to the maximum term of criminal imprisonment and a fine of five million (5,000,000) to one hundred million (100,000,000) those who import, purchase, sell, transport, store or stock toxic waste and radioactive products dangerous for the environment and originating from abroad.

If the offence was committed within the framework of the activity of a legal entity, that legal entity and its directors are criminally liable. The legal entity is punished by the penalties provided for by article 54 of this code.

However, any natural person, whether or not an agent of that legal entity, who, without being an author or accomplice, has nevertheless contributed thereto through negligence by reason of the functions they perform in the management, control or supervision of that activity, is punished by a sentence of five (05) to ten (10) years of criminal imprisonment and a fine of five million (5,000,000) to one hundred million (100,000,000) CFA francs.

###### Art. 761

The destruction and commercialization, direct or indirect, without right of protected animal or plant species pursuant to the legislative and regulatory provisions in force and the international conventions to which the Republic of Togo is a party is punished by a sentence of one (01) to five (05) year(s) of imprisonment and a fine of one million (1,000,000) to fifty million (50,000,000) CFA francs, without prejudice to any other provision of this code.

###### Art. 762

Except for the cases provided for in paragraphs 1 and 2 of article 760, when the offences referred to in articles 756 to 761 of this code result in serious and manifest harm to a natural environment, to flora, to property, to a protected environmental zone or to human health, the penalties incurred are doubled.

The same applies when the author or accomplice of the offence belongs to bodies of civil servants and agents of public services responsible in any capacity for the protection of the interests referred to in article one of the environment code.

###### Art. 763

When, following one of the offences provided for in articles 756 to 761 above except for the cases provided for in paragraphs 1 and 2 of article 760, the State, a local authority or a public establishment incurs costs to eliminate waste, remove installations, facilities and restore the premises, restore a natural environment degraded by the abandonment of waste or illegal discharges, quarries or mines, repopulate a biotope with animal or plant species, repair damage caused to public property by illegal discharges, the court orders the author or authors of the offence to reimburse the costs it considers can reasonably be attributed to their fault.

###### Art. 764

The court orders the confiscation, destruction or disposal of products, materials, substances and equipment whose manufacture, importation, possession with a view to sale or making available to a user is prohibited.

The environmental services are responsible for this destruction or disposal and take all useful measures with a view to preventing the dangers that could result therefrom.

#### Chapter II: Offences relating to the protection of natural resources

##### Section 1: Offences relating to the protection of water

###### Art. 765

In accordance with the provisions of article 41 of the water code, any waste of water, duly established by the authorities responsible for the management of water resources, is subject to a warning. In case of recurrence within a period of one (01) year from the warning, the author of the waste is liable to a fine ranging from one hundred thousand (100,000) to five hundred thousand (500,000) CFA francs.

The competent court may further order the suspension or cessation of all works or activities, the total or partial prohibition of the operation of the installation or structure.

###### Art. 766

Whoever knowingly throws, discharges, or allows to flow, into surface or underground waters, directly or indirectly, any waste or any substance whatsoever whose action or reactions cause or are likely to cause, even temporarily, harmful effects on human health or harm to biological diversity or to the balance of aquatic ecosystems is punished by a sentence of imprisonment ranging from six (06) months to two (02) years and a fine ranging from five hundred thousand (500,000) to twenty million (20,000,000) CFA francs and the repair of damaged structures, without prejudice to any other provision of this code.

###### Art. 767

Anyone who, through negligence, lack of precaution or breach of safety regulations, destroys or damages, by any means whatsoever, all or part of the hydraulic facilities and works identified in article 6 of the water code, is liable to a fine of five hundred thousand (500,000) to twenty million (20,000,000) CFA francs and to the repair of the damaged works.

###### Art. 768

Anyone who, within an immediate or close protection perimeter of a water extraction point, carries out deposits, constructs or operates an installation, or carries out an activity despite a prohibition enacted pursuant to the water code is punished by a term of imprisonment of six (06) months to two (02) years and a fine of one hundred thousand (100,000) to two million (2,000,000) CFA francs, without prejudice to any other provision of this code.

In case of recidivism, the penalties incurred are doubled.

###### Art. 769

Anyone who, within a distant protection perimeter of a water extraction point or within a protection area of a body of water allocated to human consumption, contravenes a legal prescription or a prohibition measure enacted pursuant to the water code, is punished by a term of imprisonment of one (01) to six (06) months and a fine of one hundred thousand (100,000) to five hundred thousand (500,000) CFA francs.

In case of recidivism, the penalties incurred are doubled.

###### Art. 770

Any person who carries out, without a prior environmental impact study, activities, installations, facilities and large-scale hydraulic works in violation of the criteria, standards and measures enacted in this matter by the competent authority is punished by a term of imprisonment of six (06) months to two (02) years and a fine of two million (2,000,000) to fifty million (50,000,000) CFA francs or one of these two penalties.

###### Art. 771

Anyone who, without authorization or declaration, knowingly, carries out water extractions, or carries out, modifies or operates works, installations or works, or carries out activities subject to declaration or authorization pursuant to articles 13 and 14 of the water code, is liable to a fine ranging from one hundred thousand (100,000) to five hundred thousand (500,000) CFA francs.

Anyone who carries out water extractions, or carries out, modifies or operates works, installations or works subject to concession pursuant to article 19 of the water code is punished by a fine ranging from five hundred thousand (500,000) to fifty million (50,000,000) CFA francs.

In case of recidivism, the penalties incurred are doubled.

The competent court may further order the suspension or cessation of all works or activities, the total or partial prohibition of the operation of the installation or work, the destruction of the installation or work together with the restoration of the premises.

All these ancillary sanctions may be placed under penalty payment.

###### Art. 772

Anyone who carries out a borehole, a well or a collection drain, operates or carries out extractions of groundwater or surface water without submitting to the necessary authorizations pursuant to the water code, is punished by a fine ranging from one hundred thousand (100,000) to five hundred thousand (500,000) CFA francs.

###### Art. 773

Offenders against the provisions of article 58 of the water code, relating to unauthorized discharges, are liable to a fine ranging from one hundred thousand (100,000) to five million (5,000,000) CFA francs.

###### Art. 774: The following are prohibited, in any form whatsoever

1) the sale and distribution of water not conforming to the standards in force;

2) the introduction of non-potable water for the preparation and preservation of all foodstuffs and goods intended for food.

Anyone who contravenes the provisions of the preceding paragraph is punished by a term of imprisonment of one (01) to six (06) months, and a fine ranging from one hundred thousand (100,000) to five million (5,000,000) CFA francs or one of the two penalties, without prejudice to civil liability actions.

In case of recidivism, the imprisonment penalties incurred may be increased to five (05) years.

These sanctions are without prejudice to the confiscation of operating equipment, the suspension or cessation of all works or activities, the temporary or permanent closure of the legal person responsible for distribution and any other penalty provided for by article 54 of this code in case of conviction of a legal person, which may be ordered by the competent court seized for this purpose.

###### Art. 775

Any natural or legal person who, in order to obtain the necessary authorizations for the construction of boreholes, wells, aqueducts, sewers or for the operation of a water supply, distribution or treatment system, uses false declarations, documents, analyses or any other fraudulent maneuver of any nature whatsoever is liable to the same penalties as those provided for in the preceding article.

###### Art. 776

Anyone who, through negligence or deliberate refusal to comply with legal requirements, fails to carry out the periodic potability analyses provided for in article 77 of the water code, is liable to a fine ranging from one hundred thousand (100,000) to ten million (10,000,000) CFA francs, without prejudice to the confiscation of operating equipment, the suspension or cessation of all works or activities, the temporary or permanent closure of the responsible legal person and any other penalty provided for by article 54 of this code in case of conviction of a legal person, which may be ordered by the competent court.

###### Art. 777

Any person who introduces, through negligence or imprudence, materials likely to harm the healthiness of any water, discharges wastewater directly into nature without any precaution, abandons objects, putrescible bodies in natural or artificial waters, or waters, bathes or washes animals in waters allocated to human consumption, is punished by a fine ranging from one hundred thousand (100,000) to five hundred thousand (500,000) CFA francs.

Such person may, in addition, be sentenced by the competent court to the performance of work of general interest the maximum duration of which may not exceed two (02) months.

###### Art. 778

The distribution of the proceeds of fines pronounced in application of this section is carried out by regulatory means.

##### Section 2

Offenses relating to the protection of forest and wildlife resources

###### Art. 779

Any exploitation without authorization of the resources of the forest domain of the State or of a territorial collectivity constitutes a criminal offense and is punished by a term of imprisonment of six (06) months to two (02) years and a fine of one hundred thousand (100,000) CFA francs to two million (2,000,000) CFA francs or one of these two penalties.

###### Art. 780

The following are punished by a term of imprisonment of six (06) months to two (02) years and a fine of one hundred thousand (100,000) CFA francs to two million (2,000,000) CFA francs or one of these two penalties:

1) any holder of a cutting permit who has exploited beyond the quantity of products authorized; 2) any purchaser of cutting, any holder of a forest management contract, who has felled or harvested in his cutting or on the land defined by his permit under the contract, products other than those defined by the specifications.

Any person engaging in fraudulent maneuvers tending to evade the fees due, or exploiting in a place other than that designated by his permit or having exploited products in the parts of forests located outside the perimeters defined by his exploitation title is punished by the same penalties.

###### Art. 781

Any holder of a logging permit or any purchaser of a logging operation or their representative who engages in any fraudulent maneuvers intended to pass off as coming from their logging operation wood or other forest products cut or harvested outside the perimeter of their logging operation by a third party, or who facilitates such maneuvers, is punished by a term of imprisonment of six (06) months to two (02) years and, jointly and severally with the principal perpetrators of the offense, by a fine of one hundred thousand (100,000) to five hundred thousand (500,000) CFA francs or by one of these penalties, without prejudice to confiscations or restitutions and damages.

###### Art. 782

Any holder of a logging permit, or of a forest management contract, any purchaser of a logging operation is civilly liable for damage caused by persons under their authority.

###### Art. 783

Any natural or legal person who engages for profit in logging, sawing and storage operations of wood without paying the related taxes provided for by the forestry code is punished by a fine corresponding to three (03) times the duty normally owed. This fine is increased by two hundred percent (200%) in case of recidivism.

###### Art. 784

Is punished by a term of imprisonment of one (01) to six (06) months and by a fine of one hundred thousand (100,000) to two million (2,000,000) CFA francs anyone who:

1) counterfeits or falsifies the duly registered marks of hammers;

2) uses counterfeit or falsified hammers;

3) has improperly obtained genuine hammers, with a view to fraudulent use;

4) removes or attempts to remove the marks of these hammers.

If these hammers are used for the marks of the forest resources administration, the term of imprisonment is increased from six (06) months to two (02) years.

###### Art. 785

The use of prohibited forest exploitation means and equipment is punished by a fine of one hundred thousand (100,000) to two million (2,000,000) CFA francs and by a term of imprisonment of one (01) month to six (06) months or by one of these two penalties, without prejudice to the confiscation of products and work equipment.

###### Art. 786

The import, export and re-export of unauthorized woody and non-woody forest products are punished by a penalty of six (06) months to two (02) years of imprisonment and by a fine of one hundred thousand (100,000) to two million (2,000,000) CFA francs or by one of these two penalties.

###### Art. 787

The unauthorized extraction or removal of stones, sand, peat, earth, turf, gravel, leaves, bark, roots, vines, flowers or any product in protection zones, with the exception of sacred sites referred to in article 642 of this code, are punished by a fine of one hundred thousand (100,000) to two million (2,000,000) CFA francs. In case of recidivism, an additional penalty of fifteen (15) days to one (01) month of community service is imposed.

###### Art. 788

Is punished by a fine of one hundred thousand (100,000) to two million (2,000,000) CFA francs and by a term of imprisonment of one (01) to six (06) months or by one of these two penalties, anyone who has hunted inside protected areas.

###### Art. 789

Hunting with prohibited means and hunting weapons, hunting without a permit or outside the authorized period in non-prohibited zones, night hunting and the killing of pregnant females, are punished by a term of imprisonment of one (01) to six (06) months and by a fine of one hundred thousand (100,000) to five hundred thousand (500,000) CFA francs or by one of these two penalties, without prejudice to the confiscation of products, equipment and hunting weapons.

###### Art. 790

Deposits of rubble, detritus, plastic bags, greasy papers, detergents, refuse of any kind in protected areas and reforestation perimeters are punished by a fine of one hundred thousand (100,000) to five hundred thousand (500,000) CFA francs, without prejudice to the obligation to make reparation through damages.

###### Art. 791

Any infringement of the regulations on land clearing and cultivation inside protected areas or in defense zones, any illegal occupation inside said zones are punished by a fine of one hundred thousand (100,000) to two million (2,000,000) CFA francs and by a term of imprisonment of one (01) to six (06) months, or by one of these two penalties.

###### Art. 792

Any infringement of the regulations on bush fires and forest fires is punished by a fine of one hundred thousand (100,000) to two million (2,000,000) CFA francs and by a term of imprisonment of six (06) months to two (02) years or by one of these two penalties, without prejudice to damages.

The term of imprisonment is mandatory when the fire has destroyed artificial plantations or a wooded area of at least fifty (50) hectares.

###### Art. 793

Anyone who through imprudence, negligence, inattention, non-observance of regulations, causes a fire in forest and wildlife domains, is punished by a term of imprisonment of one (01) to six (06) months and by a fine of one hundred thousand (100,000) to five hundred thousand (500,000) CFA francs or by one of these two penalties.

In case of intentional fire, the penalty is five (05) to ten (10) years of criminal imprisonment, if it results in loss of human life.

Parents or legal guardians, masters and principals are civilly liable for reparations imposed against minor children and employees, perpetrators of this fire.

###### Art. 794

Anyone who grazes or pens animals under their care or allows them to roam in parts of the forest domain not open to passage or placed under defense and whose boundaries are clearly marked, is punished by a term of imprisonment of one (01) month to six

(06) months and by a fine of one hundred thousand (100,000) CFA francs to two million (2,000,000) CFA francs or by one of these two penalties, without prejudice to the restoration of the premises.

###### Art. 795

The felling, pruning or pollarding without authorization of protected species, even to feed livestock, are punished by a fine of one hundred thousand (100,000) CFA francs to five hundred thousand (500,000) CFA francs, without prejudice to damages.

###### Art. 796

Anyone who circulates, sells, imports, exports or transits live wild animals, trophies without authorization is punished by a term of imprisonment of one (01) to six (06) months and by a fine of one hundred thousand (100,000) to five hundred thousand (500,000) CFA francs or by one of these two penalties.

###### Art. 797

Is punished by a term of imprisonment of one (01) to six (06) months and by a fine of one hundred thousand (100,000) to two million (2,000,000) CFA francs or by one of these two penalties, anyone who falsifies, attempts to falsify or transfers management plans, logging or land clearing permits, hunting permits, management contracts, or any documents relating to forest exploitation and management.

###### Art. 798

Anyone who destroys, moves or causes to disappear all or part of the boundary markers, marks or crops used to delimit the forest domains of the State and territorial collectivities is punished by a term of imprisonment of one (01) to six (06) months and by a fine of one hundred thousand (100,000) to two million (2,000,000) CFA francs or by one of these two penalties, all without prejudice to the restoration of the premises.

###### Art. 799

Anyone who obstructs the actions of agents of the forest resources administration or other specially appointed agents is punished by a penalty of six (06) days to one (01) month of community service and by a fine of one hundred thousand (100,000) to two million (2,000,000) CFA francs or by one of these two penalties.

In case of recidivism, the penalty is increased to one (01) month of imprisonment.

###### Art. 800

Anyone who evades or, without valid justification, fails to comply with a written and proper requisition made by the administrative authority for the purpose of fighting a large-scale fire is punished by a fine of one hundred thousand (100,000) to two million (2,000,000) CFA francs.

###### Art. 801

Any holder of a professional card in case of infringement of the provisions of this chapter, may have their professional card withdrawn by the judge, upon request of the forestry resources administration, or be prohibited from engaging in trade of forestry and wildlife products.

###### Art. 802

The penalties incurred for infringements of the above provisions are doubled when the perpetrator of the offence or their accomplice is a civil servant or an agent of the public services responsible for ensuring the enforcement of the provisions of the forestry code and its implementing texts.

###### Art. 803

The provisions of this section apply without prejudice to the other provisions of this code or any other legislative provisions in force.

###### Art. 804

Infringements of regulatory provisions relating to forestry exploitation are punished by:

1) a term of imprisonment of one (01) month to two (02) years and a fine equal to three times the value of the cutting permit or one of these two penalties;

2) confiscation of the products and exploitation equipment.

###### Art. 805

Any transport of forestry exploitation products not accompanied by the pass provided for by the regulatory provisions in force is punished by:

1) a fine equal to three (03) times the commercial value of the transported products;

2) the placing under sequestration of vehicles used for transporting the products until the payment of the required settlement which cannot exceed one month;

3) after expiration of the period of one (01) month, the sequestered vehicles are sold at public auction.

###### Art. 806

Mechanical sawmills and pit saws used by pit sawyers are the only work equipment authorized to produce sawn timber throughout the entire national territory.

The use of mechanical chainsaws as sawing equipment is strictly prohibited throughout the entire national territory for any operator of forestry species.

Any operator of forestry species who uses mechanical chainsaws as sawing equipment is liable to a term of imprisonment of six (06) months to two (02) years and a fine of one hundred thousand (100,000) to two million (2,000,000) CFA francs or one of these two penalties, without prejudice to the confiscation of products and work equipment.

In case of recidivism, the prison sentence is mandatory.

##### Section 3: Offences relating to plant protection

###### Art. 807

Is liable to a term of imprisonment of one (01) to six (06) months and a fine of one hundred thousand (100,000) to five hundred thousand (500,000) CFA francs or one of these two penalties, anyone who introduces, possesses, knowingly transports quarantine harmful organisms, in infringement of the legislative provisions in force.

In case of recidivism, these penalties are doubled from the maxima set above.

###### Art. 808

Is liable to a term of imprisonment of one (01) to six (06) months and a fine of two hundred thousand (200,000) to twenty million (20,000,000) CFA francs or one of these penalties anyone who imports, manufactures, packages or repackages, stores, experiments with, uses or places on the market any plant protection product not authorized or not approved, in accordance with the procedure provided for by law.

If the offence is committed within the framework of the activity of a legal person, that legal person and its directors are criminally liable. The legal person is punished by the penalties provided for by Article 54 of this code.

In case of recidivism, these penalties are doubled from the maxima set above.

###### Art. 809

Is liable to a term of imprisonment of one (01) to six (06) months and a fine of two hundred thousand (200,000) to ten million (10,000,000) CFA francs or one of these two penalties, anyone who places on the market an authorized or approved plant protection product whose chemical, biological or physical composition has been modified or whose intended use has been changed, without submitting this modification or this change to the examination of the plant protection products committee.

If the offence is committed within the framework of the activity of a legal person, that legal person and its directors are criminally liable. The legal person is punished by the penalties provided for by Article 54 of this code.

In case of recidivism, these penalties are doubled from the maxima set above.

###### Art. 810

Is liable to a fine of two hundred thousand (200,000) to five million (5,000,000) CFA francs, anyone who:

1) disseminates any advertising for a product that does not have a provisional sales authorization or approval; 2) mentions, in the advertising of an authorized or approved product, indications not contained in the authorization or approval or not in conformity with the laws and regulations in force.

In case of recidivism, this fine is doubled from the maximum set above.

###### Art. 811

Any person who obstructs the accomplishment, by plant protection agents, of the missions entrusted to them by law or the texts adopted for its application, is punished by a term of five (05) to fifteen (15) days' imprisonment or six (06) days to one (01) month of community service and a fine of one hundred thousand (100,000) to two million (2,000,000) CFA francs.

In case of recidivism, the penalty is increased to one (01) month's imprisonment.

###### Art. 812

In all cases, plant protection products introduced in violation of the rules referred to in Articles 808 and 809 above are confiscated.

##### Section 4: Offences against fishing regulations

###### Art. 813

Is punished by a term of imprisonment of one (01) month to three (03) years and a fine of one hundred thousand (100,000) to five million (5,000,000) CFA francs or one of these two penalties anyone who has:

1) fished without holding at least a valid license or authorization;

2) fished during prohibited periods or below the limits set by the regulations;

3) fished in protected areas without special authorization;

4) failed to mark their gear or used a fishing method without complying with the requirements;

5) transported, offered for sale or sold fish declared unwholesome by agents of the directorate of livestock and fisheries.

###### Art. 814

Anyone who imports or exports spawn, fry or other aquatic animals intended for farming, without authorization from the directorate of livestock and fisheries, is punished by a term of imprisonment of one (01) to six (06) months and a fine of one hundred thousand (100,000) to five hundred thousand (500,000) CFA francs or one of these two penalties.

###### Art. 815

Anyone who violates the regulatory provisions on health control, sanitary conditions for the production and marketing of fishery products is punished by a term of imprisonment of six (06) months to two (02) years and a fine of one hundred thousand (100,000) to two million (2,000,000) CFA francs or one of these two penalties.

###### Art. 816

Anyone who prevents or opposes a visit on board by an agent in the exercise of their duties or refuses to present their license or fishing authorization, is punished by a term of five (05) to fifteen (15) days' imprisonment or six (06) days to one (01) month of community service and a fine of one hundred thousand (100,000) to two million (2,000,000) CFA francs.

In case of recidivism, the penalty is increased to one (01) month's imprisonment.

###### Art. 817

Anyone who creates without authorization an aquaculture, preparation or processing establishment for fishery products is punished by imprisonment of one (01) to six (06) months and a fine of one hundred thousand (100,000) to two million (2,000,000) CFA francs or one of these two penalties.

The establishment may be closed or destroyed at the expense of the offender.

###### Art. 818

The penalties provided for in this section are doubled in case of recidivism.

###### Art. 819

Prohibited gear is seized and its destruction is ordered by the president of the court upon simple application. Fraudulent products are seized, confiscated and sold at public auction for the benefit of the State.

##### Section 5 : Des infractions relatives à la protection des ressources minières

###### Art. 820

Is punished by imprisonment of one (01) to five (05) years and a fine of five million (5,000,000) to fifty million (50,000,000) CFA francs, or one of these two penalties, anyone who:

1) engages in an illicit manner in the extraction, purchase or sale of mineral substances; in these cases, these substances are seized and their confiscation is pronounced by the courts;

2) knowingly makes a false declaration to obtain a mining title or a marketing authorization or knowingly keeps false registers, plans, account books or other documents or knowingly files false reports or other documents;

3) contravenes, in a serious and repeated manner, obligations concerning the hygiene or safety of persons, property, or obligations on the environment or the deposit or other technical aspects;

4) fails, in a substantial and repeated manner, to meet administrative or fiscal obligations;

5) commits in a continuous or repeated manner acts or negligence sanctioned in the following article.

The government may, by act having the same nature as the act granting the mining title or marketing authorization, cancel any title or any authorization of a person convicted of one of the offenses specified above.

###### Art. 821

Is punished by imprisonment of six (06) months to two (02) years and a fine of five hundred thousand (500,000) to twenty million (20,000,000) CFA francs, or one of these two penalties, anyone who:

1) destroys, moves or illicitly modifies the boundary markers of perimeters;

2) falsifies the inscriptions on a mining title or a marketing authorization;

3) purchases mineral substances from a person who does not hold a mining title, without taking the usual precautions to know the status of the person with whom he deals;

4) provides materially inaccurate information relating to an application for a mining title or a marketing authorization, fails to establish the registers, plans, account books or required documents, keeps them in an incomplete or inaccurate state or neglects to file reports or other documents or to give the required notices;

5) conducts activities in a manner that does not respect the rules of the art or that endangers persons, property, the environment, or a deposit;

6) fails to make, in due time, a payment due to the government, except in case of good faith dispute;

7) refuses to grant the administration access to activities or to registers, plans, account books and other documents or to execute a legitimate order emanating from the director general or his agents;

8) commits in a continuous or repeated manner acts or negligence sanctioned in the following article.

###### Art. 822

Is punished by a fine of five hundred thousand (500,000) to twenty million (20,000,000) CFA francs anyone who:

1) neglects to keep registers, plans, account books and other documents in a complete, accurate and up-to-date manner;

2) neglects to file reports and other documents in due time;

3) fails to conduct his activities in a regular and prudent manner or to observe regulations or instructions, even if this does not endanger the hygiene or safety of persons and property or the environment or a deposit.

###### Art. 823

The penalties provided for by this section may be increased up to double in case of recidivism within twelve (12) months following the first conviction.

##### Section 6 : Des infractions relatives aux hydrocarbures

###### Art. 824

Is punished by imprisonment of one (01) to five (05) year(s) and a fine of five million (5,000,000) to fifty million (50,000,000) CFA francs, or one of these two penalties, anyone who engages in an illicit manner in prospecting, exploration or exploitation work for hydrocarbons on the territory of the Togolese Republic and in its territorial waters.

###### Art. 825

Is punished by imprisonment of six (06) months to two (02) years and a fine of five hundred thousand (500,000) to twenty million (20,000,000) CFA francs, or one of these two penalties, anyone who:

1) destroys, moves or illicitly modifies the boundary markers, reference markers and reference points in the perimeter of a petroleum title;

2) falsifies the inscriptions on a petroleum title;

3) makes use of falsified petroleum titles;

4) provides materially inaccurate information relating to an application for a mining title or a marketing authorization, fails to establish the registers, plans, account books or required documents, keeps them in an incomplete or inaccurate state or neglects to file reports or other documents or to give the required notices;

5) conducts activities in a manner that does not respect the rules of the art or that endangers persons, property, the environment, or a deposit.

###### Art. 826

All other offenses provided for in the provisions of the hydrocarbons code and in its implementing texts are punished by a fine of five hundred thousand (500,000) to twenty million (20,000,000) CFA francs.

###### Art. 827

In case of recidivism, the penalties provided for in the provisions of this section are doubled.

###### Art. 828

The net proceeds of fines pronounced are distributed respectively for three quarters (3/4) and one quarter (1/4) between the State and the fund for promotion and development of petroleum activities.

### TITRE VII : DES INFRACTIONS RELATIVES A LA SANTE PUBLIQUE

#### Chapitre I

Des infractions relatives à la reproduction et à la protection des personnes en matière de VIH et SIDA

##### Section 1 : Des infractions relatives à la reproduction

###### Paragraphe 1 : De l'interruption volontaire de grossesse

###### Art. 829

Maneuvers practiced with a view to voluntary or induced termination of a pregnancy using remedies, substances, instruments or any objects whatsoever are prohibited, except in the following cases:

1) when the continuation of the pregnancy endangers the life and health of the pregnant woman;

2) at the request of the woman, when the pregnancy is the consequence of rape or an incestuous relationship;
3) when there exists, at the time of diagnosis, a strong probability that the unborn child will be affected by a condition of particular severity. In this last case, the treating physician has the obligation to inform the couple who will or will not make the decision to terminate the pregnancy.

The offense provided for by the preceding paragraph may be committed either by the woman on herself, or by a third party on the woman.

###### Art. 830

Any author, co-author, and accomplice of an infringement of the provisions of the preceding article is punished by a term of imprisonment of six (06) months to two (02) years and a fine of five hundred thousand (500,000) to two million (2,000,000) CFA francs or one of these two penalties. The courts order, in all cases, the confiscation of remedies, substances and other objects seized. They may, in addition, pronounce with regard to the convicted person the temporary suspension or incapacity to exercise the profession on the occasion of which the offence was committed.

Is punished by the same penalties:

1) anyone who provokes an abortion even though this attempt has not been followed by effect;

2) anyone who sells, offers for sale or causes to be sold, distributes or causes to be distributed, in any manner whatsoever, remedies, substances, instruments or any objects having been used for voluntary termination of pregnancy, knowing that they were intended to commit the offence provided for in article 829, even though this voluntary termination of pregnancy has been neither carried out nor attempted, and even though these remedies, substances, instruments or any objects proposed as effective means of terminating a pregnancy would, in reality, be unfit to achieve them.

###### Art. 831

In case of serious and permanent disability, the penalty is increased from two (02) to five (05) years of imprisonment. In case of death of the victim, the penalty is five (05) to ten (10) years of criminal imprisonment.

###### Art. 832

Any doctor who draws up a medical certificate of convenience for therapeutic abortion or who would be an accomplice thereto is punished by a term of imprisonment of six (06) months to two (02) years and a fine of five hundred thousand (500,000) to two million (2,000,000) CFA francs or one of these two penalties only.

###### Paragraphe 2 : Des infractions relatives à la contraception

###### Art. 833

The research and manufacture of contraceptive products belong to public or private pharmaceutical laboratories and pharmacies approved by the competent public authority.

Anyone who, not having the required qualification, undertakes the manufacture of contraceptive products, is punished by a penalty of six (06) months to two (02) years of imprisonment and a fine of five hundred thousand (500,000) to two million (2,000,000) CFA francs.

###### Art. 834

The importation of contraceptive products belongs to public or private pharmaceutical laboratories and pharmacies and to any other person approved by the competent public authority and having the qualification and competence required to do so.

Anyone who undertakes the importation of contraceptive products in violation of the preceding paragraph, is punished by a penalty of six (06) months to two (02) years of imprisonment and a fine of five hundred thousand (500,000) to two million (2,000,000) CFA francs.

###### Art. 835

The distribution, offering for sale and sale of contraceptive products with the exception of condoms, belong to the State or to any other natural or legal person holding the approval of the competent public authority.

Anyone who undertakes the distribution, offering for sale and sale of contraceptive products without having the required qualification and competence, and without authorization from the competent public authority, is punished by a penalty of six (06) months to two (02) years of imprisonment and a fine of five hundred thousand (500,000) to two million (2,000,000) CFA francs.

###### Art. 836

The prescription of contraceptive methods belongs to qualified health personnel approved by the competent authority.

Any person who acts in this matter without the required qualification, is punished by a penalty of six (06) months to two (02) years of imprisonment and five hundred thousand (500,000) to two million (2,000,000) CFA francs fine.

###### Art. 837

Any promotional action in favor of contraception, by writing, printed matter, audiovisual advertising, words or by any other means, is subject to approval by the competent public authority.

Anyone who undertakes this act of promotion without the required authorization is punished by a penalty of one (01) to six (06) months of imprisonment and one hundred thousand (100,000) to five hundred thousand (500,000) CFA francs fine or one of these two penalties.

###### Paragraphe 3 : Des infractions en matière d'assistance médicale à la procréation

###### Art. 838

Medical assistance with procreation, as defined by the legislation in force, must take place only in public or private hospital health structures, equipped with means and qualified and competent personnel, having the approval of the competent public authority.

Anyone who contravenes the provisions concerning the place and quality of the provider is punished by a penalty of six (06) months to two (02) years of imprisonment and five hundred thousand (500,000) to two million (2,000,000) CFA francs fine and withdrawal of practice approval.

##### Section 2

Des infractions relatives à la protection des personnes en matière de VIH et SIDA

###### Art. 839

Any public or private health establishment which, through negligence or imprudence, contravenes its obligations regarding prevention of contamination of blood and its derivatives, tissues and organs by HIV and by other pathogenic agents transmitted by blood is punished by a fine of five hundred thousand (500,000) to two million (2,000,000) CFA francs, without prejudice to civil and disciplinary actions liable to be brought against them.

###### Art. 840

Is punished by a term of imprisonment of one (01) to six (06) months and a fine of one hundred thousand (100,000) to five hundred thousand (500,000) CFA francs or one of these two penalties, anyone who, outside the cases provided for by law, proceeds to HIV screening on a person without their consent.

###### Art. 841

Is punished by a penalty of one (01) to six (06) months of imprisonment and a fine of one hundred thousand (100,000) to five hundred thousand (500,000) CFA francs, the act of recording or causing to be recorded, keeping or causing to be kept, nominative information in violation of the rules of collection, recording and conservation.

The act of disclosing or publishing, in any form whatsoever, the nominative information referred to in the preceding paragraph, outside the cases expressly provided for by law, is punished by a penalty of six (06) months to two (02) years of imprisonment and a fine of five hundred thousand (500,000) to two million (2,000,000) CFA francs.

#### Chapitre II : Des infractions aux mesures sanitaires

###### Art. 842

Any person who refuses to comply with the requisitions of a health service, addressed to them by an official authorized for this purpose, is punished by a fine of one hundred thousand (100,000) to two million (2,000,000) CFA francs and a term of imprisonment of one (01) to six (06) months or one of these two penalties.

##### Section 1 : Des infractions relatives à la pollution et à l'hygiène

###### Art. 843 : Quiconque, par négligence ou imprudence

1) damages public or commercial works intended to receive or conduct water for consumption;

2) allows the introduction of excremental matter, wastewater or any other matter likely to harm the healthiness of water from springs, fountains, wells, cisterns, conduits, aqueducts, reservoirs serving public consumption;

3) abandons animal carcasses, butchery debris, manure, excremental matter or any putrescible animal residues in cracks or excavations likely to contaminate water delivered for drinking and consumption, is sentenced by the competent court to a penalty of one (01) to three (03) months of imprisonment or to the performance of work of general interest the maximum duration of which cannot exceed two (02) months and to the payment of a fine of one hundred thousand (100,000) to five hundred thousand (500,000) CFA francs.

###### Art. 844

Any concessionaire of a drinking water distribution station who, through inattention, negligence, lack of precaution, failure to observe health regulations or the requirements of a specifications document, has caused the delivery of drinking water likely to harm public health is liable to a term of imprisonment of one (01) to six (06) months and a fine of one hundred thousand (100,000) to five million (5,000,000) CFA francs or one of these two penalties.

###### Art. 845

Any other infringement of the provisions of the public health code relating to hygiene measures, duly recorded by a verification report drawn up by public hygiene officers in collaboration with the competent technical services, is punishable by a fine of one hundred thousand (100,000) to five hundred thousand (500,000) CFA francs without prejudice to the application of other provisions of this code.

##### Section 2 : Des infractions relatives à la prévention des épidémies

###### Art. 846

Anyone who contravenes the obligation to report contagious diseases, the list of which is established by order of the minister responsible for health, is punished by a fine of one hundred thousand (100,000) to five hundred thousand (500,000) CFA francs.

Anyone who contravenes the obligation of disinfection for diseases subject to mandatory reporting, the list of which is established by order of the minister responsible for health, is punished by the same penalties.

###### Art. 847

Anyone who contravenes the mandatory hygiene and prophylaxis measures taken by decree on the proposal of the minister responsible for health, establishing a state of health alert in a locality or region for a specified and renewable period as necessary, is punished by a term of imprisonment of one (01) to six (06) months and a fine of one hundred thousand (100,000) to two million (2,000,000) CFA francs or one of these two penalties.

##### Section 3 : Des infractions relatives à certains produits et substances réglementés

###### Paragraphe 1 : Des infractions relatives aux médicaments et dispositifs médicaux

###### Art. 848

Any person who contributes to, facilitates, participates in, directly or indirectly, the introduction or sale on the national territory of counterfeit medicines is punished by a term of imprisonment of five (05) to twenty (20) years and a fine of five million (5,000,000) to fifty million (50,000,000) CFA francs, without prejudice to other penalties provided for by this code.

The court shall also order the total confiscation of the medicines and objects of the crime.

A medicine is counterfeit when there is a false representation of its identity and/or its source. This applies to the product, its packaging or any other information concerning the packaging or labelling. Counterfeiting may apply to proprietary products or generic products. Counterfeit products may be products containing the right ingredients or components or wrong ingredients or components, containing

no active ingredient or containing an active ingredient in insufficient quantity or products whose packaging has been falsified.

The following are not assimilated to counterfeiting of medical products:

1) patent violations or disputes concerning patents subject to the provisions of chapter VI of title III of this code;

2) medical products, generic or proprietary, provided that they have obtained marketing authorization in another State;

3) batches not meeting standards, quality defects or non-compliance with good manufacturing or distribution practices for medical products.

###### Art. 849

Any person who contributes to, facilitates, participates directly or indirectly in the introduction into the national territory of medicines or products subject to the pharmaceutical monopoly that are not registered and authorized for importation, is liable to a term of imprisonment of one (01) to six (06) months and a fine of five hundred thousand (500,000) to twenty million (20,000,000) CFA francs or one of these two penalties.

In case of recidivism, these penalties may be doubled.

In addition, goods fraudulently introduced into the national territory are seized as well as any vehicle or equipment involved in the commission of the offence.

Seized medicines may not be sold. They are handed over to the competent services of the ministry responsible for health.

Medicines and products not authorized on the territory, of poor quality or expired, are destroyed under the supervision of the directorate of pharmacies and the judicial authority.

###### Art. 850

Infringements of the regulation of medicine prices committed by pharmacists or staff working under their direct responsibility are punished by fines proportional to the seriousness of the infringement found and not less than five hundred thousand (500,000) CFA francs.

###### Art. 851

Any pharmacist responsible for a medicine manufacturing establishment who has, through his functions or actions, permitted, directly or indirectly, the illegal practice of pharmacy by selling to persons not authorized to trade in them medicines or products presented as conforming to a

pharmacopoeia or by agreeing to manufacture unauthorized products, is subject to the following penalties:

1) a term of imprisonment of up to six (06) months to two (02) years and a fine of two million (2,000,000) to fifteen (15,0000,000) CFA francs; depending on the seriousness of the facts found, or one of these two penalties;

2) temporary suspension from practising the profession of pharmacist in any field whatsoever, pronounced by the competent authority;

3) permanent prohibition pronounced by the judicial authority.

In addition, the medicines and products concerned by the offence are confiscated as well as the equipment used for their manufacture. In all cases, the minister responsible for health may order the administrative closure of the establishment.

###### Art. 852

Any individual who has made visits to doctors, pharmacists or other members of the health professions with a view to providing medical or scientific information or who has developed activities intended to promote the sale of medicines on the national territory without being authorized to do so is liable to a term of imprisonment of one (01) to six (06) months and a fine of one hundred thousand (100,000) to five hundred thousand (500,000) CFA francs or one of these two penalties.

When the above-mentioned criminal actions relate to unauthorized medicines, the penalties may be doubled.

###### Art. 853

Any natural or legal person who has employed on a gratuitous basis or as an employee an unauthorized person for the purpose of carrying out medical information or medicine sales promotion activities is liable to a term of imprisonment of one (01) to six (06) months and a fine of one hundred thousand (100,000) to five hundred thousand (500,000) CFA francs or one of these two penalties.

When the person responsible for the criminal acts referred to in the preceding paragraph is a pharmacist, the penalties provided for in that paragraph are doubled.

###### Art. 854

Any natural or legal person, authorized or not, who has participated, directly or indirectly, in inaccurate medical information is liable to a term of imprisonment of one (01) to six (06) months and a fine of five hundred thousand (500,000) to five million (5,000,000) CFA francs.

When this information is likely to create risks to human health, the penalties provided for are doubled.

###### Art. 855

All material, objects and equipment used for unauthorized information and sales promotion activities for medicines shall be confiscated together with the samples concerned by order of the minister responsible for health.

Final seizure shall be ordered by the judicial authority.

###### Art. 856

Any manufacturer, user of a medical device and third party having knowledge of an incident or risk of incident involving a medical device having caused or likely to cause the death or serious deterioration of the state of health of a patient, user or third party who fails to report it without delay to the ministry responsible for health shall be punished by imprisonment of three (03) to five (05) years and a fine of three million (3,000,000) to ten million (10,000,000) CFA francs or one of these two penalties.

###### Art. 857

Any infringement of the legislative provisions in force relating to the manufacture, import, export and wholesale distribution of veterinary medicines shall be punished by the following penalties:

1) imprisonment of one (01) to six (06) months and a fine of two million (2,000,000) to twenty million (20,000,000) CFA francs, depending on the seriousness of the facts established, or one of these two penalties;

2) temporary suspension from practising the profession of pharmacist or veterinarian in any field whatsoever, pronounced by the competent authority;

3) permanent prohibition pronounced by the judicial authority.

###### Paragraphe 2 : Des restrictions relatives aux réactifs de laboratoire

###### Art. 858

Placing on the market laboratory reagents whose marketing is not authorized constitutes an offence punishable by imprisonment of one (01) to six (06) months and a fine of two million (2,000,000) to twenty million (20,000,000) CFA francs or one of these two penalties.

###### Art. 859

Any establishment manufacturing, importing or distributing reagents intended for medical biology analysis laboratories which:

1) does not make a prior declaration to the competent service of the ministry responsible for health accompanied by a file whose content is fixed by order of the minister responsible for health;

2) does not carry out the manufacture, import and distribution of these reagents in accordance with good practices whose principles are defined by order of the minister responsible for health.

shall be punished by a fine of five million (5,000,000) to fifty million (50,000,000) CFA francs.

###### Paragraphe 3 : Des restrictions aux radiations ionisantes et radioéléments artificiels

###### Art. 860

The sale, purchase, use and possession of natural radioactive elements shall be subject to the conditions determined by decree of the council of ministers.

Any infringement of the provisions of the decree adopted pursuant to this article shall be punished by imprisonment of one (01) to six (06) months and a fine of five hundred thousand (500,000) to five million (5,000,000) CFA francs or one of these two penalties.

###### Art. 861

Ionizing radiation may only be used on the human body for exclusively medical purposes, for diagnosis and therapy.

The conditions for the use of ionizing radiation in this field shall be determined by order of the minister responsible for health.

Anyone who uses ionizing radiation in violation of the regulatory provisions adopted pursuant to this article shall be punished by imprisonment of one (01) to six (06) months and a fine of one million (1,000,000) to five million (5,000,000) CFA francs or one of these two penalties.

In case of recidivism, the penalties shall be doubled and imprisonment shall be mandatory.

###### Art. 862

The following shall be punished by imprisonment of one (01) to six (06) months and a fine of five hundred thousand (500,000) to five million (5,000,000) CFA francs or one of these two penalties, without prejudice to the penalties provided for by customs regulations:

1) any person who prepares, imports or exports artificial radioelements, in any form whatsoever, in the absence of authorization issued for this purpose by the competent authorities, in particular the ministers responsible for health and scientific research after opinion of the technical radiation protection commission;

2) holders of artificial radioelements or products containing them, who use them in contravention of the conditions set for them at the time of their allocation;

3) any person who participates, directly or indirectly, in any advertising relating to the use in

human or veterinary medicine of artificial radioelements or products containing them, with the exception of advertising carried out to doctors, dental surgeons, pharmacists and veterinarians;

4) any person who adds artificial radioelements or products containing them to food, hygiene products and so-called beauty products.

In case of recidivism, the penalties shall be doubled and imprisonment shall be mandatory.

For the application of this article, artificial radioelement means any radioelement obtained by nuclear synthesis or fission.

###### Paragraphe 4 : Des restrictions relatives au tabac et aux boissons alcoolisées

###### Art. 863

Any person who:

1) contravenes, in any manner whatsoever, the regulations on advertising in favour of alcoholic beverages and tobacco established by joint order of the ministers responsible for health, communication and trade.

2) contravenes the obligation to display, on advertising panels and the packaging of alcoholic beverages and tobacco, the mention "seriously harmful to health" and the rate or degree of toxicity:

a) nicotine and tar content for tobacco; b) alcohol content for alcoholic beverages.

shall be punished by imprisonment of one (01) to six (06) months and a fine of one million (1,000,000) to five million (5,000,000) CFA francs or one of these two penalties.

###### Art. 864

The sale or making available of tobacco to a minor shall be punished by imprisonment of one (01) to six (06) months and a fine of one million (1,000,000) to five million (5,000,000) CFA francs or one of these two penalties.

###### Art. 865

The use of tobacco in public places, in workplaces and in public transport shall be punished by imprisonment of one (01) to six (06) months and a fine of one million (1,000,000) to five million (5,000,000) CFA francs or one of these two penalties.

###### Art. 866

In case of recidivism for the offences provided for in the preceding articles, imprisonment shall be mandatory and authorization to sell tobacco or alcohol may be withdrawn by the competent minister on the proposal of the minister responsible for health.

###### Art. 867

Any producer or manufacturer of essences or ethanol that may be used in the manufacture of alcoholic beverages who sells or offers free of charge said essences to any person other than beverage manufacturers having the status of warehouse keeper vis-à-vis the indirect tax administration, pharmacists, perfumers, manufacturers of food or industrial products and direct exporting traders, is punished by a fine of one million (1,000,000) to five million (5,000,000) CFA francs. Any person authorized to purchase said products, who resells them on the domestic market to any person other than a pharmacist is liable to a fine of five hundred thousand (500,000) to two million (2,000,000) CFA francs.

Any pharmacist who dispenses said products without a medical prescription is liable to a term of imprisonment of one (01) to three (03) year(s) and a fine of one million (1,000,000) to five million (5,000,000) CFA francs or one of these two penalties.

In addition, the court orders the permanent closure of the establishment. Offences are recorded and prosecuted as in matters of indirect taxes. In case of repeat offence, the minimum and maximum penalties provided for in this article are doubled.

###### Art. 868

Without prejudice to the prohibitions provided for in the general tax code, any infringement of the joint orders of the ministers responsible for health, trade and industry setting the conditions under which essences that may be used in the manufacture of alcoholic beverages, as well as absinthe essences and similar products or products likely to replace them may, in whatever form, be imported, manufactured, put into circulation, held or sold, is punished by a term of imprisonment of one (01) to three (03) year(s) and a fine of one million (1,000,000) to five million (5,000,000) CFA francs and or one of these two penalties.

Confiscation of the goods is always ordered. In case of repeat offence, the term of imprisonment is compulsorily ordered and the fine is doubled. In addition, the court orders the closure of the establishment.

#### Chapter III: Offences relating to animal health police

###### Art. 869

Are punished by the maximum term of criminal imprisonment, all those who have voluntarily spread, by any means whatsoever, epizootics having resulted in death of a person and/or serious consequences for the national economy.

###### Art. 870

Are punished by a term of five (05) to ten (10) years of criminal imprisonment and a fine of five hundred thousand (500,000) to two million (2,000,000) CFA francs or one of

these two penalties, all those who have spread epizootics transmissible to humans or dangerous for the livestock economy through failure to comply with health legislation and regulations in force or through serious and repeated negligence.

###### Art. 871

Is punished by a term of imprisonment of one (01) to six (06) months and a fine of one hundred thousand (100,000) to five hundred thousand (500,000) CFA francs or one of these two penalties, the perpetrator of a clandestine apanage or of a slaughter carried out under sanitary conditions not in conformity with the regulations.

###### Art. 872

Are punished by a term of imprisonment of six (06) months to two (02) years and a fine of five hundred thousand (500,000) to two million (2,000,000) CFA francs or one of these two penalties:

1) all those who refuse to submit their animals to compulsory vaccinations or attempted to evade them; 2) all those who move or have transported, sell or offer for sale livestock infected or coming from regions declared infected or neglect to isolate an animal affected or suspected of being affected by a disease deemed contagious.

###### Art. 873

Are punished by a term of five (05) to ten (10) years of criminal imprisonment and a fine of five hundred thousand (500,000) to two million (2,000,000) CFA francs or one of these two penalties, all those who have sold or offered for sale meat from animals that they knew had died of diseases of any kind, or slaughtered as being affected by contagious diseases, when the consumption of this meat has not been authorized by the sanitary inspection service for livestock and fisheries.

###### Art. 874

The penalties provided for in articles 870 to 873 above are doubled from the maximum set in case of repeat offence or when the offence is committed by an agent of the livestock and fisheries service or by judicial police officers and agents in any capacity whatsoever.

### TITLE VIII: OFFENCES AGAINST LABOUR AND SOCIAL SECURITY REGULATIONS

#### Chapter I: Offences against labour regulations

###### Art. 875

Is punished by a fine of one hundred thousand (100,000) CFA francs any assessor of the labour court who has not responded to the summons served on him.

In case of repeat offence, the fine is doubled and the court may, in addition, declare him incapable of exercising in the future the functions of assessor at the labour court.

The judgment is posted at the expense of said assessor in the courtroom of the court. The fines are ordered by the labour court.

###### Art. 876

Are punished by a fine of one hundred thousand (100,000) to five hundred thousand (500,000) CFA francs and, in case of repeat offence, by a fine of five hundred (500,000) to two million (2,000,000) CFA francs, employers and all other persons responsible for the failure to post minimum wage rates as well as the conditions of remuneration for task work or piecework in the employer's offices and at the places where staff are paid.

###### Art. 877

Are punishable by a fine of one hundred thousand (100,000) to five hundred thousand (500,000) CFA francs and, in case of recidivism, by a fine of five hundred thousand (500,000) to two million (2,000,000) CFA francs:

1) any person who:

a) except where otherwise provided in the host country, recruits or assigns a Togolese worker for abroad in the absence of an employment contract concluded in writing, previously endorsed by the authorities of the host country and approved by the director general of labour;

b) recruits in Togo or outside Togo a worker of foreign nationality without having previously obtained a hiring authorization and in the absence of an employment contract concluded in writing and endorsed by the director general of labour;

c) does not pay for work performed during the probationary period at the rate of the category in which the worker was engaged, in accordance with the professional classifications of the collective agreement or convention; d) contravenes the provisions of the labour code relating to the establishment of a de facto employment contract following the retention in service of the worker upon expiry of the probationary period or who does not take into account the probationary period, renewal included, for the determination of rights and benefits attached to the duration of the worker's service in the enterprise;

e) contravenes the provisions of the labour code relating to the obligations of the contractor towards workers in case of insolvency of the subcontractor;

f) contravenes the obligation of the subcontractor to indicate his capacity as subcontractor, the name and address of the contractor, by means of a notice posted permanently in each of the workshops, stores or sites used;

g) contravenes the obligation, provided for by article 207 paragraphs 2 and 3 of the labour code, of every employer subject to the provisions of the labour code to maintain the three parts of the employer register, to make it available to the labour and social legislation inspector without displacement, and to preserve it for the five years following the last entry made therein.

2) the perpetrators of infringements of the provisions of the order of the minister responsible for labour, issued after opinion of the technical advisory committee on occupational safety and health, setting the conditions and particular measures of hygiene, safety and health in the workplace, as provided for by article 169 of the labour code;

3) employers or any other persons responsible for the failure to display the prices of goods offered for sale in company stores.

###### Art. 878

Are punishable by a fine of one hundred thousand (100,000) to five hundred thousand (500,000) CFA francs, and in case of recidivism, by a fine of five hundred thousand (500,000) to two million (2,000,000) CFA francs:

1) any person who:

a) contravenes the conditions for suspension of the employment contract and the rules for calculating effective service time as provided for by article 55 of the labour code;

b) contravenes the conditions for termination of the indefinite-term contract as provided for by article 65 of the labour code;

c) contravenes the obligation of the employer, provided for by article 172 of the labour code, to report to the labour and social legislation inspector, within forty-eight (48) working hours, any workplace accident that has occurred or any occupational disease identified in the enterprise;

d) does not make a prior declaration to the labour and social legislation inspector of the opening of an enterprise or establishment of any nature whatsoever, as provided for by article 206 of the labour code;

e) contravenes the obligation of the employer, provided for by article 207 of the labour code, to keep permanently up to date, at the place of the principal establishment, an employer register whose model is fixed by order of the minister responsible for labour, after opinion of the national labour council.

2) the perpetrators of infringements of the provisions of the orders provided for by articles 206 and 207 of the labour code, issued by the minister responsible for labour, after opinion of the national labour council, determining the modalities of the declaration of opening of an enterprise or establishment and the time limit within which existing enterprises must make this declaration, and fixing the model of employer register;

With regard to infringements of the order provided for in article 207 of the labour code, fixing the model of employer register, the fine is applied as many times as there are omitted or erroneous entries without being able to exceed five million (5,000,000) CFA francs.

###### Art. 879

Are punishable by a fine of one hundred thousand (100,000) to five hundred thousand (500,000) CFA francs and, in case of recidivism, by a fine of five hundred thousand (500,000) to two million (2,000,000) CFA francs:

1) the perpetrators of infringements of the provisions of articles 126, 127 and 128 of the labour code, relating to methods of payment of wages;

2) the perpetrators of infringements of the provisions of article 139 of the labour code relating to the conditions for opening company stores;

3) the perpetrators of infringements of the general principles of worker placement, as defined in articles 196, 197, 198, 199 and 200 of the labour code.

###### Art. 880

Are punishable by a fine of five hundred thousand (500,000) to two million (2,000,000) CFA francs, the administrators of trade unions or directors of mutual aid funds who contravene:

1) the freedom to form trade unions and to join the union of one's choice, as defined in article 6 of the labour code;

2) the prohibition of any activity that is not of a nature to promote the study and defence of rights, as well as the material, social and moral interests of a professional nature, both collective and individual, of the employees and professions covered by the statutes of the union;

3) the prohibition for trade unions, defined in article 23 of the labour code, in the absence of authorization to that effect by their statutes or if they distribute profits, in particular in the form of rebates, to their members:

a) to purchase for renting, lending or distributing among their members, everything necessary for the exercise of the profession, in particular raw materials, tools, instruments, machines, fertilizers, seeds, plants, animals and food products for livestock;

b) to lend their free assistance for the sale of products originating exclusively from the personal work or farms of union members;

c) to facilitate this sale through exhibitions, announcements, publications, groupings of orders and shipments, without being able to carry it out in their name or under their responsibility;

4) the decision of the minister responsible for labour to recognize the representative character of a workers' or employers' organization provided for by article 25 of the labour code;

5) the obligations of trade union federations to declare the name and headquarters of the unions that compose them and to determine in their statutes the rules of representation of affiliated unions, as defined by article 31 of the labour code.

In case of false declaration relating to the statutes, name, nationality and capacity of administrators or directors, the fine may be doubled.

###### Art. 881
