1) the pilot in command who fails to keep or have kept one of the on-board documents prescribed by the regulation relating to the operation of aircraft in public air transport; 2) crew members specifically responsible for keeping these documents;

3) the owner or lessee registered in the registration register who fails to keep one of the on-board documents for three (03) years from the last entry;

4) those who contravene the regulations relating to the technical conditions of use of aircraft;

5) those who contravene the provisions relating to overflight of towns or built-up areas.

In case of recidivism, the term of imprisonment is pronounced.

There is recidivism when a judgment has been rendered against the offender, in the preceding year, for one of these contraventions.

###### Art. 998

Are punished by a fine of two hundred thousand (200,000) to one million five hundred thousand (1,500,000) CFA francs, those who contravene the provisions relating to air shows.

They may be punished, in addition, depending on the circumstances, by a term of imprisonment of three (03) to six (06) months.

###### Art. 999

The prohibition from operating or participating in the operation of an aircraft may be pronounced by the judgment or order, for a period of three (3) months to two (02) years, against the crew member convicted pursuant to articles 990, 993, and 994 above.

If the crew member is convicted a second time for one of these same offences within the period provided for in article 996, the prohibition from operating or participating in the operation of an aircraft is pronounced and its duration is increased to the maximum and may be doubled.

The licenses and certificates held by the offenders remain deposited, for the entire duration of the prohibition, at the registry of the court that pronounces the prohibition.

The convicted persons deposit these licenses and certificates, either at this registry or at that of their domicile within five (05) days following the date on which the conviction became final, failing which, they are punished by a fine of one million (1,000,000) to five million (5,000,000) CFA francs, without prejudice to the penalties provided for in article 990 above in the event that they operate or participate in the operation of an aircraft during the period of prohibition and which cannot be combined.

###### Art. 1000

Any unauthorized throwing of objects from an aircraft in flight is punished by a penalty of three (03) to six (06) months' imprisonment and a fine of two hundred thousand (200,000) to one million five hundred thousand (1,500,000) CFA francs or one of these two penalties, even if this throwing has caused no damage, and without prejudice to more severe penalties that could be incurred in case of other offences.

###### Art. 1001

Any pilot in command of an aircraft who, knowing that it has just caused or occasioned an accident on the surface, does not notify without delay the authorities of the nearest airport with which he can enter into communication and thus attempts to escape the criminal and civil liability he may have incurred, is punished by the penalties provided for to sanction the offence of hit and run.

###### Art. 1002

All provisions of laws relating to the repression of offences against customs regulations are applicable to goods imported or exported by aircraft under any customs regime.

All unauthorized unloading and throwing of goods, other than those essential to the safety of the aircraft, are sanctioned by the penalties prescribed by customs laws on smuggling.

In case of offence and as guarantee or security for payment of the fine incurred, the aircraft may only be subject to conservatory seizure, the release of which is given if a surety is provided or a deposit is made up to the amount of said fine.

###### Art. 1003

The public prosecutor, the investigating judge, the police officers and agents designated by the code of criminal procedure, the civil aviation technical corps officials, the military or naval personnel and the civil or military authority agents commissioned for this purpose, the gendarmes and the customs agents have the right to seize explosives, weapons and war munitions, carrier pigeons, prohibited photographic equipment, negatives and postal correspondence as well as radiotelegraphic and radiotelephone equipment that would be found on board without the special authorization provided for by the regulations. They may seize carrier pigeons, photographic equipment and negatives that are found on board aircraft authorized to carry these objects in the event that such aircraft have passed over prohibited zones.

The confiscation of lawfully seized objects and equipment is ordered by the court.

###### Art. 1004

The aircraft whose certificate of airworthiness and certificate of registration cannot be produced or whose registration marks do not match those of the certificate of registration may be detained at the expense of the owner or, in case of lease of the aircraft registered in the registration register, at the expense of the registered lessee, by the authorities responsible for ensuring the enforcement of the civil aviation code until the identity of the owner has been established.

###### Art. 1005

The penalties provided for in articles 999 to 1004 are applicable without prejudice to the application of the provisions of the civil code and the code of civil procedure.

###### Art. 1006

Is punished by a term of imprisonment of one (01) to five (05) year(s) anyone who, using a device, a substance or a weapon commits against a person in an airport serving international civil aviation, an act of violence which causes or is likely to cause serious injury.

###### Art. 1007

Is punished by ten (10) to twenty (20) years of criminal imprisonment and a fine of ten million (10,000,000) to thirty million (30,000,000) CFA francs anyone who:

1) destroys or damages buildings or installations intended to ensure the control of aircraft traffic, aeronautical telecommunications, air navigation aids or meteorological assistance;

2) disrupts, by any means whatsoever, the operation of these installations;

3) destroys or damages an aircraft within the perimeter of an aerodrome;

4) obstructs, in any manner whatsoever, the navigation or traffic of aircraft;

5) interrupts, using a material device, a substance or a weapon, the operation or service of an aerodrome if this act affects or is likely to affect safety within the perimeter of that aerodrome;

6) communicates information which he knows to be false and, as a result, compromises the safety of an aircraft in flight.

###### Art. 1008

If the acts referred to in articles 1006 and 1007 above have resulted in the death of one or more persons, the maximum term of criminal imprisonment may be required.

###### Art. 1009

Anyone who stays or enters the areas prohibited by the general regulations and instructions of aerodromes assigned to a public service or allows livestock or draft, burden or riding animals to remain there is punished by a term of imprisonment of fifteen (15) days to one (01) month and a fine of one hundred thousand (100,000) francs to five hundred thousand (500,000) CFA francs or one of these two penalties and may be, in addition, deprived of any right to compensation in case of accident.

###### Art. 1010

Any person found in the restricted zone of an airport without access authorization issued by the competent authority in material state, is punished by a term of imprisonment of ten (10) days to two (02) months and a fine of one hundred thousand (100,000) to five hundred thousand (500,000) CFA francs or one of these two penalties and may be, in addition, deprived of the right to ingenuity in case of accident.

###### Art. 1011

Violations of regulatory provisions concerning aeronautical servitudes of clearance and marking established in the interest of air traffic are punished by a fine of three hundred thousand (300,000) to two million (2,000,000) CFA francs.

In case of recidivism, the violations are punished by a term of imprisonment of one (01) month and a fine of six hundred thousand (600,000) to five million (5,000,000) CFA francs or one of these two penalties.

###### Art. 1012

Upon requisition of the public prosecutor acting at the request of the interested minister, the court seized of the prosecution grants to persons who violate the regulatory provisions concerning aeronautical servitudes, under penalty of a periodic penalty of five thousand (5,000) to twenty-five thousand (25,000) CFA francs per day of delay, a time limit to modify or remove the structures subject to servitudes or to provide for their marking.

In the event that this time limit is not observed, the periodic penalty pronounced runs from the expiration of said time limit until the day when the situation is effectively regularized.

The court may authorize the reversal of part of the periodic penalties when the situation is regularized and the debtor establishes that he was prevented from observing, by a circumstance independent of his will, the time limit that had been granted to him. In addition, if upon the expiration of the time limit set by the judgment, the situation has not been regularized, the administration may have the work carried out ex officio at the expense and risk of the responsible persons.

###### Art. 1013

The periodic penalties and fines are collected by the public treasury accountants, upon requisition of the interested minister.

###### Art. 1014

Any air transport company that carries out its activity in Togo without authorization or in violation of the requirements imposed by the authorizations that may have been issued to it is liable to a fine payable immediately, proportional to the tonnage of the aircraft and established on the basis of fifty thousand (50,000) CFA francs per ton and per transport.

The tonnage taken into account is the maximum take-off weight corresponding to the certificate of airworthiness of the aircraft. Any fraction of tonnage is counted as one ton.

In case of refusal to pay the fine or in case of recidivism, the aircraft may be placed under sequestration at the request of the competent civil aviation authorities.

###### Art. 1015

Is punished by a term of imprisonment of one (01) to fifteen (04) months and a fine of two hundred thousand (200,000) to one million two hundred thousand (1,200,000) CFA francs or one of the two penalties, any person who exercises one of the positions corresponding to the licenses and qualifications of civil aeronautics flight personnel in violation of the provisions of this code.

Is punished by the same penalty the manager of any company who entrusts one of these positions to a person who does not meet the conditions required by the civil aviation code.

The penalties provided for by this article are enacted without prejudice to the application of those of the criminal or tax laws in force. They are furthermore applied without prejudice to administrative or disciplinary sanctions and damages, if applicable.

###### Subsection 7: Offences relating to the merchant navy

###### Paragraph 1: Crimes and offences affecting the internal policing of the vessel

###### Art. 1016

Is punished by a term of imprisonment of ten (10) days to six (06) months and a fine of thirty-six thousand (36,000) to three hundred and sixty thousand (360,000) CFA francs or one of these two penalties, any officer, master or crew member who is guilty of irregular absence from the vessel when assigned to a watch or security post or when his absence has resulted in his missing the vessel's departure.

###### Art. 1017

Any captain who, except in cases of force majeure, breaks his engagement and abandons his vessel before being replaced, is punished if the vessel was in safety in a port by a term of imprisonment of ten (10) days to two (02) years, and if the vessel was in an open roadstead or at sea, by a term of imprisonment of one (01) to two (02) year(s).

###### Art. 1018

Is punished by a fine of fifty thousand (50,000) to three hundred thousand (300,000) CFA francs any captain who does not personally remain in his vessel at the entry and exit of ports, harbours or rivers.

###### Art. 1019

Any captain, officer or master who abuses his authority or who orders or tolerates an abuse of authority towards a person on board, is punished by a term of imprisonment of ten (10) days to six (06) months and a fine of fifty thousand (50,000) to two hundred thousand (200,000) CFA francs, or one of these two penalties.

Is punished by the same penalty, any captain, officer or master guilty of outrage characterized by word, gesture or threat towards crew members.

Any captain, officer or master who, outside the legitimate grounds provided by law, has used or caused to be used violence, in the exercise or on the occasion of the exercise of his functions is punished in accordance with the provisions of this code.

In the cases provided for in the two preceding paragraphs, the penalty may be doubled if it concerns a novice or a cabin boy.

###### Art. 1020

Is punished for each of the offences referred to below by a fine of fifty thousand (50,000) to two hundred thousand (200,000) CFA francs any captain who refuses or neglects without legitimate grounds:

1) to make the findings required in the event of a crime or offence committed on board;

2) to draw up either, civil status documents, reports of disappearance and wills in the cases provided for by the code of persons and the family;

3) to keep regularly the logbook, the discipline book and other regulatory documents.

###### Art. 1021

Is punished by the penalty provided by this code for forgery, any captain, officer, master or crew member who fraudulently records in the vessel's documents altered facts or facts contrary to the truth.

###### Art. 1022

Is punished by a term of imprisonment of ten (10) days to six (06) months any captain who favours, by his consent, the usurpation of the exercise of command on his vessel.

The same term of imprisonment to which may be added a fine of fifty thousand (50,000) to three hundred thousand (300,000) CFA francs is pronounced against any person who has unduly taken command of a vessel and against the shipowner who would be his accomplice.

###### Art. 1023

Any person on board, other than the captain, who commits or attempts to commit, with criminal intent and without the knowledge of the shipowner, an act of fraud or smuggling likely to result in a criminal conviction for the shipowner, is punished by a term of imprisonment of ten (10) days to six (06) months.

If the offender is a captain, the penalty may be doubled.

###### Art. 1024

Is punished by a penalty of five (05) to ten (10) years of criminal imprisonment any captain who, with fraudulent intent, diverts for his own benefit the vessel whose conduct is entrusted to him, or who, voluntarily and with criminal intent, takes a false route or destroys without necessity all or part of the cargo, provisions or effects on board.

###### Art. 1025

Is punished by the penalty provided for in the preceding article, any captain who, with fraudulent intent is guilty of one of the acts referred to by the commercial code, or who sells, outside the case of legally established unseaworthiness, the vessel of which he has command or who carries out unloadings in contravention of legislative and regulatory provisions in force.

###### Art. 1026

Any person on board who intentionally suppresses or abusively retains a letter entrusted to him to be delivered to a person on board the same vessel, instead of having it reach the addressee or who, in the same conditions, opens a letter entrusted to his care is punished by a term of imprisonment of ten (10) days to three (03) months and a fine of fifty thousand (50,000) to two hundred thousand (200,000) CFA francs.

###### Art. 1027

Any captain, officer or crew member who adulterates goods forming part of the cargo is punished by the penalties provided by this code.

Any person on board who voluntarily adulterates provisions, beverages or other objects of consumption by the mixture of non-harmful substances is punished by a term of imprisonment of ten (10) days to six (06) months.

If harmful substances have been used, the penalty is two (02) to five (05) years of imprisonment. If it has resulted for one or more persons a serious illness, the penalty is that of five (05) to ten (10) years of criminal imprisonment. If death has resulted without intention to cause it, the penalty is the maximum of criminal imprisonment for a fixed term.

###### Art. 1028

Any person on board who voluntarily diverts, damages or sells an object useful for navigation, manoeuvring or the safety of the vessel, or who sells provisions embarked for the service of the vessel, is punished by a term of imprisonment of one (01) month to two (02) years.

###### Art. 1029

Thefts committed on board are punished in accordance with the provisions of this code relating to theft.

The preceding provisions do not prevent the application of disciplinary sanctions provided for by legislative and regulatory provisions in force.

###### Art. 1030

Any seaman who, after having received advances on wages or shares before the maritime authority, abstains without legitimate grounds from taking up his service on board and does not put himself in a position to reimburse the advances granted to him, is punished by the penalties provided by this code relating to breach of trust.

###### Art. 1031

Is punished by a term of imprisonment of ten (10) days to one (01) month any person on board, guilty of having introduced on board alcohol or spirituous beverages or of having facilitated the introduction on board without the express authorization of the captain.

Is punished by a double penalty, the captain or shipowner who has embarked or caused to be embarked alcohol or spirituous beverages intended for the consumption of the crew in quantities exceeding the regulatory quantities or authorizes the embarkation thereof.

###### Art. 1032

Is punished by a term of imprisonment of ten (10) days to six (06) months, any captain who was found in a state of intoxication on board his vessel and any officer, master or crew member who habitually gets drunk or who was found in a state of intoxication during the watch.

Is punished by the same penalty any pilot who, in a state of intoxication has undertaken to steer a vessel.

Double the penalty is pronounced against any captain who habitually gets drunk without prejudice to disciplinary measures provided for by legislative and regulatory provisions in force.

###### Art. 1033

Is punished by a term of imprisonment of ten (10) days to six (06) months and a fine of fifty thousand (50,000) to two hundred thousand (200,000) CFA francs or one of these two penalties, any officer, master or crew member who is guilty of insult by word, gesture, or threat towards a superior.

Is punished by the penalties provided for in this code any person on board who is guilty of assault against the captain.

###### Art. 1034

Is punished by a term of imprisonment of ten (10) days to six (06) months any crew member who, after a formal summons from the captain or an officer specially designated for this purpose by the captain, refuses to obey or resists an order concerning the service.

If the offender is an officer or master, the penalty provided for in the preceding paragraph is doubled.

###### Art. 1035

Persons on board who, collectively and whether armed or not, engage in violence on board or rise up against the authority of the captain and refuse after a formal summons to return to order, are punished: officers or masters, by a penalty of one (01) to three (03) year(s) of imprisonment and other persons on board by a penalty of six (06) months to two (02) years of imprisonment.

However, persons who do not hold a salaried position on board are punished as officers or masters, if they were the instigators of the resistance.

In the cases provided for above, the resistance of the captain and the persons who have remained loyal to him is considered an act of legitimate defense.

###### Art. 1036

Any person involved in a conspiracy or in an attack against the safety, freedom or authority of the captain is punished: officers or masters, by a penalty of three (03) to five (05) years of imprisonment and other persons on board by a penalty of one (01) to three (03) year(s) of imprisonment.

There is a conspiracy as soon as the resolution to act is agreed upon between two or more persons on board a vessel.

###### Art. 1037

The third offense and subsequent offenses against discipline committed during the same embarkation are considered as offenses and punished by a term of imprisonment of ten (10) days to six (06) months.

However, when the nature of the offense and the circumstances that accompanied it do not appear sufficient to the maritime authority to enable it to refer the matter to the public prosecutor, the maritime authority may retain the character of the infraction as an offense and impose a disciplinary fine.

###### Paragraphe 2 : Des délits concernant la police de la navigation

###### Art. 1038

Any person, even foreign, on board a Togolese or foreign vessel, who, in maritime waters and up to the limit of Togolese territorial waters does not comply with the regulations or orders emanating from the maritime authority and relating either to the policing of waters and roadsteads, or to the policing of maritime navigation, or to the safety of navigation, is punished by a term of imprisonment of ten (10) days to six (06) months and a fine of ten thousand (10,000) to fifty thousand (50,000) CFA francs or one of these two penalties.

The same penalty is incurred by any person on board a Togolese vessel, who, outside Togolese territorial waters, does not comply with orders regularly given by a consul general, consul or vice-consul of Togo or by the commander of a Togolese warship.

When the person having committed one of the offenses provided for in the two preceding paragraphs is on board a Togolese or foreign vessel which is or comes to be in a port, roadstead or anchorage of Togo, the vessel may be provisionally detained until consignment of the presumed amount of the fine incurred by the offender or provision of a solvent surety.

A decree shall establish the conditions for application of this provision. If the offenses under this article were committed in time of war, the penalty may be tripled.

###### Art. 1039

Any captain requisitioned by the competent authority who, without legitimate reason refuses to take charge of the investigation file or evidence or to ensure the transport of an accused person or who does not deliver the accused person or the file entrusted to his care to the maritime authority designated to receive them is punished by a fine of twenty-five thousand (25,000) to two hundred fifty thousand (250,000) CFA francs

without prejudice if applicable in case of escape or complicity in escape, to the application to persons on board and to the accused person of the provisions of this code relating to escapes.

###### Art. 1040

Is punished by the penalty provided for in the preceding article, any captain who, without legitimate reason, refuses to comply with the requisition of the maritime authority to repatriate Togolese nationals to Togo.

###### Art. 1041

Any captain who, at sea, does not obey the call of a Togolese warship or of a State to which equivalent rights have been recognized and compels it to use force is punished by a term of imprisonment of six (06) months to two (02) years.

###### Art. 1042

Any captain who has left ashore in a port where there is no Togolese authority, an officer, a master or a crew member who is sick or injured, does not provide him with the means to ensure his treatment and repatriation, is punished by a term of imprisonment of ten (10) days to two (02) months and a fine of twenty-five thousand (25,000) to two hundred fifty thousand (250,000) CFA francs or one of the two penalties.

The same penalty is incurred by the captain who, having left ashore, before he has reached his place of destination a sick or injured passenger does not give notice of this measure to the consular authority of the country to which the disembarked passenger belongs, or failing that, to the local authority.

###### Art. 1043

Is punished by a fine of twenty-five thousand (25,000) to two hundred fifty thousand (250,000) CFA francs for each offense found, any shipowner or vessel owner who does not comply with the legislative and regulatory requirements relating to the regulations on work, food and sleeping arrangements on board vessels. Is punished by the same penalty without prejudice to the disciplinary measures provided for by law, any captain who personally commits or in agreement with the shipowner or the vessel owner, the offenses provided for in the preceding paragraph.

However, the penalty imposed on the captain may be reduced to one quarter of that imposed on the shipowner or the owner if it is proven that the captain received a written or verbal order from that shipowner or that owner.

The penalties provided for in the two preceding paragraphs may be doubled in case of recidivism. There is recidivism when the offender has suffered, in the twelve months preceding a conviction for acts punished by the preceding article.

###### Art. 1044

Any person who, on a Togolese vessel exercises without the authorization of the maritime authority and outside cases of force majeure, either the command of the vessel, or any other function on board, without satisfying the conditions required by maritime laws and regulations is punished by a term of imprisonment of ten (10) days to one (01) year and a fine of ten thousand (10,000) to fifty thousand (50,000) CFA francs or one of these two penalties.

Is punished by the same penalty, any person who, without a regular commission from the pilot of the station, undertakes or attempts to undertake the conduct of a vessel in the capacity of commissioned pilot.

###### Art. 1045

Any person who engages in maritime navigation without being provided, in accordance with laws and regulations, with a crew list, or who does not exhibit their crew list at the first request of the authorities is punished by a fine of fifty thousand (50,000) to three hundred thousand (300,000) CFA francs if the vessel has a gross tonnage exceeding twenty-five (25) tons, fifty thousand (50,000) to two hundred thousand (200,000) CFA francs otherwise.

###### Art. 1046

Any captain who embarks or disembarks a member of the crew without having this embarkation or disembarkation mentioned on the crew list by the maritime authority is punished, for each person irregularly embarked or disembarked, by a fine of fifty thousand (50,000) to two hundred thousand (200,000) CFA francs.

The same penalties are incurred for each passenger admitted on board without having been subsequently registered on the crew list. However, special provisions may be established by order for certain navigations; violations of these provisions are punished by a fine of fifty thousand (50,000) to three hundred thousand (300,000) CFA francs

###### Art. 1047

Except in the case provided for in the following paragraph, any shipowner or ship owner who violates the prescriptions of the merchant marine code or those of the regulations adopted for their application relating to ship-owning companies is punished by a fine of fifty thousand (50,000) to one million (1,000,000) CFA francs.

Any shipowner or owner who operates a ship for which the safety certificate has expired, been refused or suspended is punished by a term of imprisonment of one (01) month to one (01) year and a fine of one hundred thousand (100,000) to two million (2,000,000) francs or one of these two penalties. However, if the validity of the safety certificates expires during a voyage, the validity of these certificates is deemed extended until the next port where the ship calls.

The captain who has committed one of the offences provided for and punished in this article is liable to the same penalties.

The maximum penalty is however reduced by one quarter if it is proven that the captain received a written or verbal order from the shipowner or the owner.

Any member of the crew who has provoked an inspection on board by knowingly producing inaccurate allegations is punished by a term of imprisonment of three (03) to six (06) days and a fine of five thousand (5,000) to twenty-five thousand (25,000) CFA francs or one of these two penalties.

###### Art. 1048

The fines and imprisonment penalties provided for in the preceding article may be doubled in case of recidivism. There is recidivism when in the twelve (12) months preceding the criminal acts, the offender has already been convicted for acts punished by the preceding article.

These same penalties are reduced by half with regard to violations of the prescriptions concerning ships with a gross tonnage of less than five hundred (500) tons.

###### Art. 1049

Any person who enters into or attempts to enter into a maritime engagement by knowingly producing false identity documents or a professional booklet obtained fraudulently is punished by a term of imprisonment of ten (10) days to six (06) months. The penalty is doubled in case of recidivism.

###### Art. 1050

Any person other than officials and agents of public services who enters on board a ship without a ticket or without authorization from the captain or the shipowner or without being called there by operational needs is punished by a fine of fifty thousand (50,000) to two hundred thousand (200,000) CFA francs.

In case of recidivism within the year, the fine is doubled and the court may also impose a term of imprisonment of three (03) days to one (01) month.

Any person who fraudulently enters a ship with the intention of making a voyage is punished by a term of imprisonment of ten (10) days to six (06) months and a fine of fifty thousand (50,000) to two hundred thousand (200,000) CFA francs or one of these two penalties.

In case of recidivism, the fine is one hundred thousand (100,000) to five hundred thousand (500,000) CFA francs and the term of imprisonment is six (06) months to two (02) years.

Any person who, either on board or on land, has facilitated the embarkation or disembarkation of a stowaway, concealed them or provided them with food without the captain's knowledge is punished by a fine of thirty-six thousand (36,000) to three hundred sixty thousand (360,000) CFA francs and a term of imprisonment of eleven (11) days to six (06) months

The maximum of these two penalties must be imposed on persons who have grouped together to facilitate clandestine embarkations.

In case of recidivism, the term of imprisonment is six (06) months to two (02) years and the fine is two hundred fifty thousand (250,000) to one million (1,000,000) CFA francs.

The penalty is double the maximum for persons who have grouped together to facilitate clandestine embarkations.

The costs of expulsion from the territory of stowaways of foreign nationality are charged to the ship on board which the offence was committed.

###### Art. 1051

Any person on board who, without the captain's knowledge, introduces onto a ship, with a view to having them transported, goods not listed on the manifest, is punished by a term of imprisonment of ten (10) days to six (06) months and a fine of fifty thousand (50,000) to two hundred thousand (200,000) CFA francs or one of these two penalties without prejudice to the captain's right to throw overboard the goods improperly loaded on the vessel.

###### Art. 1052

Any captain who, except in case of legitimate impediment, does not deposit their crew list and discipline book at the office of the maritime authority or at the consulate chancellery, either within twenty-four hours of their arrival in a Togolese port or in a foreign port where a consul general, consul or vice-consul of Togo or consular authorities to whom equivalent rights have been recognized resides when the vessel is to stay more than twenty-four (24) hours in the port (public holidays excluded) or upon arrival, if the vessel is to stay less than twenty-four hours in the port, is punished by a fine of twenty-five thousand (25,000) to one hundred twenty-five thousand (125,000) CFA francs.

###### Art. 1053

Any captain who, unless for legitimate reasons of impediment, refrains upon arrival in a foreign roadstead from going on board the Togolese warship is punished by a fine of fifty thousand (50,000) to two hundred thousand (200,000) CFA francs.

###### Art. 1054

Any captain who does not comply with the provisions established by the decree on the external identification marks of ships, or who erases, alters, covers or masks said marks, is punished by a fine of fifty thousand (50,000) to three hundred thousand (300,000) CFA francs.

###### Art. 1055

In case of failure to declare the discovery of a maritime wreck, the offender is punished by a fine of one thousand (1,000) to ten thousand (10,000) CFA francs.

###### Art. 1056

Any person who has misappropriated or attempted to misappropriate or concealed a maritime wreck is punished by the penalties provided for in Title III of the merchant marine code.

###### Paragraphe 3

Loss of ships, collisions, groundings and other navigation accidents

###### Art. 1057

Any person who voluntarily grounds, loses or destroys with criminal intent, any ship by any means whatsoever, is punished by a term of five (05) to ten (10) years of criminal imprisonment.

The maximum penalty is applied to the offender who is charged in any capacity whatsoever with the conduct of the ship or who directs it as a pilot.

###### Art. 1058

Any captain or officer of the watch who commits an infraction of the rules prescribed by maritime regulations, whether concerning lights to be lit at night and signals to be made in foggy weather, or concerning the route to be followed, or maneuvers to be executed in case of encountering a vessel, is punished by a penalty of ten (10) days to three (03) months' imprisonment and a fine of fifty thousand (50,000) to three hundred thousand (300,000) CFA francs or one of these two penalties.

Any pilot who commits an infraction of the rules concerning the route to be followed is punished by the same penalty.

###### Art. 1059

Any captain or officer of the watch who commits an infraction of the rules prescribed by maritime regulations, whether concerning lights to be lit at night and signals to be made in foggy weather, or concerning the route to be followed, or maneuvers to be executed in case of encountering a vessel, is punished by a penalty of ten (10) days to three (03) months' imprisonment and a fine of fifty thousand (50,000) to three hundred thousand (300,000) CFA francs or one of these two penalties.

Any pilot who commits an infraction of the rules concerning the route to be followed is punished by the same penalty.

###### Art. 1060

Any member of the crew other than the captain, the officer of the watch or the pilot who, during service, commits an act of inexcusable negligence, a lack of vigilance or any other breach of service obligations having caused, for any vessel, either a collision, or a grounding or impact against a visible or known obstacle, or serious damage to a vessel or its cargo, is punished by a penalty of imprisonment of ten (10) days to two (02) months and a fine of fifty thousand (50,000) to two hundred thousand (200,000) CFA francs or one of these two penalties.

If the infraction has resulted in the loss or absolute unseaworthiness of a vessel or the loss of a cargo or if it has caused either serious injuries or death to one or more persons, the offender is punished by a penalty of ten (10) days to six (06) months' imprisonment and a fine of fifty thousand (50,000) to three hundred thousand (300,000) francs or one of these two penalties.

###### Art. 1061

Any captain who, after a collision and insofar as he can do so without danger to his vessel, his crew and his passengers, neglects to employ all means at his disposal to save from the danger created by the collision the other vessel, its crew and its passengers, is punished by a penalty of imprisonment of one (01) month to two (02) years and a fine of fifty thousand (50,000) to one million (1,000,000) CFA francs or one of these two penalties.

Any captain who, except in cases of force majeure, moves away from the scene of the incident before having ascertained that further assistance is useless to the other vessel, its crew and its passengers and if the vessel has sunk, before having made every effort to rescue the shipwrecked persons, is punished by the same penalty. If one or more persons have perished as a result of the non-performance of the obligations referred to in this paragraph, the penalty may be doubled.

After a collision, the captain of each of the collided vessels who, if he can do so without danger to his vessel, his crew or his passengers, does not inform the captain of the other vessel of the names of his own vessel and the ports of registry, departure and destination thereof, is punished by a penalty of imprisonment of ten (10) days to three (03) months and a fine of fifty thousand (50,000) to two hundred thousand (200,000) CFA francs or one of these two penalties.

###### Art. 1062

Any captain who, in case of danger, abandons his vessel during the voyage without the advice of the officers and principal members of the crew is punished by a penalty of imprisonment of ten (10) days to six (06) months.

- Any captain who, in case of danger and before abandoning his vessel, neglects to organize the rescue of the crew and passengers and to save the ship's papers, postal dispatches and the most valuable goods of the cargo is punished by a penalty of imprisonment of one (01) to two (02) year(s).

Any captain who, forced to abandon his vessel, does not remain on board last is punished by the penalty provided for in the preceding paragraph.

###### Article 1063

Any captain who, when he can do so without serious danger to his vessel, his crew or the passengers, does not render assistance to any person, even an enemy, in danger of perishing, is punished by a penalty of imprisonment of one (01) month to two (02) year(s) and a fine of fifty thousand (50,000) to one million (1,000,000) CFA francs or one of these two penalties.

Any pilot who does not render assistance to a vessel in danger is punished by the same penalty.

###### Art. 1064

The provisions of articles 1058 and 1061 are applicable to persons, even foreign, who are on a foreign vessel when the infraction took place in maritime waters and up to the limit of Togolese territorial waters.

In the case where one of the infractions provided for by articles 1058, 1059, 1061 and 1062 has been committed by a person exercising command under the irregular conditions determined by article 1070, the penalty is doubled.

###### Art. 1065

Any person who, being on land or on board, incites by words or in writing a crew member of a vessel to commit one of the offenses provided for by this subsection is punished by the same penalty of one (01) to five (05) year(s)' imprisonment and a fine of fifty thousand (50,000) to two hundred thousand (200,000) CFA francs or one of these two penalties.

###### Paragraphe 4: Offences concerning maritime fishing police

###### Art. 1066: How an infraction of maritime fishing regulations

1) any industrial fishing establishment, excluding traditional fisheries, any park, any deposit of shellfish formed on the seashore, along the coasts, on lagoons and on the banks of lagoons, subject to authorization which contravene the decree in council of ministers setting the conditions for this authorization. Offenders are liable to a fine of twelve thousand (12,000) to one hundred twenty thousand (120,000) CFA francs;

2) any captain of a foreign vessel who fishes himself or whose crew fishes in Togolese territorial waters within the limit set at twelve nautical miles offshore from the low water mark. Offenders are liable to:

a) a fine of one hundred twenty thousand (120,000) to one million two hundred thousand (1,200,000) CFA francs;

b) seizure and detention of the vessel until payment of all custody and maintenance costs, legal costs and fines. After a period of three (03) months from the day the judgment becomes final, the vessel may be sold by court authorization.

c) in case of recidivism within a period of two (02) years, an imprisonment sentence of fifteen (15) days to three (03) months, double the fine penalty and mandatory confiscation of fishing gear and fishing products;

3) any person who contravenes the prohibition on:

a) using for maritime fishing or river fishing explosives or explosive materials of any nature whatsoever, drugs capable of destroying, intoxicating or modifying the usual behavior of fish, crustaceans, shellfish or any aquatic animals;

b) selling, transporting and peddling the product of fishing prohibited in the preceding point.

Offenders are liable to an imprisonment sentence of one (01) to five (05) year(s), a fine of fifty thousand (50,000) to five hundred thousand (500,000) CFA francs or one of these two penalties, without prejudice to the mandatory confiscation of explosives or drugs and fishing products and the optional confiscation of vessels or boats used for the offence and vehicles used for transporting explosives or drugs or the product of prohibited fishing;

4) any person who contravenes the provisions of decrees taken for the application of the law of July 11, 1998 on fishing regulations. Offenders are liable to an imprisonment sentence of fifteen (15) days to six (06) months and a fine of twelve thousand (12,000) to one hundred twenty thousand (120,000) CFA francs or one of these two penalties, without prejudice to the confiscation of fishing gear and fish if applicable.

The penalties provided for in this article may be doubled when the offences were committed at night.

###### Paragraphe 5: Infractions to the general organization of transport

###### Art. 1067

Is punished by a fine of one hundred thousand (100,000) to five million (5,000,000) CFA francs any shipowner or vessel owner who violates the regulatory provisions relating to the administrative organization of the merchant navy and the public pilotage service provided to port captains for the navigation of vessels at the entrance and exit of ports, in ports and within the limits of the pilotage zone, as referred to in the merchant navy code.

###### Sous-section 8: Maritime piracy

###### Art. 1068: Are prosecuted and tried as pirates

1) any individual who seizes any vessel, any fixed platform, or who exercises control over it by force or under threat of force;

2) any individual who commits acts of violence, detains or sequesters persons present on that vessel or on that platform;

3) any individual who commits depreciation on a vessel or platform, its equipment and installations, and goods present on board;

4) any individual who voluntarily participates in the use of a vessel while knowing that it is a pirate vessel;

5) any individual forming part of the crew of an armed vessel and sailing without being or having been provided for the voyage with a passport, crew list, commissions or other acts establishing the legitimacy of the expedition;

6) any captain of an armed vessel and holder of a commission issued by two or more different powers or States; 7) any individual forming part of the crew of a Togolese vessel which commits armed acts of depreciation or violence, either against Togolese vessels or vessels of a power with which Togo is not in a state of war, or against the crews or cargoes of these vessels;

8) any individual forming part of a foreign vessel which, outside a state of war and without being provided with letters of marque and regular commissions, commits said acts against Togolese vessels, their crews or cargoes;

9) the captain and officers of any vessel whatsoever who commits acts of hostility under a flag other than that of the State from which it has a commission;

10) any Togolese who, having obtained, even with government authorization, a commission from a foreign power to command an armed vessel, commits acts of hostility against Togolese vessels or States to which equivalent rights have been recognized, their crews or their cargoes;

11) any individual forming part of the crew of a Togolese vessel who, by fraud or violence against the captain, seizes said vessel;

12) any individual forming part of the crew of a Togolese vessel who delivers it to pirates or to the enemy.

Any person or group of persons who seizes a vessel with a view to committing an offence against property or persons, is punished by a sentence of twenty (20) years of criminal imprisonment, increased to thirty (30) years in case of hostage-taking, in association with a ransom demand or in case of homicide or injuries having accompanied or followed the seizure of control of the vessel.

National courts have jurisdiction to hear and punish acts of piracy committed beyond the outer limit of the territorial sea, regardless of the nationality of their perpetrators and regardless of the flag of the vessels or platforms involved.

###### Art. 1069

In the case provided for by point 5 of article 1068, pirates are punished by a sentence of one (01) to three (03) year(s) of imprisonment.

Any individual guilty of the crime specified in point 6 of the same article is punished by a sentence of three (03) to five (05) years of imprisonment.

###### Art. 1070

In the cases provided for by points 7 and 8 of article 1068, if depredations and violence were committed without homicides or injuries, the guilty parties are punished by a sentence of five (05) to ten (10) years of criminal imprisonment.

If these depredations and violence were preceded, accompanied or followed by homicides or injuries, a sentence of ten (10) to twenty (20) years of criminal imprisonment is indiscriminately pronounced against the officers and other crew members.

The crime specified in point 9 of the same article is punished by five (05) to ten (10) years of criminal imprisonment.

###### Art. 1071

Whoever has been found guilty of the crime provided for by point 10 of article 1068 is punished by thirty (30) years of criminal imprisonment.

###### Art. 1072

In the case provided for by point 11 of article 1068, the sentence is that of ten (10) to twenty (20) years of criminal imprisonment against the crew members.

If the act was preceded, accompanied or followed by homicides or injuries, the sentence is increased to thirty (30) years of criminal imprisonment.

The crime provided for by point 12 of the same article is also punished by thirty (30) years of criminal imprisonment.

###### Art. 1073

The sale of ships captured for piracy is ordered by the court and the proceeds of the sale are intended to repair damages and pay costs.

#### Chapter IV: Banking and Financial Offences

##### Section 1: Offences against Banking Regulations

###### Art. 1074

No one may direct, administer or manage a bank or financial institution, or one of their agencies, unless he or she has Togolese nationality or that of a WAEMU member country, unless he or she enjoys, by virtue of an establishment agreement, assimilation to Togolese nationals.

The Minister of Finance may grant, upon the favourable opinion of the Banking Commission, individual derogations to the provisions of this article.

###### Art. 1075

Any conviction for a common law crime, for forgery or use of forgery in public documents, for forgery or use of forgery in private, commercial or banking documents, for theft, for fraud or offences punished by the penalties for fraud, for breach of trust, for bankruptcy, for misappropriation of public funds, for embezzlement by a public depositary, for extortion of funds or securities, for issuing cheques without provision, for offences against exchange control legislation, for undermining State credit or for receiving things obtained by means of these offences, or any conviction for an offence assimilated by law to one of those listed above, entails by operation of law a prohibition on:

1) directing, administering or managing a bank or financial institution or one of their agencies;

2) carrying out on one's own account, in the context of one's habitual profession, credit, credit sale or exchange operations, or habitually receiving funds used for one's own account in investment operations, or habitually acting as an intermediary as a commission agent, broker or otherwise in all or part of these operations;

3) proposing to the public the creation of a bank or financial institution.

Any conviction for attempt or complicity in the commission of the above offences entails the same prohibition.

The same prohibition applies to unrehabilitated bankrupts, to dismissed ministerial officers and to managers suspended or removed pursuant to disciplinary sanctions for offences against banking regulations pronounced by the Banking Commission, in accordance with the convention establishing said Commission.

The above prohibitions apply by operation of law when the conviction, bankruptcy, dismissal, suspension or removal was pronounced abroad. In this case, the public prosecutor or the interested party may refer to the competent court a request seeking to establish whether the conditions for application of the above prohibitions are met or not; the court rules after verification of the regularity and legality of the foreign decision, the interested party being duly summoned in chambers. The decision may only be subject to an appeal in cassation.

When the decision from which one of the prohibitions referred to in this article results is subsequently withdrawn or reversed, the prohibition ceases by operation of law, unless the new decision is subject to appeals.

###### Art. 1076

Whoever contravenes one of the prohibitions provided for in articles 1074 and 1075 above is punished by a term of imprisonment of one (01) to five (05) year(s) and a fine of two million (2,000,000) to five million (5,000,000) CFA francs or one of these two penalties.

central bank or to the Banking Commission inaccurate documents or information or opposes one of the controls carried out by the Banking Commission and the central bank, in accordance with the legislative and regulatory provisions in force.

###### Art. 1077

Any person convicted of one of the acts provided for in article 1074 paragraphs 1 and 2, and in article 1076 above may not be employed, in any capacity whatsoever, by a bank or financial institution. The provisions of article 1075 paragraphs 4 and 5 above are applicable to this prohibition.

In case of infringement of this prohibition, the perpetrator is liable to the penalties provided for in article 1076 above and the employer, to a fine of five million (5,000,000) to ten million (10,000,000) CFA francs.

In case of recidivism, the maximum penalty is increased to two (02) years' imprisonment and twenty million (20,000,000) CFA francs fine.

###### Art. 1081

Is punished by a fine of two million (2,000,000) to twenty million (20,000,000) CFA francs, any bank or financial institution that contravenes one of the provisions of articles 18, 27, 30, 40, 41 and 42 or the provisions provided for in articles 44 and 45 of the law on banking regulations, all without prejudice to the other sanctions provided for by the same law and this section.

###### Art. 1078

Is punished by a term of imprisonment of one (01) month to two (02) years and a fine of two million (2,000,000) to twenty million (20,000,000) CFA francs, or one of these two penalties:

1) whoever, acting on his own account or that of a third party, carries out an activity of receiving funds and/or carrying out credit, credit sale or exchange operations, or investment, claims the status of bank or banker or creates the appearance of this status without having been previously approved and registered on the list of banks; 2) any financial institution of one category that carries out the activities of another category without prior authorization as provided for by the legislation in force.

In case of recidivism, the maximum penalty is increased to five (05) years' imprisonment and fifty million (50,000,000) CFA francs fine.

###### Art. 1079

Is punished by a term of imprisonment of one (01) month to two (02) years and a fine of two million (2,000,000) to twenty million (20,000,000) CFA francs, or one of these two penalties, whoever uses confidential information of which he has knowledge in the context of his activities to carry out directly or indirectly operations on his own account or to benefit other persons.

In case of recidivism, the maximum penalty is increased to five (05) years' imprisonment and fifty million (50,000,000) CFA francs fine.

###### Art. 1080

Is punished by a term of imprisonment of one (01) month to one (01) year and a fine of one million (1,000,000) to ten million (10,000,000) CFA francs, or one of these two penalties, whoever, acting on his own account or that of a third party knowingly communicates to the

The same penalty may be pronounced against the managers responsible for the offence and against any auditor who contravenes his obligation to communicate to the Banking Commission, at its request, all reports, documents and other documents as well as any information deemed useful for the accomplishment of its mission.

Are liable to the same penalty, personnel who have taken or transferred a participation in a bank or financial institution in contravention of the legislative provisions in force relating to operations subject to the prior authorization of the Minister of Finance.

###### Art. 1082

Legal entities that are not considered as banks or financial institutions under the law governing banking regulation must, under penalty of the sanctions provided for in the preceding article and according to the procedures of Article 54 paragraph 1, communicate to the central bank, upon its request, the information and documents necessary for the exercise of its functions, as defined by the treaty establishing the West African Monetary Union, by its statutes and by the laws and regulations in force.

In the case of provision of inaccurate documents or information, the provisions of Article 1080 above are applicable.

###### Art. 1083

Any natural or legal person, with the exception of banks and financial institutions, who makes it their profession, as a principal or ancillary activity, to bring business to banks and financial institutions or to operate on their behalf, may not carry on their activity without the prior authorization of the Minister of Finance. The application for authorization is processed by the central bank. The authorization specifies the designation that may be used by that person, by way of derogation from the legislative provisions in force relating to the approval required to carry on a

banking activity, as well as the information that they must provide to the central bank and the frequency thereof. Any cessation of activity is notified in advance to the Ministry of Finance and to the central bank.

The provisions of this article do not apply to managers and staff of approved banks and financial institutions, in the exercise of their functions.

Anyone who, acting on their own behalf or that of a third party, contravenes the provisions of this article, is punished by a fine of one million (1,000,000) to ten million (10,000,000) CFA francs.

In the event of a repeat offense, they are punished by a term of imprisonment of two (02) months to two (02) years and a fine of two million (2,000,000) to twenty million (20,000,000) CFA francs, or one of these two penalties.

###### Art. 1084

Subject to the provisions relating to decree authorizations regarding the deposit of public funds and the laws and regulations specific to certain natural or legal persons, it is prohibited for any natural or legal person other than a bank to solicit or accept deposits of public funds regardless of the term.

Anyone who, acting on their own behalf or that of a third party, contravenes the provisions of the preceding paragraph is punished by a term of imprisonment of one (01) month to two (02) years and a fine of two million (2,000,000) to ten million (10,000,000) CFA francs or one of these two penalties. In the event of a repeat offense, the maximum penalty is increased to five (05) years' imprisonment and fifty million (50,000,000) CFA francs fine.

The following are not considered as received from the public:

1) funds constituting the capital of the legal entity; 2) funds received from the managers of the legal entity as well as from shareholders, partners or members holding at least ten percent (10%) of the share capital;

3) funds received from banks or financial institutions in connection with credit transactions;

4) funds received from the staff of the legal entity, provided that their amount remains less than ten percent (10%) of the actual equity of the legal entity.

Funds from an issue of certificates of deposit are always considered as deposits of public funds.

##### Section 2: Offenses relating to the regulation on Payment Instruments

###### Art. 1085

Anyone is punished by a term of imprisonment of one (01) to three (03) year(s) and a fine of one hundred thousand (100,000) to two million five hundred thousand (2,500,000) CFA francs or one of these two penalties:

1) the account holder or agent who, knowingly, issues a check without provision, or, after issuing a check, withdraws by any means whatsoever, all or part of the provision;

2) the drawer or agent who, knowingly, issues a check domiciled on a closed account;

3) the drawer who issues one or more checks, in defiance of the injunction addressed to them by the drawee bank in the absence of regularization, following the refusal of payment of a check for lack of provision, within the time limit provided by law, to return to all bankers of whom they are a client the check forms in their possession and in that of their agents;

4) the agent who, knowingly, issues one or more checks the reissue of which was prohibited to their principal in application of Article 74 of the uniform law on payment instruments;

5) any person who has forbidden the drawee to pay outside the cases provided for by the law on payment instruments; 6) any person who accepts, knowingly, a check without provision.

The aforementioned fine may be increased to three million (3,000,000) CFA francs if the drawer is a merchant.

###### Art. 1086

Anyone is punished by a term of imprisonment of one (01) to five (05) year(s) and a fine of one hundred thousand (100,000) CFA francs to five million (5,000,000) CFA francs or one of these two penalties:

1) any person who counterfeits or falsifies a check; 2) any person who, knowingly, uses or attempts to use a counterfeit or falsified check; 3) any person who, knowingly, agrees to receive a counterfeit or falsified check.

###### Art. 1087

In all cases provided for in the two preceding articles, the court must prohibit the convicted person, for a period of one (01) to five (05) year(s), from issuing checks other than those that exclusively allow the withdrawal of funds by the drawer from the drawee or those that are certified.

This prohibition may be declared provisionally enforceable. It is accompanied by an injunction addressed to the convicted person to return to the bankers who had issued them the forms in their possession and in that of their agents. The court may order, at the expense of the convicted person, the publication by extracts of the decision imposing the prohibition in the newspapers designated and according to the procedures it determines. As a consequence of the aforementioned prohibition, any banker informed thereof by the central bank in accordance with the legislation in force, must refrain from issuing to the convicted person and their agents check forms other than those mentioned in the preceding paragraph.

When the conviction is pronounced following a payment incident noted on a joint account with or without joint and several liability, the prohibition provided for in the first paragraph is automatically applicable to the other holders with respect to said account.

###### Art. 1088

The drawer who issues one or more checks in violation of the prohibition pronounced in application of Article 1087 paragraph 1 is punished by a term of imprisonment of one (01) to five (05) year(s) and a fine of one hundred thousand (100,000) to two million five hundred thousand (2,500,000) CFA francs, or one of these two penalties.

The agent who, knowingly, issues one or more checks the issue of which was prohibited to their principal in application of Article 1087 paragraph 1 is punished by the same penalties.

###### Art. 1089

All acts punished by Articles 1085 and 1086 above are considered, for the application of the provisions concerning repeat offenses, as constituting the same offense.

In the event of a repeat offense, the maximum penalty is incurred.

###### Art. 1090

The drawee who, without having complied with the provisions of the legislation in force, refuses payment of a check on the grounds that the drawer has stopped payment on it is liable to a fine of one hundred thousand (100,000) to two million (2,000,000) CFA francs.

###### Art. 1091

Is liable to a fine of one hundred thousand (100,000) to three million (3,000,000) CFA francs:

1) the drawee who indicates a provision lower than the existing and available provision;

2) the drawee who rejects a cheque for insufficiency or unavailability of provision without indicating, where such is the case, that the cheque was issued in defiance of an injunction addressed pursuant to the legislation in force or in violation of a prohibition pronounced pursuant to article 1087 paragraph 1 above;

3) the drawee who has not declared, under the conditions provided for, payment incidents as well as the offences provided for by articles 1085 points 1 to 6, 1086 and 1088 above; 4) the drawee who contravenes the provisions of article 1087 paragraph 2 above and articles 72, 74, 81 of the uniform law on payment instruments above; 5) the drawee who contravenes one of the obligations listed below:

a) the obligation to verify the identity and address of the person requesting the opening of an account and of the co-holder of a joint account;

b) the obligation to inform clients to whom a chequebook is issued of the penalties incurred in case of prohibition to pay;

c) the obligation to send to its clients an account statement at least once per quarter;

d) the obligation to inquire into the situation of the applicant by consulting the payment incidents file before any delivery of cheque forms.

###### Art. 1092

The use of information held and communicated by the central bank, relating to cheque payment incidents, banking prohibitions and judicial prohibitions to issue cheques as well as the lifting of these same prohibitions, for purposes unrelated to those of the legislation in force is liable to engage the civil and, where applicable, criminal liability of its author.

###### Art. 1093: Is punished by the penalties provided for in article 1091 above

1) any issuer who delivers a payment card in violation of article 100 paragraphs 1 and 2 of the uniform law on payment instruments;

2) any issuer who refrains from informing the central bank in time of the existence of an established abuse, the use of the card within a period of four (04) working days.

###### Art. 1094: Are punished by the penalties provided for in article 1086 above

1) those who have counterfeited or falsified a payment or withdrawal card;

2) those who, knowingly, use or attempt to use a counterfeited or falsified payment or withdrawal card;

3) those who, knowingly, agree to receive a payment by means of a counterfeited or falsified payment card.

###### Art. 1095

Are punished by the penalties provided for in article 1085 paragraph 1 above, those who use a payment card after expiration of said card, or after opposition for loss or theft.

Are punished by the same penalties, those who, despite an injunction to return, not to use or to destroy, continue to use the irregularly held card.

###### Art. 1096

Final judgments rendered pursuant to articles 1094 and 1095 above are notified by the public prosecutor's office to the central bank, which is required to disseminate to issuing institutions the content of these judgments, without the need for a special provision in the judgment or a special mention in the notification from the public prosecutor's office.

Failure to perform the obligation prescribed in the preceding paragraph is punished by a fine of one million (1,000,000) to three million (3,000,000) CFA francs.

##### Section 3

Offences against the regulation of usury and the fixing of the legal interest rate.

###### Art. 1097

Is punished by a term of imprisonment of six (06) months to two (02) years and a fine of one million (1,000,000) to five million (5,000,000) CFA francs or one of these two penalties, whoever grants to another a usurious loan or knowingly provides, in whatever capacity and in whatever manner, directly or indirectly their assistance in obtaining or granting a usurious loan. In case of recidivism, the maximum penalty is increased to five (05) years' imprisonment and fifteen million (15,000,000) CFA francs fine.

###### Art. 1098

In addition to the penalties fixed by the preceding article, the court may order:

1) the publication of its decision at the expense of the convicted person in the newspapers it designates, as well as in any form it deems appropriate;

2) the penalties provided for by article 54 of this code when a legal person is found guilty of the offence defined in the preceding article.

The provisional or permanent closure of the legal person which has engaged or whose managers have engaged in usurious operations is accompanied by the appointment of an administrator or liquidator.

In case of provisional closure, the offender or the legal person continues to pay to its staff the salaries and allowances of any kind to which they are entitled. This period may not exceed three (03) months.

In case of recidivism, permanent closure is ordered.

##### Section 4: Offences against the rules relating to cash payments

###### Art. 1099: Cash payment is prohibited in the following cases

1) for any payment transaction, concerning ferrous or non-ferrous metals, ores and petroleum or gas products, concluded between merchants and professionals; 2) for any payment transaction exceeding two million (2,000,000) CFA francs per transaction, concluded by merchants or professionals;

3) for any payment transaction exceeding five million (5,000,000) CFA francs per transaction, concluded by individuals with merchants or professionals. Any infringement of the foregoing provisions is punished by a fine of fifty thousand (50,000) to thirty million (30,000,000) CFA francs, without prejudice to the application of the provisions of articles 53 and 54 of this code relating to the liability of legal persons.

#### Chapter V

Offences in the award of public contracts and the obligation to disclose the beneficial owners of companies engaged in extractive or gas or oil exploitation activities

###### Art. 1100

Any public official who intentionally fails to comply with one or more legislative or regulatory provisions intended to guarantee freedom of access and equality of candidates in public procurement is punished by five (05) to ten (10) years' imprisonment, in particular:

1) by working to declare as successful bidder a tenderer who has not complied with the procedural rules for submitting public tenders or who has not met the conditions required by the applicable legislative or regulatory provisions; 2) by creating an institution in the name of third parties for the purpose of tendering for a public contract; 3) by voluntarily informing, prior to submission, any successful bidder of the conditions for awarding the contract.

Any enterprise, whatever its legal form, tendering for a public contract, must make a truthful declaration and communicate to the competent authority, in its response to the call for tenders, all elements enabling the identification of natural persons, whether or not residing in the territory of the Togolese Republic, who are the real and ultimate economic beneficiaries of the tendering enterprise in whatever legal, economic or financial form.

Failure to comply with the obligation to declare and communicate as provided for in the preceding paragraph results in the inadmissibility of the tender and is punished by a fine of five hundred thousand (500,000) to twenty million (20,000,000) CFA francs.

Successful bidder enterprises shall declare to the competent authority, within sixty (60) days of its occurrence, any modification, with supporting documents, relating to the identification of the real and ultimate economic beneficiaries of these enterprises during the execution of the contract(s) and for the two (02) years following the end of their execution.

Failure to comply with the obligation to declare and communicate as provided for in the preceding paragraph is punished by a sentence of six (06) months to two (02) years' imprisonment and a fine of five hundred thousand (500,000) to twenty million (20,000,000) CFA francs.

Any declaration and communication made pursuant to this article which proves to be false, misleading or inaccurate is punished by a sentence of six (06) months to two (02) years' imprisonment and a fine of five hundred thousand (500,000) to twenty million (20,000,000) CFA francs.

The sanctions referred to in this article do not exclude those that may be taken by the administrative authority.

Failure to comply with the obligation to declare and communicate within the forty-five (45) day period provided for in paragraph 1 of this article is punished by a sentence of one (01) to six (06) months' imprisonment and a fine of five hundred thousand (500,000) to two million (2,000,000) CFA francs.

Any declaration or communication made pursuant to this article which proves to be false, misleading or inaccurate is punished by a sentence of six (06) months to two (02) years' imprisonment and a fine of five hundred thousand (500,000) to twenty million (20,000,000) CFA francs.

Failure to comply with the obligation to declare and communicate after formal notice to regularize within thirty (30) days which may be sent by the competent authority at the end of the forty-five (45) day period provided for in paragraph 1 of this article, is treated as a refusal to declare and communicate and punished by a fine of five hundred thousand (500,000) to twenty million (20,000,000) CFA francs and a sentence of six (06) months to two (02) years' imprisonment.

Failure to declare any modification relating to the identification of the persons referred to in paragraph 1 of this article is punished by the penalties provided for in the preceding paragraph.

The sanctions referred to in this article do not exclude those that may be taken by the administrative authority.

###### Art. 1102

For the application of articles 1100 and 1101 of this code, the de facto or de jure managers of the enterprises concerned may be prosecuted, as well as any natural or legal person directly or indirectly holding these enterprises, whether or not they reside in the territory of the Togolese Republic.

The real and ultimate economic beneficiaries of the enterprises concerned are punished by five times the fines and double the prison sentences incurred by the persons referred to in the preceding paragraph.

### TITRE X: OFFENCES RELATING TO OHADA LAW

#### Chapitre I

Offences relating to commercial company law and economic interest groupings (GIE)

##### Section 1: Offences relating to the formation of companies

###### Art. 1103

It constitutes an offence for the founders, the chairman and chief executive officer, the chief executive officer, the managing director or the deputy managing director of a public limited company to issue shares before registration or at any time when registration is obtained by fraud or when the company is irregularly formed.

###### Art. 1101

Any enterprise engaged in extractive or gas or oil exploitation activity, whatever its legal form:

1) is required to make a truthful declaration and communicate to the competent authority, within forty-five (45) days of the entry into force of this code, all elements enabling the identification of natural persons, whether or not residing in the territory of the Togolese Republic, who are the real and ultimate beneficiaries of this enterprise in whatever legal, economic or financial form;

2) is required to make a truthful declaration and communicate to the competent authority, within sixty (60) days of its occurrence, any modification, with supporting documents, relating to the identification of the persons referred to in this article.

###### Art. 1104

Any person guilty of the offence provided for in the preceding article is punished by a sentence of imprisonment of six (06) months to three (03) years and a fine of two hundred thousand (200,000) to two million (2,000,000) CFA francs or one of these two penalties.

###### Art. 1105

Those who are punished by a sentence of six (06) months to three (03) years' imprisonment and a fine of two hundred thousand (200,000) to two million (2,000,000) CFA francs or one of these two penalties:

1) knowingly, by drawing up the notarial declaration of subscription and payment or the depositary's certificate, affirm as truthful and accurate subscriptions which they knew to be fictitious or declare that funds which have not been definitively placed at the disposal of the company have been actually paid;

2) hand over to the notary or the depositary a list of shareholders or subscription and payment bulletins mentioning fictitious subscriptions or payments of funds which have not been definitively at the disposal of the company;

3) knowingly, by simulating subscription or payment or by publishing subscription or payment which do not exist or any other false facts, have obtained or attempted to obtain subscriptions or payments;

4) knowingly, to induce subscriptions or payments, publish the names of persons designated contrary to the truth as being or having to be attached to the company in any capacity whatsoever;

5) fraudulently, attribute to a contribution in kind a valuation higher than its actual value.

###### Art. 1106

Any person who knowingly negotiates is punished by a sentence of imprisonment of one (01) to three (03) months and a fine of one hundred and fifty thousand (150,000) to six million (6,000,000) CFA francs or one of these two penalties:

1) shares not fully paid up; 2) cash shares for which payment of one quarter of the nominal value has not been made.

##### Section 2

Offences relating to the management, administration and direction of companies

###### Art. 1107

Any company director who, in the absence of an inventory or by means of a fraudulent inventory, knowingly distributes fictitious dividends among the shareholders or members, is punished by a term of imprisonment of one (01) to three (03) year(s) and a fine of one million (1,000,000) to six million (6,000,000) CFA francs or one of these two penalties.

###### Art. 1108

Any company director who, knowingly, even in the absence of any distribution of dividends, publishes or presents to the shareholders or members, with a view to concealing the true situation of the company, summary financial statements that do not give, for each financial year, a true and fair view of the operations of the financial year, the financial situation and that of the company's assets, at the expiration of that period, is punished by a term of imprisonment of six (06) months to three (03) years and a fine of one million (1,000,000) to six million (6,000,000) CFA francs or one of these two penalties.

###### Art. 1109

Any company director who does not file the summary financial statements within the month following their approval is punished by a fine of one million (1,000,000) to six million (6,000,000) CFA francs.

###### Art. 1110

The manager of the limited liability company, the directors, the chairman and chief executive officer, the chief executive officer, the deputy chief executive officer, the chairman of the simplified joint-stock company, the general manager or the deputy general manager who, in bad faith, make use of the company's assets or credit in a manner they know to be contrary to the interests of the company, for personal purposes, material or moral, or to favour another legal entity in which they had an interest, directly or indirectly, are punished by a term of imprisonment of one (01) to six (06) year(s) and a fine of one million (1,000,000) to six million (6,000,000) CFA francs or one of these two penalties.

The court seized may pronounce against the offender disqualification from the right to exercise a professional activity for up to five (05) years and from civil rights for up to two (02) years.

###### Art. 1111

Is punished by a penalty of six (06) months to three (03) years' imprisonment and a fine of two hundred thousand (200,000) to two million (2,000,000) CFA francs, any company director who, knowingly:

1) does not include the company name on all acts and documents emanating from the company and intended for third parties;

2) does not immediately precede or follow the company name with the indication, in legible characters, of the form of the company; the amount of its share capital, the address of its registered office and the mention of its registration in the trade and movable credit register.

###### Art. 1112

Is punished by a fine of two hundred thousand (200,000) to two million (2,000,000) CFA francs, any company director of a foreign company or the foreign natural person whose branch, beyond a period of two (02) years, has been neither contributed to a pre-existing company or one to be created under the law of one of the States parties to the Treaty on the Harmonisation of Business Law in Africa nor struck off under the conditions laid down by the revised OHADA Uniform Act on the law of commercial companies and economic interest groups.

##### Section 3 : Des infractions relatives aux assemblées générales

###### Art. 1113

Any person who, knowingly, prevents a shareholder or member from participating in a general meeting is punished by a fine of two hundred thousand (200,000) to two million (2,000,000) CFA francs.

###### Art. 1114

Any company director who, knowingly, does not draw up the minutes of general meetings in the forms required by the revised OHADA Uniform Act on the law of commercial companies and economic interest groups is punished by a fine of two hundred thousand (200,000) to two million (2,000,000) CFA francs.

##### Section 4

Des infractions relatives aux modifications du capital des sociétés anonymes

###### Paragraphe 1 : De l'augmentation de capital

###### Art. 1115
