Are punished by a term of imprisonment of six (06) months to one (01) year and a fine of three million (3,000,000) to six million (6,000,000) CFA francs or one of these two penalties, the directors, the chairman of the board of directors, the chairman and chief executive officer, the chief executive officer, the deputy chief executive officer, the general manager or the deputy general manager of a public limited company or the chairman of a simplified joint-stock company who, during a capital increase, issue shares or share certificates:

1) before the depositary's certificate has been drawn up; 2) without the formalities prior to the capital increase having been duly completed; 3) without the previously subscribed capital of the company having been fully paid up;

4) without the new shares having been paid up by at least one quarter of their nominal value at the time of subscription;

5) where applicable, without the entire share premium having been paid up at the time of subscription.

The persons referred to in this article who have not maintained the cash shares in registered form until their full payment are also punished by the same penalties.

###### Art. 1116

Are punished by a term of imprisonment of six (06) months to one (01) year and a fine of three million (3,000,000) to six million (6,000,000) CFA francs or one of these two penalties, the managers of a limited liability company who, during a capital increase, issue shares without these new shares having been paid up by at least half of their nominal value at the time of subscription.

###### Art. 1117

Are punished by a term of imprisonment of six (06) months to one (01) year and a fine of three million (3,000,000) to six million (6,000,000) CFA francs or one of these two penalties, the company directors who, during a capital increase:

1) have not granted shareholders, in proportion to the amount of their shares, a preferential right to subscribe for cash shares when this right has not been removed by the general meeting and the shareholders have not waived it;

2) have not reserved for shareholders a period of at least twenty (20) days, from the opening of the subscription,

except when this period has been closed early;

3) have not allocated the shares made available, for lack of a sufficient number of subscriptions on a non-reducible basis, to shareholders who have subscribed on a reducible basis for a number of shares greater than that which they could subscribe for on a non-reducible basis, in proportion to the rights they hold; 4) have not reserved the rights of holders of subscription warrants.

###### Art. 1118

Any company director who, knowingly, gives or confirms inaccurate information in the reports presented to the general meeting called upon to decide on the removal of the preferential subscription right is punished by a term of imprisonment of six (06) months to one (01) year and a fine of three million (3,000,000) to six million (6,000,000) CFA francs or one of these two penalties.

###### Paragraphe 2 : Capital reduction

###### Art. 1119

Punishable by a fine of three million (3,000,000) to six million (6,000,000) CFA francs are the directors, the chairman and chief executive officer, the chief executive officer, the deputy chief executive officer, the president of the simplified joint-stock company, the managing director or the deputy managing director who, knowingly, proceed with a capital reduction:

1) without respecting the equality of shareholders; 2) without having communicated the capital reduction project to the statutory auditors forty-five (45) days before the holding of the general meeting called to decide on the capital reduction.

##### Section 5 : Offences relating to the control of companies

###### Art. 1120

Any corporate officer who does not initiate the appointment of the statutory auditors of the company or does not summon them to general meetings is punished by a fine of three million (3,000,000) to six million (6,000,000) CFA francs.

The court seized may pronounce against him the disqualification from exercising a professional activity for a maximum of five (05) years.

###### Art. 1121

Any person who, either in his personal name or as a partner of a statutory auditors' firm, knowingly accepts, exercises or retains the functions of statutory auditor, notwithstanding legal incompatibilities, is punished by a fine of three million (3,000,000) to six million (6,000,000) CFA francs.

###### Art. 1122

Any statutory auditor who, either in his personal name or as a partner of a statutory auditors' firm, knowingly gives or confirms false information about the situation of the company or who does not disclose to the public prosecutor criminal acts of which he has become aware is punished by a fine of three million (3,000,000) to six million (6,000,000) CFA francs.

The court seized may pronounce against him the disqualification from exercising a professional activity for a maximum of five (05) years.

###### Art. 1123

Any corporate officer or any person in the service of the company who, knowingly, obstructs the verifications or control of the statutory auditors or who refuses the communication, on site, of all documents useful for the exercise of their mission, in particular all contracts, books, accounting documents and minutes registers, is punished by a term of imprisonment of six (06) months to one (01) year and a fine of three million (3,000,000) to six million (6,000,000) CFA francs or one of these two penalties.

The court seized may pronounce against him the disqualification from exercising a professional activity for a maximum of five (05) years and civil rights for a maximum of three (03) years.

##### Section 6 : Offences relating to the dissolution of companies

###### Art. 1124

Punishable by a fine of three hundred thousand (300,000) to three million (3,000,000) CFA francs are the corporate officers who, knowingly, when the company's equity becomes less than half of the share capital due to losses recorded in the summary financial statements:

1) do not convene within the four (04) months following the approval of the summary financial statements having revealed these losses, the extraordinary general meeting for the purpose of deciding, if necessary, the early dissolution of the company;

2) do not file with the trade and movable credit register and do not publish in a newspaper authorized to receive legal notices, the dissolution of the company.

The court seized may order the closure of the establishment.

##### Section 7 : Offences relating to the liquidation of companies

###### Art. 1125

Punishable by a fine of three hundred thousand (300,000) to three million (3,000,000) CFA francs is any liquidator of a company who, knowingly:

1) does not publish, within one (01) month from his appointment, in a newspaper authorized to receive legal notices of the place of the registered office, the act appointing him liquidator and does not file with the trade and movable credit register the decisions pronouncing the dissolution;

2) does not convene the partners, at the end of the liquidation, to decide on the final account of the liquidation, on the discharge of his management and the release of his mandate and to record the closure of the liquidation;

3) does not file his final accounts with the registry of the court responsible for commercial matters of the place of the registered office, nor request judicial approval thereof.

###### Art. 1126

Punishable by a fine of three hundred thousand (300,000) to three million (3,000,000) CFA francs, when the liquidation occurs by court decision, is any liquidator who, knowingly:

1) does not present, within six (06) months of his appointment, a report on the assets and liabilities of the company in liquidation and on the continuation of liquidation operations, nor request the necessary authorizations to complete them; 2) does not prepare within three (03) months of the close of each financial year, the summary financial statements in view of the inventory and a written report in which he accounts for the liquidation operations during the past financial year;

3) does not allow the partners to exercise, during the liquidation period, their right to access corporate documents under the same conditions as previously;

4) does not convene the partners, at least once a year, to report to them on the summary financial statements in case of continuation of the company's operations;

5) does not deposit in an account opened in a bank in the name of the company in liquidation, within fifteen (15) days from the distribution decision, the sums allocated to distributions among the partners and creditors;

6) does not deposit in a consignment account opened in the books of the public treasury, within one (01) year from the closure of the liquidation, the sums allocated to creditors or partners and not claimed by them.

###### Art. 1127

Punishable by a term of imprisonment of one (01) to five (05) year(s) and a fine of three hundred thousand (300,000) to six million (6,000,000) CFA francs is any liquidator who, in bad faith:

1) makes use of the assets or credit of the company in liquidation in a manner he knows to be contrary to the interest of the company, for personal purposes or to favor another legal entity in which he had an interest, directly or indirectly;

2) transfers all or part of the assets of the company in liquidation to a person who had in the company the capacity of general partner, limited partner, manager, member of the board of directors, managing director or statutory auditor, without having obtained the unanimous consent of the partners or, failing that, the authorization of the competent court.

##### Section 8: Offences in case of public offering

###### Art. 1128

The presidents, directors or general managers of companies who issue securities offered to the public are punished by imprisonment of three (03) months to one (01) year and a fine of three hundred thousand (300,000) to six million (6,000,000) CFA francs:

1) without a notice being inserted in a newspaper authorized to receive legal announcements, prior to any publicity measure;

2) without the prospectuses and circulars reproducing the statements of the notice provided for in point 1 above, and containing mention of the insertion of this notice in the newspaper authorized to receive legal announcements with reference to the issue in which it was published;

3) without the posters and announcements in newspapers reproducing the same statements, or at least an extract of these statements with reference to said notice, and indications of the issue number of the newspaper authorized to receive legal announcements in which it was published;

4) without the posters, prospectuses and circulars mentioning the signature of the person or representative of the company from which the offer emanates and specifying whether the securities offered are listed or not and, if so, on which stock exchange.

Persons who have served as intermediaries on the occasion of the transfer of securities without the requirements of this article having been complied with are punished by the same penalty.

#### Chapter II: Offences relating to security rights

###### Art. 1129

Any person guilty of registration of a movable security effected by fraud or bearing inaccurate entries given in bad faith, is punished by a fine of three hundred thousand (300,000) to three million (3,000,000) CFA francs.

The court seized may further order the rectification of the inaccurate entry in the terms it determines.

###### Art. 1130

Any person who, by fraudulent maneuvers, totally or partially deprives a lessor of his privilege over the movables furnishing the leased premises, is punished by imprisonment of six (06) months to three (03) years and a fine of one hundred thousand (100,000) to five hundred thousand (500,000) CFA francs or one of these two penalties.

#### Chapter III

Offences relating to simplified recovery procedures and enforcement methods

###### Art. 1131

Any debtor subject to seizure or any third party holder in whose hands the seizure of corporeal property has been effected who misappropriates them, is punished by imprisonment of six (06) months to three (03) years and a fine of one hundred thousand (100,000) to five hundred thousand (500,000) CFA or one of these two penalties.

The attempt to misappropriate seized objects is punished by the same penalties.

###### Art. 1132

Any auctioneer or any other auxiliary of justice responsible for the sale of seized property, personally liable for the price of adjudications, who receives a sum above the bid, is punished by imprisonment of one (01) to five (05) year(s) and a fine of one hundred thousand (100,000) to five million (5,000,000) CFA francs or one of these two penalties.

#### Chapter IV

Offences relating to collective procedures for clearing liabilities

##### Section 1: Bankruptcy

###### Paragraph 1: Simple bankruptcy

###### Art. 1133

Simple bankruptcy is constituted by the act of a natural person in a state of cessation of payments of:

1) contracting without receiving values in exchange, commitments deemed too significant having regard to his situation when he contracted them;

2) intentionally delaying the finding of the cessation of his payments by making purchases with a view to resale below the market price or employing ruinous means to procure funds;

3) not making, at the registry of the competent court, the declaration of his state of cessation of payments within the period of thirty (30) days, without legitimate excuse;

4) having incomplete or irregularly kept accounts or keeping no accounts in conformity with accounting rules and recognized practices of the profession having regard to the importance of the enterprise;

5) having been declared twice in a state of cessation of payments within a period of five (05) years; these proceedings having been closed for insufficiency of assets.

###### Article 1134

Any person guilty of simple bankruptcy is punished by imprisonment of one (01) month to two (02) year(s) and a fine of two hundred thousand (200,000) to one million (1,000,000) CFA francs.

###### Paragraph 2: Fraudulent bankruptcy

###### Art. 1135

Fraudulent bankruptcy is constituted by the act of a natural person in a state of cessation of payments of:

1) removing his accounts;

2) misappropriating or squandering all or part of his assets;

3) acknowledging himself either in his writings, or by public acts or private agreements, or in his balance sheet, as debtor of sums he did not owe;

4) exercising the commercial profession contrary to a prohibition provided for by uniform acts or by law;

5) paying a creditor to the prejudice of the body of creditors after the cessation of payments;

6) stipulating with a creditor particular advantages by reason of his vote in the deliberations of the body of creditors or making with a creditor a particular agreement from which would result for the latter an advantage at the expense of the debtor's assets from the day of the opening decision.

###### Art. 1136

Fraudulent bankruptcy is also constituted by the act of a natural person on the occasion of judicial settlement proceedings of:

1) presenting or having presented in bad faith, an income statement, a balance sheet, a statement of debts and liabilities or an active and passive statement of privileges and securities, that is inaccurate or incomplete;

2) accomplishing without the authorization of the president of the competent court, one of the following prohibited acts:

a) paying, in whole or in part, debts arising prior to the decision to suspend individual proceedings and covered by it;

b) making an act of disposition unrelated to the normal operation of the enterprise or granting a security;

c) compensating the guarantors who have discharged debts arising prior to the decision to suspend individual proceedings.

###### Art. 1137

Any person guilty of fraudulent bankruptcy is punished by imprisonment of one (01) to five (05) year(s) and a fine of two million (2,000,000) to six million (6,000,000) CFA francs.

##### Section 2: Offences assimilated to bankruptcy

###### Paragraphe 1: Offences assimilated to simple bankruptcy

###### Art. 1138

Constitutes an offence assimilated to simple bankruptcy the act by natural persons who are directors of legal persons subject to collective proceedings and natural persons who are permanent representatives of legal persons who are directors of legal persons subject to collective proceedings, in that capacity and in bad faith, of:

1) consuming sums belonging to the legal person by carrying out pure chance transactions or fictitious transactions;

2) making purchases with a view to resale below market price or using ruinous means to procure funds with the intention of delaying the finding of cessation of payments of the legal person;

3) paying or causing to be paid a creditor to the detriment of the estate, after cessation of payments of the legal person;

4) causing the legal person to contract, on behalf of others, without receiving assets in exchange, commitments deemed too significant having regard to its situation when those commitments were contracted;

5) keeping, causing to be kept or allowing to be kept irregularly or incompletely the accounts of the legal person or keeping no accounts in conformity with accounting rules and recognized professional practices having regard to the size of the enterprise;

6) failing to file with the registry of the competent court, within thirty (30) days, the declaration of the state of cessation of payments of the legal person;

7) misappropriating or concealing, attempting to misappropriate or conceal part of their assets or fraudulently acknowledging themselves to be debtors of sums they did not owe with a view to removing all or part of their patrimony from the pursuit of the legal person in a state of cessation of payments or from those of the partners or creditors of the legal person.

###### Art. 1139

Directors within the meaning of the preceding article means all directors de jure or de facto and, in general, any person having directly or through an intermediary, administered, managed or liquidated the legal person under cover of or in place of its legal representatives.

###### Art. 1140

Any person guilty of an offence assimilated to simple bankruptcy is punished by a term of imprisonment of one (01) month to two (02) year(s) and a fine of two hundred thousand (200,000) to one million (1,000,000) CFA francs.

###### Art. 1141

Any legal or de facto representative of a legal person having partners indefinitely and jointly liable for its debts, who, without legitimate excuse, does not file with the registry of the competent court, within thirty (30) days, the declaration of its state of cessation of payments or if that declaration does not include the list of jointly liable partners with indication of their names and addresses, is punished by a term of imprisonment of one (01) month to two (02) year(s) and a fine of two hundred thousand (200,000) to one million (1,000,000) CFA francs.

###### Paragraphe 2: Offences assimilated to fraudulent bankruptcy

###### Art. 1142

Constitutes an offence assimilated to fraudulent bankruptcy the act by natural persons who are directors of legal persons subject to collective proceedings and natural persons who are permanent representatives of legal persons who are directors of legal persons subject to collective proceedings, of fraudulently:

1) removing the books of the legal person;
2) misappropriating or concealing part of its assets;
3) acknowledging the legal person to be a debtor of sums it did not owe, either in the records, or by public acts or commitments under private signature, or in the balance sheet;
4) exercising the profession of director contrary to a prohibition provided for by uniform acts or by law;
5) stipulating with a creditor, in the name of the legal person, special advantages by reason of his vote in the deliberations of the estate or making with a creditor a particular agreement from which would result for the latter an advantage at the expense of the assets of the legal person, from the day of the decision declaring cessation of payments.

###### Art. 1143

Also constitutes an offence assimilated to fraudulent bankruptcy, the act by natural persons who are directors of legal persons subject to collective proceedings and natural persons who are permanent representatives of legal persons who are directors of legal persons subject to collective proceedings, on the occasion of a preventive settlement procedure, of:

1) presenting or causing to be presented in bad faith, an income statement, a balance sheet, a statement of debts and liabilities or an active and passive statement of privileges and securities, that is inaccurate or incomplete;

2) accomplishing, without the authorization of the president of the competent court, one of the following prohibited acts:

a) paying, in whole or in part, debts arising prior to the decision to suspend individual proceedings and covered by it;

b) making an act of disposition unrelated to the normal operation of the enterprise or granting a security;

c) compensating sureties who have discharged debts arising prior to the decision to suspend individual proceedings.

###### Art. 1144

Any person guilty of an offence assimilated to fraudulent bankruptcy is punished by a term of imprisonment of one (01) to five (05) year(s) and a fine of two million (2,000,000) to six million (6,000,000) CFA francs.

##### Section 3: Other offences

###### Art. 1145

Is punished by a term of imprisonment of one (01) to five (05) year(s) and a fine of two million (2,000,000) to six million (6,000,000) CFA francs:

1) any person convicted of having, in the interest of the debtor, removed, concealed, or hidden all or part of his movable or immovable property;

2) any person convicted of having fraudulently submitted in the collective proceedings, either in his own name, or by interposition or impersonation, fictitious debts;

3) any person who, carrying on trade under the name of another, or under a fictitious name, has, in bad faith, misappropriated or concealed, attempted to misappropriate or conceal part of his assets.

###### Art. 1146

Any spouse, descendant, ascendant, collateral or relative by marriage of the debtor who, without his knowledge, removes, diverts or conceals assets forming part of the estate of said debtor in a state of cessation of payments, is punished by a term of imprisonment of six (06) months to three (03) years and a fine of fifty thousand (50,000) to five hundred thousand (500,000) CFA francs or one of these two penalties.

###### Art. 1147

In case of acquittal of the accused in the cases provided for in articles 1145 and 1146, the court seized rules on damages and on the reintegration, into the patrimony of the debtor, of the assets, rights or actions removed.

###### Art. 1148

Constitutes an offence, the act by a trustee of collective proceedings of:

1) carrying on a personal activity under the cover of the debtor's business concealing his actions; 2) disposing of the debtor's credit or property as his own;

3) dissipating the debtor's property;

4) abusively and in bad faith pursuing, in his personal interest, either directly or indirectly, a loss-making operation of the debtor's business;

5) acquiring for his own account, directly or indirectly, the debtor's property, in violation of the provisions which prohibit the trustee and all those who have participated in the administration of any collective proceedings, from acquiring personally, either directly or indirectly, amicably or by judicial sale, all or part of the movable or immovable assets of the debtor in a state of preventive settlement, judicial reorganisation or liquidation of assets.

###### Art. 1149

Any trustee of collective proceedings who is guilty of one of the offences provided for in the preceding article, is punished by a term of imprisonment of one (01) to five (05) year(s) and a fine of two hundred thousand (200,000) to one million (1,000,000) CFA francs or one of these two penalties.

###### Art. 1150

The court seized may pronounce against the guilty party the forfeiture of the right to exercise his professional activity for up to five (05) years without prejudice to the deprivation of other rights provided for in this code.

###### Art. 1151: Constitutes an offence, the act by a creditor of

1) stipulating with the debtor as lawyer any other persons, particular advantages by reason of his vote in the deliberations of the mass;

2) making a particular agreement from which there would result in his favour an advantage at the expense of the debtor's assets from the day of the decision to open collective proceedings.

###### Art. 1152

Any creditor guilty of one of the offences provided for in the preceding article is punished by a term of imprisonment of six (06) months to three (03) years and a fine of fifty thousand (50,000) to five hundred thousand (500,000) CFA francs or one of these two penalties.

###### Art. 1153

The court seized orders the annulment of the agreements referred to in article 1151, with regard to all persons, even the debtor.

###### Art. 1154

All conviction decisions rendered by virtue of the provisions of this chapter are, at the expense of those convicted, posted and published in a newspaper authorised to receive legal notices as well as, by summary extract, in the official gazette mentioning the number of the legal notices newspaper where the first insertion was published, without prejudice to the provisions relating to the criminal record.

#### Chapter V

Offences relating to the organisation and harmonisation of business accounts

###### Art. 1155

Constitutes an offence, the act by individual entrepreneurs and company directors:

1) of not, for each financial year, drawing up the inventory and establishing the annual financial statements as well as, where applicable, the management report and the social balance sheet; 2) of knowingly, establishing and communicating financial statements not delivering a true and fair view of the assets, the financial position and the result of the financial year.

###### Art. 1156

Any individual entrepreneur or any company director guilty of one of the offences provided for in the preceding article is punished by a term of imprisonment of one (01) month to two (02) year(s) and a fine of two hundred thousand (200,000) to one million (1,000,000) CFA francs.

#### Chapter VI: Offences relating to general commercial law

###### Art. 1157

Any person required to complete one of the formalities prescribed for registration in the trade and movable credit register who abstains from it or carries out a formality by fraud, is punished by a term of imprisonment of (06) months to three (03) years and a fine of one hundred thousand (100,000) to five hundred thousand (500,000) CFA francs or one of these two penalties.

###### Art. 1158

Any person guilty of a movable security registration carried out by fraud or bearing inaccurate entries given in bad faith, is punished by a term of imprisonment of (06) months to three (03) years and a fine of one hundred thousand (100,000) to five hundred thousand (500,000) CFA francs or one of these two penalties.

The court seized may furthermore order the rectification of the inaccurate entry in the terms it determines.

###### Art. 1159

Constitutes an offence the act by a tenant-manager of not indicating at the head of his order forms, invoices and other documents of a financial or commercial nature, his capacity as tenant-manager of the business with his registration number in the trade and movable credit register.

###### Art. 1160

Any tenant-manager guilty of the offence provided for in the preceding article is punished by a term of imprisonment of one (01) month to one (01) year and a fine of fifty thousand (50,000) to two hundred thousand (200,000) CFA francs or one of these two penalties.

### TITLE XI: CONTRAVENTIONS

###### Art. 1161

Public regulations may enact police penalties to sanction contraventions of the regulations they prescribe.

In the case where the regulation does not specify the quantum of the penalty, it consists of a fine of five thousand (5,000) to one hundred and fifty thousand (150,000) CFA francs.

###### Art. 1162

Public regulations may provide in addition to police penalties, complementary penalties of special confiscation, closure of establishment and publication of convictions.

###### Art. 1163

Is punished by a fine of ten thousand (10,000) to fifty thousand (50,000) CFA francs any person who:

1) soils or encumbers the public highway, the squares, the beaches or any other public space by abusively depositing materials, refuse or things of any kind;

2) neglects to signal temporary deposits or excavations that he has made on the public highway, when these deposits or excavations present risks of accident for users;

3) affixes without authorisation, posters or places inscriptions on the movable or immovable property of others; 4) damages lawns, plantations, ornaments, street lamps, signage panels and other objects of public utility in public places or places accessible to the public;

5) allows domestic animals to wander on the public highway.

###### Art. 1164

Is punished by a fine of ten thousand (10,000) to fifty thousand (50,000) CFA francs, any person who:

1) outside the cases provided for by articles 696, 699 and 706; voluntarily damages the movable property of others; 2) by clumsiness, imprudence, lack of precaution or non-observance of safety regulations involuntarily causes damage to the property of others; 3) without authorisation, drives or allows livestock to pass on the land of others sown or loaded with crops; 4) without necessity, commits an act of cruelty towards an animal.

###### Art. 1169

The act of refusing to respond without legitimate reason to the requisitions of the judicial police officer intervening in the context of telecommunications operations in accordance with the provisions of the code of criminal procedure is punished by a fine of fifty thousand (50,000) to five hundred thousand (500,000) CFA francs.

###### Art. 1170

Are punished by one (01) to three (03) months' imprisonment and a fine of fifty thousand (50,000) to two hundred thousand (200,000) CFA francs or one of these two penalties persons who refuse to submit to the taking of fingerprints or photographs, as well as to a psychiatric examination authorised by the public prosecutor or the investigating judge in accordance with the code of criminal procedure.

###### Art. 1165

Any person who refuses to accept at its official value currency that is neither counterfeit nor altered and has legal tender is punished by a fine of ten thousand (10,000) to fifty thousand (50,000) CFA francs.

### TITRE XII: OFFENCES RELATING TO THE CONDUCT OF CRIMINAL PROCEEDINGS

###### Art. 1166

In places where a crime has been committed, it is prohibited, under penalty of a fine of fifty thousand (50,000) to five hundred thousand (500,000) CFA francs, for any unauthorized person to modify the state of the premises or to take any samples therefrom before the initial judicial investigation operations.

However, an exception is made when such modifications or samples are required by the demands of public safety or health, or by care to be given to victims.

###### Art. 1167

The refusal by a person against whom there exist one or more plausible reasons to suspect that they have committed or attempted to commit an offence to submit to the sampling operations provided for by the code of criminal procedure on the occasion of examinations, home visits, searches ordered by the judicial police officer, is punished by six (06) months to one (01) year of imprisonment and a fine of one hundred thousand (100,000) to one million (1,000,000) CFA francs.

###### Art. 1168

The act of abstaining from responding without legitimate reason to the requisition of the public prosecutor or the judicial police officer under the conditions provided for in matters of investigation and identity checks on the occasion of flagrant crimes and offences and requisitions to a qualified person in accordance with the code of criminal procedure is punished by a fine of fifty thousand (50,000) to five hundred thousand (500,000) CFA francs.

###### Art. 1171

Subject to the requirements of the judicial investigation, any communication or disclosure without authorization of the person charged or their successors in title or the signatory or the addressee of a document originating from a search, to a person not qualified by law to take cognizance thereof, is punished by six (06) months to two (02) years of imprisonment and a fine of one hundred thousand (100,000) to one million (1,000,000) CFA francs or one of these two penalties.

###### Art. 1172

If a witness does not appear or refuses to appear, or if they appear but refuse to take the oath or to testify, the investigating judge may, upon requisitions of the public prosecutor, compel them by public force and sentence them to a fine of ten thousand (10,000) to thirty thousand (30,000) CFA francs. If they subsequently appear, they may however, upon production of their excuses and justifications, be discharged from this penalty by the investigating judge, after requisitions of the public prosecutor.

###### Art. 1173

Subject to the provisions of the code of criminal procedure in matters of investigation and in particular, on the occasion of interrogations and hearings, the act, for a party to whom a reproduction of documents or acts of an investigation procedure has been handed over in application of the provisions of the code of criminal procedure, of disseminating it to a third party is punished by a penalty of six (06) months to two (02) years of imprisonment and a fine of one hundred thousand (100,000) to one million (1,000,000) CFA francs or one of these two penalties.

###### Art. 1174

Any juror who, without legitimate reason, has not complied with the summons they received may be sentenced by the criminal court to a fine of fifty thousand (50,000) CFA francs.

###### Art. 1175

Except where ordered by the president of the criminal court or the criminal jurisdiction from the opening of the hearing of the criminal court or the criminal jurisdiction, the use of any sound recording or broadcasting device, television or cinema camera, photographic equipment is prohibited under penalty of a fine of one million (1,000,000) CFA francs. The devices are moreover confiscated.

###### Art.1176

If, during the hearing of the criminal court or the criminal jurisdiction, the president of the jurisdiction orders the expulsion of a person present from the courtroom and they resist this order or cause tumult, they may be, on the spot, placed under committal warrant, tried and punished by six (06) months to two (02) years of imprisonment, without prejudice to the penalties provided for against perpetrators of insults and violence against magistrates.

###### Art. 1177

The disregard, by the person who has been the subject thereof, of the prohibitions in matters of security measures ordered in case of declaration of criminal irresponsibility due to mental disorder provided for by the code of criminal procedure is punished by six (06) months to two (02) years of imprisonment and a fine of one million (1,000,000) to five million (5,000,000) CFA francs or one of these two penalties.

###### Art. 1178

Any person who refuses to submit to the medical examinations provided for by the code of criminal procedure in matters of use and trafficking of narcotics is punished by imprisonment of three (03) months to one (01) year and a fine of two hundred and fifty thousand (250,000) to one million two hundred and fifty thousand (1,250,000) CFA francs or one of these two penalties.

###### Art. 1179

Whoever contravenes the ordered closure of any hotel, furnished house, boarding house, drinking establishment, restaurant, club, circle, dance hall, place of entertainment or their annex or any place open to the public or used by the public, where one or more of the offences provided for in articles 256 to 261, 263 and 264 of the penal code have been committed is sentenced to imprisonment of two (02) to five (05) years and a fine of two hundred and fifty thousand (250,000) to one million two hundred and fifty thousand (1,250,000) CFA francs or one of these two penalties.

###### Art. 1180

In case of provision by an enterprise of erroneous or false information in the context of the simple preliminary administrative investigation in case of proceedings for violation of competition rules provided for by the code of criminal procedure, the enterprise incurs a fine of twenty thousand (20,000) to one hundred and fifty thousand (150,000) CFA francs.

###### Art. 1181

Whoever has taken the name of a third party, in circumstances which have determined or would have determined the recording of a conviction in the criminal record of the latter, is punished by six (06) months to five (05) years of imprisonment and a fine of twenty thousand (20,000) to five hundred thousand (500,000) CFA francs, without prejudice to prosecutions to be brought where applicable for forgery.

The penalty thus pronounced is served immediately after that incurred for the offence on the occasion of which the recording of the false name was committed.

Is punished by the penalties provided for in paragraph 1 whoever, by false declarations relating to the civil status of an accused person, has knowingly been the cause of the recording of a conviction on the criminal record of a person other than the accused person.

###### Art. 1182

Whoever by taking a false name or a false capacity, has obtained an extract from the criminal record of a third party is punished by ten (10) days to two (02) months of imprisonment and a fine of twenty thousand (20,000) to one hundred and fifty thousand (150,000) CFA francs or one of these two penalties.

Is punished by the same penalties whoever provides false identity information which has caused or could have caused erroneous entries in the criminal record.

Is punished by the same penalties whoever obtains from the person concerned all or part of the entries of the full statement referred to in the code of criminal procedure.

###### Art. 1183

Any failure to comply with the duty to inform provided for in the code of criminal procedure in the context of investigations into the use or trafficking of narcotics and imposed on the following persons is punishable by three (03) months to one (01) year of imprisonment and a fine of five million (5,000,000) to fifty million (50,000,000) CFA francs:

- persons who, in the exercise of their functions, carry out, control or advise on operations involving capital movements;

- directors of public or private banking establishments, postal services, insurance companies, mutual societies, stock exchange companies;

- manual money changers of goods.

### TITRE XIII : DES INFRACTIONS EN MATIERE

ELECTORALE ET AU FINANCEMENT DE LA VIE
POLITIQUE

##### Section 1 : Des infractions en matière électorale

###### Art. 1184

Any person who registers under a false name, a false capacity, or who, upon registering, has concealed an incapacity provided for by law, or who has fraudulently registered on more than one list, is punished by imprisonment of three (03) months to one (01) year and a fine of one hundred thousand (100,000) to three hundred thousand (300,000) CFA francs.

Such person is also automatically removed from the electoral rolls for a period of three (03) years.

Any person who obtains or produces a false certificate of registration or removal from the electoral rolls is punished by the same penalties.

###### Art. 1185

Any person who, having been deprived of the right to vote, either as a result of a judicial conviction or as a result of bankruptcy not followed by rehabilitation, has voted, either by virtue of registration on lists prior to such deprivation or by virtue of subsequent registration carried out with his or her participation, is punished by imprisonment of three (03) months to one (01) year and a fine of one hundred thousand (100,000) to three hundred thousand (300,000) CFA francs.

Any person who, on polling day, distributes or causes to be distributed ballot papers is punished by the same penalties.

###### Art. 1186

Any person who has voted during an electoral consultation, by virtue of registration on the electoral rolls outside the revision periods obtained in the cases provided for by the provisions of the electoral code, by falsely assuming the names and capacities of a registered voter, is punished by imprisonment of three (03) months to one (01) year and a fine of one hundred thousand (100,000) to three hundred thousand (300,000) CFA francs.

###### Art. 1187

Any person who prevents, by wilful non-observance of the law, the registration on an electoral roll of a citizen meeting the conditions set by the electoral code is punished by the penalties provided for in the preceding article.

The penalty is doubled for any person who has taken advantage of multiple registration to vote more than once.

###### Art. 1188

Any person who, being responsible during a ballot for receiving, counting or scrutinising the votes of citizens, has removed, added or altered ballot papers or has deliberately read a name other than that written, is punished by imprisonment of one (01) to three (03) year(s) and deprivation of the right to vote and to be eligible for five (05) years.

Any other person guilty of the acts set out in the preceding paragraph is punished by imprisonment of six (06) months to two (02) years and deprivation of the right to vote and to be eligible for three (03) to five (05) years.

###### Art. 1189

Any person, with the exception of law enforcement officers whose presence is strictly justified by their functions, who participates in an electoral consultation with a visible or concealed weapon is punished by imprisonment of six (06) months to two (02) years and a fine of five hundred thousand (500,000) CFA francs to two million (2,000,000) CFA francs.

###### Art. 1190

Any person who, by gatherings, clamours or threatening demonstrations, disturbs the operations of an electoral consultation or infringes upon the exercise of the right or freedom to vote, is punished by imprisonment of six (06) months to two (02) years and deprivation of the right to vote and to be eligible for three (03) to five (05) years.

###### Art. 1191

Any intrusion into a polling station, completed or attempted with violence, with a view to infringing upon the exercise of the right or freedom to vote, is punished by imprisonment of one (01) to three (03) year(s) and a fine of five hundred thousand (500,000) to one million (1,000,000) CFA francs.

If the perpetrators are carrying weapons or if the ballot has been violated, the penalty is increased to five (05) to ten (10) years of criminal imprisonment.

###### Art. 1192

Any attack on a container holding votes cast and not yet counted is punished by a penalty of imprisonment of six (06) months to two (02) years and a fine of two hundred thousand (200,000) to six hundred thousand (600,000) CFA francs.

If this attack was carried out by an organised gang or by members of the polling station or agents of the authority responsible for guarding the ballots not yet counted, the penalty is increased to one (01) to three (03) year(s) of imprisonment and a fine of five hundred thousand (500,000) to one million (1,000,000) CFA francs.

###### Art. 1193

Convictions pronounced pursuant to the provisions of this section may under no circumstances have the effect of annulling or modifying the results of an election declared valid by the competent authorities in electoral matters.

###### Art. 1194

Any person who violates the prohibition on campaigning and propaganda on polling days is punished by imprisonment of three (03) months to one (01) year and a fine of one hundred thousand (100,000) to three hundred thousand (300,000) CFA francs.

###### Art. 1195

Any person who, by granting or promising financial or material advantage, influences or attempts to influence the exercise or non-exercise of the right or freedom to vote of one or more voters, directly or indirectly, is punished by a penalty of six (06) months to three (03) years of imprisonment and a fine of one million (1,000,000) to five million (5,000,000) CFA francs.

In the event of conviction, the persons concerned are deprived of their right to vote for a period of three (03) years.

###### Art. 1196

Any person may report to the public prosecutor the acts referred to in the preceding article.

###### Art. 1197

Any involuntary non-observance of the law or regulations relating to the organisation or conduct of electoral operations is punished by a fine of two hundred and fifty thousand (250,000) to one million (1,000,000) CFA francs.

###### Art. 1198

Public prosecution instituted pursuant to this section is time-barred after six (06) months from the fifteenth (15th) day following the vote.

###### Art. 1199

The offences provided for in this section, when committed by an organised gang, are punished, except where otherwise provided in this section, by doubling the penalties incurred for such offences.

##### Section 2: Offenses relating to the financing of political life

###### Paragraphe 1: Public financing

###### Art. 1200

Is punished by a fine of seven million (7,000,000) to thirty million (30,000,000) CFA francs and imprisonment of six (06) months to one (01) year or one of these two penalties, without prejudice to civil and administrative sanctions provided by law:

1) any leader of a political party, any candidate and any member of a list of candidates for an election who does not maintain regular financial management accounting and accounting for property within the framework of allocated resources;

2) any leader of a political party who does not prepare an activity report or annual financial report or fails to file the activity report and annual financial report

with the court of auditors within a period of three (03) months after the end of the fiscal year;

3) any party leader, any candidate and any member of a list of candidates having benefited from State subsidy for electoral campaigns, who does not prepare a financial report or who has failed to file a financial report presenting the campaign account, accompanied by supporting documents for resources and expenditures made, with the court of auditors within a period of three (03) months after the publication of the final election results.

A complementary penalty of ineligibility may be pronounced for a period of three (03) years.

###### Paragraphe 2: Private financing

###### Art. 1201

Is punished by a fine of ten million (10,000,000) to thirty million (30,000,000) CFA francs and a term of imprisonment of six (06) months to three (03) years or one of these two penalties:

1) any leader of a political party, any candidate and any member of a list of candidates who receives, directly or indirectly, material or financial assistance from a foreign government, a natural or legal person of foreign nationality, a foreign political party, a foreign non-profit organization, with the exception of organizations composed mainly of natural persons of Togolese nationality, or an international organization, intended for the financing of the activities of a political party or an electoral campaign;

2) any natural or legal person of foreign nationality, with the exception of foreign non-profit organizations composed mainly of natural persons of Togolese nationality, who offers, promises, or gives, to any political party, alliance, coalition or grouping of political parties, any candidate and any member of a list of Togolese candidates, material or financial assistance intended for the financing of the activities of a political party or an electoral campaign.

###### Art. 1202

For purposes of application of the preceding article, any legal person owned, directly or indirectly, totally or partially, by foreign public or private interests is assimilated to a legal person of foreign nationality.

### TITRE XIV: TRANSITIONAL AND FINAL PROVISIONS

###### Art. 1203

Sentences of life imprisonment pronounced by competent jurisdictions, having become final on the date of entry into force of this code, are commuted by operation of law into a sentence of fifty (50) years of criminal imprisonment.

###### Art. 1204

This law repeals law No. 80-1 of 13 August 1980 establishing the penal code as amended to date and all provisions of prior laws and ordinances contrary to the new penal code.

###### Art. 1205

This law shall be published in the Official Journal and executed as law of the State.

Done in Lomé on 24 November 2015

The President of the Republic

Faure Essozimna GNASSINGBE

The Prime Minister

Selom Komi KLASSOU
